# Plan 12a — Staff Roles and Permissions — Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** An academy gives people other than its admins controlled access to the dashboard. The admin composes staff roles from TutorHamster's permission matrix and assigns them to staff accounts, and every route and screen then honours those permissions. Every academy starts with a Supervisor role.

**Architecture:**
- **One permission class.** `etqan.platform.permissions.HasCode` checks the code a view declares in `permission_codes` (HTTP method or viewset action → code, or a tuple meaning any one of them). Admins always pass; a staff account passes when one of its active roles holds the code; nobody else ever does. Views that also serve teachers, students or parents keep those branches (`HasCode | (ReadOnly & ReadsOwn)`, `HasCode | IsTeacher`, …).
- **The platform stays free of business imports.** A user's codes come from a loader that `etqan.access` registers when it starts (`register_code_loader`). They are loaded once per request and cached on `request.user`: one query for staff, none for admins.
- **A new tenant app, `etqan.access`.** It holds:
  - the registry (`registry.py`): 22 resources × TutorHamster's 12 verbs, 4 pages, 5 widgets, and which codes are in use;
  - `StaffRole` (names, codes, active, members → User);
  - the Supervisor preset;
  - the staff and roles services, with the §4.2 escalation guards;
  - the `access/` API (registry, roles, staff).
- **Staff accounts.** `User.Role` gains `staff`. Staff accounts are created, invited, edited and deactivated through `identity.services` (Plan 1's invite flow); `access` reaches identity only through those services.
- **Data scope.** Past a route's code, staff see what admins see (R-5): the scope helpers and the admin-only payload flags use `is_office(user)`.
- **Dashboard.**
  - `can(me, code)` reads `me/.permissions`; `CanProvider`/`useCan` narrow only staff accounts.
  - Office layouts guard by role (`requireOffice`); every office screen names its code (`staticData.permission`), and `PermissionGate` shows "You don't have access to this page." without it.
  - The nav, the home's revenue widget and every write action follow the codes.
  - New screens: Settings → Roles (a list and a matrix editor) and People → Staff (a list with Add and Edit dialogs).

**Tech Stack:** Django 6 / DRF 3.18 / django-tenants 3.14, PostgreSQL 18; React 19 + TanStack Router/Query + Vite (base `/app/`), react-hook-form + zod 4, i18next, Radix UI; Playwright through the Caddy edge.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-28-roles-permissions-design.md`, Phase B1, plan 3 of 4. This plan covers its **12a** part only:
- §1–§3.4: accounts, roles, the registry and the preset;
- §4.1–§4.3: enforcement, the staff and roles admin, `me/`;
- §5.1: the API;
- §6.1: the dashboard;
- §7: the 12a seeds;
- §8: the 12a tests.

Session supervision (§3.5, §4.4, §5.2, §6.2) is Plan 12b. This plan only defines `session.supervise` in the registry, not in use yet (§3.3), and puts it in the Supervisor preset (§3.4), so 12b switches it on without a data migration. It builds on:
- Plan 1: one `User` per academy with one `role`, the invite flow and `me/`;
- Plans 3–11: every admin-only route and screen this plan converts.

Where this plan fills a gap in the spec, the Decisions below say so.

**Verified:** a script applied the code in Tasks 1–13 in this plan's order, exactly as written here, to fresh copies of `backend@981be5f` and `dashboard@8726108` (the current `main` of each). That covers every `Create`, `replace … with` and `Delete` block, the `makemigrations` command and the route-tree regeneration.
- After each task, the resulting tree was identical to the reference branch the plan was written from; only the generated migrations' `# Generated by Django … on <date>` line differs.
- Before each task's code went in, its new tests were run and failed. The ones that passed are guards named in their task's Step 2: mostly Task 4's rows for admins and unchanged refusals, plus one test each in Tasks 3, 8 and 11.
- After each task, its format, lint, import-contract (16, then 18 kept), type, test and coverage commands passed. `ruff format --check` and `biome check` found the plan's code already formatted.
- Backend: 1427 → 1917 tests, coverage 98.0% → 98.12%.
- Dashboard: 692 → 818 tests, lines 94.37% → 94.56%, branches 88.08% → 88.99%, functions 82.08% → 82.82%.
- One of six coverage runs at Task 9's state, under heavy machine load, exited 1: every test passed, but vitest reported an "unhandled error" from a Radix toast timer firing after `StudentsList.test.tsx`'s jsdom was torn down. That file is unchanged by this plan, and five reruns there and five at the baseline were clean. It is recorded in `STATE.md`'s follow-ups.

The e2e suite then passed through Caddy, run the way the CI `e2e` job runs it:
- Django (`config.settings.local`, the file email backend, eager Celery) and the Vite preview, on a freshly migrated and seeded scratch database;
- the marketing server on `:4321`;
- a `caddy:2.11.4-alpine` edge on `:80` in front of Django on `:8000`, as in CI.

That was 21 of 21, `roles.spec.ts` included, twice on the same database, with no retry.

## Global Constraints

**Repos and branches**
- Repos: `backend/` (trunk `main`), `dashboard/` (trunk `main`), meta repo (trunk `master`).
- The meta repo is already on `feat/roles` (the spec is committed there).
- The controller creates `feat/roles` in `backend/` (from `main` at `981be5f`) and in `dashboard/` (from `main` at `8726108`) before Task 1 and Task 7. Check with `git -C backend branch --show-current` and `git -C dashboard branch --show-current`.
- Nothing is merged without the user's approval. After merge, bump the submodule pointers in meta.
- Commit messages: Conventional Commits, ending with exactly `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>`.

**Backend commands**
- Run from `backend/` with the virtualenv `backend/.venv`. Export this environment once per shell:
  `export DATABASE_URL=postgres://etqan:etqan@localhost:55432/etqan CELERY_BROKER_URL=redis://localhost:56379/0 DJANGO_SECRET_KEY=test-secret DJANGO_READ_DOT_ENV_FILE=False DJANGO_TENANT_BASE_DOMAIN=etqan.localhost`
- Tests: `.venv/bin/pytest …`. Add `--create-db` once after Task 2, which adds migrations.
- Format: `.venv/bin/ruff check --fix . && .venv/bin/ruff format .`
- Verify: `.venv/bin/ruff check . && .venv/bin/ruff format --check . && .venv/bin/lint-imports && .venv/bin/pytest -q --cov=etqan`
- Ruff selects `PL`, `FBT`, `DTZ`, `BLE`, `ERA` and `E501` (88 columns).

**Dashboard commands**
- Run from `dashboard/` through `npx pnpm@10`.
- Format: `npx pnpm@10 exec biome check --write src e2e` (it also sorts imports, so a new import may be placed anywhere before formatting).
- New route files are picked up by the TanStack Router plugin. Regenerate `src/routeTree.gen.ts` with `npx pnpm@10 exec vite build` before `tsc` (Tasks 9 and 10), and commit it.
- Verify: `npx pnpm@10 exec vite build && npx pnpm@10 tsc --noEmit && npx pnpm@10 lint && npx pnpm@10 test:coverage && npx pnpm@10 build`
- `pnpm lint` is `biome ci . && node scripts/check-colors.mjs`: no hex colour literals and no Tailwind palette utilities anywhere, comments included. Semantic tokens only.

**Coverage and dev data**
- Coverage gates: backend ≥ 80% (`pytest --cov=etqan`); dashboard lines 80 / statements 80 / branches 70 / functions 70.
- Seeded dev password `e2e-EtqanTest-2026`, for admins only. Academies:
  - `demo` (`admin@demo.test`), which also gets Sara Supervisor (`sara@demo.test`, no password, as seeded teachers have none);
  - `other` (`admin@other.test`).

**Tenancy and module boundaries**
- **The new app.** `etqan.access` goes in `TENANT_APPS` only. Its migrations run in every academy schema (`migrate_schemas`), and its data migration returns early in the public schema all the same.
- **Migrations.** `access` adds new tables only. `identity`'s `0016_alter_user_role` changes the `role` field's choices only, so there is no schema change and no NOT NULL column on an existing table; the old colour keeps serving during a blue/green switch (`STATE.md`'s standing warning).
- **The platform** imports no business module. `HasCode` gets codes from the loader `etqan.access` registers in `AccessConfig.ready()`.
- **`etqan.access`** reaches `etqan.identity` only through `identity.services` (`create_person`, `update_person`, `activate`, `deactivate`, `people_queryset`, `account_state`). Its FK and M2M point at `settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL`, never at an import.
- **Other apps** reach `access` only through `access.services` (`tenants` seeds the preset and the demo staff member).
- **`lint-imports`** goes from 16 to 18 contracts kept:
  - two new: "access reaches other apps only through their services" and "other apps reach access only through its services";
  - two extended: the platform contract and "no app imports notifications".
- **Keyword-only arguments** with no default for flags and selectors: `create_role(*, name_ar, name_en, permissions, by)`, `update_role(role, *, fields, by)`, `create_staff(*, full_name, email, role_ids, by)`, `update_staff(user, *, fields, by)`. `by` is the user asking, or `None` for seeds and tests.

**API and data rules**
- **Routes.** All under `/api/v1/`: `access/registry/`, `access/roles/`, `access/roles/<id>/`, `access/staff/`, `access/staff/<id>/`. Every existing route changes only its permission class, as the route table (D2) sets out.
- **Spec values, verbatim:**
  - A role's `name_ar` and `name_en` are required, up to 60 characters, and each is unique in the academy whatever the case.
  - Unknown codes are refused, and a retired role grants nothing.
  - A roles row is `id, name_ar, name_en, permissions, is_active, members, created_at`.
  - A staff row is `id, full_name, email, is_active, roles [{id, name_ar, name_en}], invited, created_at`.
  - `me/` gains `permissions` (every registry code for an admin, the active roles' codes for staff, none for anyone else) and `is_super_admin`.
- **Writes.** Every write answers with a fresh read. There is no `PUT` and no `DELETE` on roles or staff; a role is retired (`is_active`), never deleted.
- **Errors name their field:**
  - roles: `name_ar`, `name_en`, `permissions`;
  - staff: `full_name`, `email`, `role_ids`, `is_active`.
  - The §4.2 guards are `403 {"<field>": [message]}` (`ForbiddenError`); validation errors are `400`.
- **Filters.** Any id from a query string goes through `etqan.platform.params.as_int` (moved from `people_filters`, which re-exports it): a filter never 500s, a 5000-digit id included.
- **Query counts.** The permission check costs at most one query per request. The roles list, the staff list and `me/` stay flat as roles and members grow.
- **Other academies.** Their roles and staff are never visible or assignable, and every cross-academy test forces their pks above this academy's (`until_pk_exceeds`).

**Dashboard strings and helpers**
- Every string is in `src/locales/en/common.json` and `src/locales/ar/common.json`.
  - zod messages are i18n keys rendered through `useFieldError`;
  - server field errors land through `applyServerErrors`;
  - toasts use `errorText`.
- Where one accessible name is a prefix of another, tests match exactly: "view" and "view all", "restore" and "restore all", "Edit" and "Edit Sara".
- Screens work RTL and at phone width (the role matrix wraps its verbs; no table is wider than the page).
- Reuse `Pager`, `Fact`, `clean`, `applyServerErrors`/`errorText` and `useFieldError`; make no copies.
- A Radix dialog closes (`flushSync(() => setOpen(false))`) before any state change that can unmount its trigger (Plan 11's finding).

**Tests**
- Each new assertion fails without the code under test. Guards that pass beforehand are named in their Step 2.
- Cross-academy tests hold data in both academies, with the other academy's pk forced above this one's (`until_pk_exceeds`).
- Query counts leave out django-tenants' `SET search_path` statements.

### Decisions this plan makes where the spec is silent or conflicts with the code

- **D1 — The registry.** Resource codes and labels (spec §3.3 names the areas, not the codes):

  | Code | English | Arabic | In use |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | `student`, `teacher`, `parent`, `family` | Students, Teachers, Parents, Families | الطلاب، المعلمون، أولياء الأمور، العائلات | view, view_any, create, update |
  | `tag` | Tags | الوسوم | view_any, create, update |
  | `course`, `package` | Courses, Packages | الدورات، الباقات | view, view_any, create, update, delete, replicate |
  | `subscription` | Subscriptions | الاشتراكات | view, view_any, create, update, delete |
  | `session` | Sessions | الحصص | view, view_any, create, update (+ `supervise`, defined, not in use) |
  | `attendance` | Attendance | الحضور | update |
  | `session_report` | Session reports | تقارير الحصص | view, view_any, update |
  | `invoice` | Invoices | الفواتير | view, view_any, create, update |
  | `payment` | Payments | المدفوعات | view_any, create, delete |
  | `payroll_rate`, `payroll_adjustment` | Payroll rates, Payroll adjustments | أسعار الرواتب، تسويات الرواتب | view_any, create, update, delete |
  | `payslip` | Payslips | كشوف الرواتب | view, view_any, create, update |
  | `academy_settings`, `notification_settings` | Academy settings, Notification settings | إعدادات الأكاديمية، إعدادات الإشعارات | view, update |
  | `site` | Site pages and branding | صفحات الموقع والهوية | view, view_any, create, update, delete |
  | `inquiry` | Inquiries | الاستفسارات | view_any, update |
  | `staff` | Staff | الموظفون | view, view_any, create, update |
  | `role` | Roles | الأدوار | view, view_any, create, update (of its 6 verbs) |

  - Every resource carries TutorHamster's 12 verbs, except `role` (6, RBAC-003) and `session` (12 + `supervise`).
  - The in-use set is exactly the codes the routes declare. `test_every_code_in_the_table_is_in_the_registry_and_in_use` keeps the two equal.
  - Pages: `page.quick_translate`, `page.themes`, `page.two_factor`, `page.system_status`, all greyed (R-7).
  - Widgets:
    - `widget.revenue_stats` is in use. It gates the home's billing summary card and its route, `billing/summary/`.
    - `widget.students_stats`, `widget.subscriptions_stats`, `widget.sessions_stats` and `widget.students_overview` are greyed: the dashboard has no such card yet. The Today board is a screen, gated by `session.view_any`.
- **D2 — The route table.** It is below, and `etqan/access/tests/test_routes.py` holds it as `ROUTES`. Where §4.1's verb rule is silent:
  - `DELETE` is `delete`; the catalogue's `duplicate` is `replicate`; a `PUT` (the site viewsets, writing a report) is `update`.
  - Adding, editing, ending or deleting a subscription's pauses and slots changes the subscription: `subscription.update`. A subscription's session list and the Today board are session lists: `session.view_any`.
  - A lookup that feeds a form takes the code of the write the form makes:
    - `billing/payers/` is `invoice.create`;
    - `people/families/payers/` is any of `family.create`, `family.update` or `student.update` (the students' bulk Create family);
    - `access/roles/`'s `GET` is any of `role.view_any`, `staff.create` or `staff.update` (the staff dialogs' roles picker).
  - `people/admins/` declares no code: only admins see, create or edit admins (§4.2).
  - `academy/settings/`'s `GET` keeps its `ReadOnly` branch: every signed-in user reads it (forms need the currency and the country). The code gates the Settings screen and the write.
- **D3 — How a view declares its code, and how the old compositions become code checks.**
  - An `APIView` sets `permission_codes = {"GET": "invoice.view_any", "POST": "invoice.create"}`. A viewset uses `viewset_codes("course", duplicate="replicate")`, keyed by action. The people routes compute theirs from the URL's `kind` (a property reading `self.kwargs`).
  - `required_code(view, request)` reads the action, else the method (HEAD as GET); a tuple means any one.
  - The old compositions become:
    - `IsAdmin` → `HasCode`;
    - `IsAdmin | ReadOnly` on scoped reads (subscriptions, sessions, pauses, slots) → `HasCode | (ReadOnly & ReadsOwn)`, so a staff account never reads through `ReadOnly`;
    - `IsAdmin | ReadOnly` on academy settings → `HasCode | ReadOnly`;
    - catalogue `IsAdmin | (IsTeacher & ReadOnly)` → `HasCode | (IsTeacher & ReadOnly)`;
    - billing `READERS` → `HasCode | (ReadOnly & (IsParent | IsStudent))`;
    - payroll `READERS` → `HasCode | (ReadOnly & IsTeacher)`;
    - `IsAdmin | IsTeacher` → `HasCode | IsTeacher`;
    - the report's `ANY_ROLE` → `HasCode | ReadsOwn`;
    - `site.permissions.IsAcademyAdmin` is deleted.
  - Enforcement tests:
    - the coverage test walks every `api/v1/` URL pattern and fails on any handler that is not in `ROUTES`, `ADMIN_ONLY` or `SELF_SERVICE` (with its reason), or whose declared code differs from the table;
    - a parametrised test proves, for every row, 403 without the code (with every other code held) and not-403 with it alone;
    - it also proves 403 with no role or a retired role;
    - and that admins pass every row.
- **D4 — Loading codes once.**
  - `codes_of(user)` caches `frozenset` codes on the user object (`_etqan_codes`). DRF's `request.user` is one object per request, so the check and `me/` share one query; `_me_payload` reads the codes off the caller's object before re-reading the user.
  - `access.services.codes_for` returns the registry's codes for an admin with no query, the union of the active roles' codes for staff (one `values_list` query, intersected with the registry so a stale code counts for nothing), and nothing for anyone else.
- **D5 — The escalation guards (§4.2).** Each is `403 {"<field>": [message]}`, checked before anything is written:
  - granting a role with a code the staff user lacks: `role_ids`, "You can only grant roles whose permissions you hold yourself." A role they already hold is kept, and removing roles is allowed.
  - adding a code the staff user lacks to a role: `permissions`, "You can only add permissions you hold yourself." Keeping or removing codes is allowed.
  - changing their own role set: `role_ids`, "You can't change your own roles." Resending the same set, with a new name, is allowed.
  - deactivating themselves: `is_active`, "You can't deactivate yourself."
  - admins are never staff accounts. `access/staff/<admin id>` is a 404, and `people/admins/*` is admin-only (D2). Only admins create or edit admins.
- **D6 — Staff accounts reuse identity.**
  - `User.Role.STAFF`; `create_person("staff", …)` needs an email ("A staff member needs an email address.") and refuses a profile. It sends Plan 1's invite after the request commits.
  - Staff are not a `people/<kind>/` kind: they are listed and edited only under `access/staff/`.
  - `access.services.create_staff` and `update_staff` call `identity.services`, and deactivation signs them out everywhere, as for a teacher.
  - The M2M lives on `StaffRole.members` (`related_name="staff_roles"`).
- **D7 — Data scope for staff (R-5).** `platform.permissions.is_office(user)` (admin or staff) replaces `role == "admin"` in:
  - `identity.scopes`, `catalogue.scopes`, `billing.scopes`, `payroll.scopes` and `scheduling.scopes`;
  - the billing, scheduling and payroll views' `is_admin` payload flags and their CSV check;
  - the session row's staff-only fields;
  - `reports.can_read_report`;
  - the attendance undo.

  Notifications stay admin-only (R-7): `recipients`, `links` and `active_admins()` are unchanged, so staff receive no notices.
- **D8 — Dashboard gating.**
  - `can(me, code)`: an admin always; a staff account when `me.permissions` lists the code; nobody else.
  - `CanProvider` (in the app shell) and `useCan()`: inside the shell only a staff account is narrowed. A teacher's, student's or parent's own actions are checked by role on the server, so shared components (attendance, reports) hide nothing from them. Outside the shell (a unit test, a printed page) nothing is hidden.
  - The office layouts use `requireOffice` (admin or staff; others go home, as before). Every office screen names `staticData.permission`: a code, or `newOrView(param, resource)` for a detail route whose "new" page creates. People → Admins names `ADMINS_ONLY`.
  - `PermissionGate` in `_authed` renders `NoAccess` ("You don't have access to this page.", a heading, and a link home) when a match's code is missing. A test walks the route tree and fails on an office screen without a code.
  - The nav's office items carry their code. Website shows for any of its tabs' codes, and `/website` opens on the first tab the user may see.
  - The actions follow the route table:
    - create entry points, row and page actions, bulk selections and Generate buttons are hidden without their code;
    - a detail form a staff account may see but not change is read-only: `useForm({ disabled })` disables every registered and controlled field, and Save is hidden.
- **D9 — Where Staff and Roles live, and the matrix editor.**
  - People → Staff sits after Teachers. Settings → Roles sits after Tags.
  - The editor is a page, `/settings/roles/new` and `/settings/roles/$roleId`, not a dialog: 22 resources × up to 13 verbs don't fit one.
    - Each resource is a fieldset (its legend names it) with "Select all of {name}" and its verbs as wrapping checkboxes, so there is no horizontal scroll at phone width. There is "Select every permission" above the tabs Resources · Pages · Widgets.
    - A verb not in use is dimmed and labelled "(not used yet)", but stays checkable: R-1 says such codes are "stored and shown", and the preset holds some.
  - The Arabic "Admins" nav item becomes "المديرون", which no longer clashes with the Supervisor preset's "مشرف".
- **D10 — Roles and assignment.**
  - `members` counts the role's active staff accounts.
  - A staff row lists every role the account holds, retired ones too. The dialog offers active roles, plus the retired ones already held, marked "(retired)".
  - A retired role is kept when already held but never newly given: "A retired role can't be added.", as for tags (Plan 10 D4).
  - Unknown or other-academy role ids are `400 {"role_ids": ["Choose roles from the list."]}`.
- **D11 — The Supervisor preset (§3.4).**
  - Its 20 codes: `view` and `view_any` on the eight resources, `update` on sessions, attendance and session reports, and `session.supervise`, held now so 12b needs no data migration.
  - It is seeded by `0002_supervisor_preset` in every academy and by `create_academy`, idempotent by either name whatever the case, and editable.
- **D12 — Seeds (§7).** `seed_staff` ensures the preset in every academy, and in `demo` adds Sara Supervisor holding it (no invite, no password, like the seeded teachers). An existing account with her email is left alone, and a missing or retired preset prints `skip:`.
- **D13 — `Me` in the dashboard.** `permissions` and `is_super_admin` are optional in the `Me` type (the server always sends them), so the older `Me` fixtures need not spell them out; `can()` treats a missing list as empty.

### The route table (D2)

| Route | Method → code |
|---|---|
| `people/students/`, `parents/`, `teachers/` | GET → `<role>.view_any`; POST → `<role>.create` |
| `people/<kind>/<id>/` | GET → `<role>.view`; PATCH → `<role>.update` |
| `people/<kind>/<id>/invite\|password-reset\|deactivate\|activate/` | POST → `<role>.update` |
| `people/admins/…` (all of the above) | no code: admins only |
| `people/students/bulk/` | POST → `student.update` |
| `people/students/<id>/guardians/` | GET → `student.view`; POST → `student.update` |
| `people/students/<id>/guardians/<parent_id>/` | DELETE → `student.update` |
| `people/parents/<id>/children/` | GET → `parent.view` |
| `people/tags/` · `tags/<id>/` | GET → `tag.view_any`; POST → `tag.create` · PATCH → `tag.update` |
| `people/families/` · `families/<id>/` | GET → `family.view_any`; POST → `family.create` · GET → `family.view`; PATCH → `family.update` |
| `people/families/payers/` | GET → any of `family.create`, `family.update`, `student.update` |
| `catalogue/courses/`, `packages/` (viewsets) | list → `view_any`; retrieve → `view`; create → `create`; partial_update → `update`; destroy → `delete`; duplicate → `replicate` |
| `academy/settings/` | GET → `academy_settings.view` (and every signed-in user); PATCH → `academy_settings.update` |
| `notifications/settings/` | GET → `notification_settings.view`; PATCH → `notification_settings.update` |
| `site/admin/branding/`, `landing/` | GET → `site.view`; PATCH → `site.update` |
| `site/admin/pages/`, `testimonials/` (viewsets) | list → `site.view_any`; retrieve → `site.view`; create → `site.create`; update/partial_update → `site.update`; destroy → `site.delete` |
| `site/admin/inquiries/` · `<id>/handle/` | GET → `inquiry.view_any` · POST → `inquiry.update` |
| `subscriptions/` · `<id>/` | GET → `view_any`; POST → `create` · GET → `view`; PATCH → `update`; DELETE → `delete` (all `subscription.*`) |
| `subscriptions/<id>/renew/`, `cancel/` | POST → `subscription.update` |
| `subscriptions/<id>/pauses/`, `slots/` | GET → `subscription.view`; POST → `subscription.update` |
| `pauses/<id>/` · `pauses/<id>/end/` · `slots/<id>/` | DELETE, POST, PATCH, DELETE → `subscription.update` |
| `subscriptions/<id>/sessions/`, `schedule/today/`, `sessions/` | GET → `session.view_any` |
| `schedule/generate/` | POST → `session.create` |
| `sessions/bulk/`, `sessions/<id>/cancel/`, `restore/` | POST → `session.update` |
| `sessions/<id>/` | GET → `session.view` |
| `sessions/<id>/attendance/` | POST → `attendance.update` (teachers keep `IsTeacher`) |
| `sessions/<id>/report/` | GET → `session_report.view`; PUT → `session_report.update` |
| `reports/missing/` | GET → `session_report.view_any` |
| `billing/invoices/` · `<id>/` · `<id>/void/` | GET → `view_any`; POST → `create` · GET → `view`; PATCH → `update` · POST → `update` (all `invoice.*`) |
| `billing/invoices/<id>/payments/` · `billing/payments/<id>/` | GET → `payment.view_any`; POST → `payment.create` · DELETE → `payment.delete` |
| `billing/summary/` · `billing/payers/` | GET → `widget.revenue_stats` · GET → `invoice.create` |
| `payroll/rates/` · `<id>/` | GET → `view_any`; POST → `create` · PATCH → `update`; DELETE → `delete` (all `payroll_rate.*`) |
| `payroll/adjustments/` · `<id>/` | the same, `payroll_adjustment.*` |
| `payroll/payslips/` · `generate/` · `<id>/` · `issue/`, `mark-paid/` | GET → `view_any` · POST → `create` · GET → `view` · POST → `update` (all `payslip.*`) |
| `access/registry/` | GET → `role.view_any` |
| `access/roles/` · `<id>/` | GET → any of `role.view_any`, `staff.create`, `staff.update`; POST → `role.create` · GET → `role.view`; PATCH → `role.update` |
| `access/staff/` · `<id>/` | GET → `staff.view_any`; POST → `staff.create` · GET → `staff.view`; PATCH → `staff.update` |
| **Exempt (self-service):** `identity/csrf, resend-verification, verify-email, login, logout, me, me/password, password/reset(/confirm), me/emails(/…)`; the caller's own `notifications/`, `unread-count/`, `read-all/`, `<id>/read/`; the public `site/`, `site/branding/`, `site/pages/<slug>/`, `site/inquiries/`; `schema/`, `docs/`, `redoc/` (DocsPermission) | — |

## Review Focus

- **A staff account editing itself.** The Edit dialog sends the whole form, its own unchanged roles included. That must save, and only a changed role set or deactivation is refused, with a 403 on that field. Tests:
  - Task 3 `test_staff_never_change_their_own_roles` (the same set with a new name passes) and `test_a_staff_manager_meets_every_guard`;
  - Task 10 `StaffDialog` "never lets anyone change their own roles or deactivate themselves".
- **A retired role.** It must grant nothing yet keep its members, count again once restored, never be newly given, and stay on an account that already holds it. Tests:
  - Task 2 `test_a_retired_role_counts_again_once_restored`;
  - Task 3 `test_a_retired_role_already_held_is_kept` and `test_a_retired_role_cannot_be_given`;
  - Task 4 `test_a_staff_account_with_no_role_or_a_retired_one_gets_nothing`.
- **A teacher on a screen shared with the office.** Attendance controls, the report form and the missing reports list must hide nothing from a teacher, whose actions are checked by role, never by a code. Test: Task 7 `useCan` "hides nothing from a teacher: their own actions are checked by role".
- **Junk ids.** `?role=x`, `-1`, `²` and a 5000-digit id must be ignored, never a 500; `role_ids` of `["x"]`, `null` or another academy's id must be a 400 on `role_ids`. Tests:
  - Task 3 `test_the_list_searches_and_filters_and_ignores_junk`, `test_unknown_roles_are_refused_and_nothing_is_written` and `test_another_academys_roles_and_staff_are_never_reachable`.
- **A staff account holding every code.** It must still be refused on admin accounts, and every route must answer 403 to a staff account holding every code but the route's own. Tests:
  - Task 4 `test_admin_accounts_are_for_admins_only` and `test_staff_pass_with_the_code_and_get_403_without_it`.

### Task 1: Platform: `HasCode`, codes loaded once per request, and a 403 that names its field

**Files:**
- Modify: `backend/etqan/platform/permissions.py`, `backend/etqan/platform/exceptions.py`, `backend/etqan/platform/drf.py`
- Test: `backend/etqan/platform/tests/test_codes.py` (new), `backend/etqan/platform/tests/test_drf.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `role_of(user)`, `ReadOnly`, `IsTeacher`, `IsStudent`, `IsParent` (Plan 1/3), and the platform's `EtqanError` and exception handler.
- Produces, in `etqan.platform.permissions`:
  - `OFFICE_ROLES = {"admin", "staff"}`, `OWN_DATA_ROLES = {"teacher", "student", "parent"}` and `VIEWSET_VERBS`;
  - `is_office(user) -> bool`;
  - `class ReadsOwn(BasePermission)`: a teacher, student or parent;
  - `register_code_loader(loader)`, where `loader(user) -> Iterable[str]`;
  - `codes_of(user) -> frozenset[str]`, cached on the user object as `_etqan_codes`;
  - `viewset_codes(resource, **verbs) -> dict[str, str]`;
  - `required_code(view, request) -> str | tuple[str, ...] | None`;
  - `class HasCode(BasePermission)`, which reads `view.permission_codes`.
- Produces, in `etqan.platform.exceptions`: `ForbiddenError(message, field=None)`, answered `403 {field: [message]}` by the platform's exception handler.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

§4.1's permission class:
- admins always pass, without loading codes;
- staff pass with the declared code only, HEAD as GET, and a tuple means any one;
- teachers, students, parents and visitors never pass a code check;
- a view without codes is for admins only;
- codes are loaded once per user object;
- viewsets are checked by action;
- `is_office` and `ReadsOwn` name the right roles;
- `ForbiddenError` is a 403 that names its field.

Create `backend/etqan/platform/tests/test_codes.py`:

```python
"""Plan 12 §4.1: the one permission class, and codes loaded once per user."""

from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from etqan.platform import permissions
from etqan.platform.permissions import HasCode
from etqan.platform.permissions import ReadsOwn
from etqan.platform.permissions import codes_of
from etqan.platform.permissions import is_office
from etqan.platform.permissions import required_code
from etqan.platform.permissions import viewset_codes


@pytest.fixture
def loader(monkeypatch):
    calls = []

    def load(user):
        calls.append(user)
        return user.granted

    monkeypatch.setitem(permissions._LOADER, "codes", load)  # noqa: SLF001
    return calls


def _user(role, *granted, authenticated=True):
    return SimpleNamespace(is_authenticated=authenticated, role=role, granted=granted)


def _request(user, method="GET"):
    return SimpleNamespace(user=user, method=method)


class _View:
    permission_codes = {"GET": "invoice.view_any", "POST": "invoice.create"}


class _Either:
    permission_codes = {"GET": ("family.create", "family.update")}


def allowed(user, method="GET", view=None):
    return HasCode().has_permission(_request(user, method), view or _View())


def test_admins_always_pass_without_loading_codes(loader):
    assert allowed(_user("admin"))
    assert allowed(_user("admin"), "DELETE")
    assert loader == []


def test_staff_pass_with_the_declared_code_only(loader):
    clerk = _user("staff", "invoice.view_any")
    assert allowed(clerk)
    assert not allowed(clerk, "POST")
    assert not allowed(clerk, "PUT")


def test_head_is_checked_as_get(loader):
    assert allowed(_user("staff", "invoice.view_any"), "HEAD")


def test_a_tuple_means_any_one_of_the_codes(loader):
    assert allowed(_user("staff", "family.update"), view=_Either())
    assert not allowed(_user("staff", "family.view"), view=_Either())


@pytest.mark.parametrize("role", ["teacher", "student", "parent", None])
def test_nobody_else_ever_passes_a_code_check(loader, role):
    user = _user(role, "invoice.view_any", authenticated=role is not None)
    assert not allowed(user)


def test_a_view_without_codes_is_for_admins_only(loader):
    assert allowed(_user("admin"), view=object())
    assert not allowed(_user("staff", "invoice.view_any"), view=object())


def test_codes_are_loaded_once_per_user_object(loader):
    clerk = _user("staff", "invoice.view_any")
    for _ in range(3):
        assert codes_of(clerk) == frozenset({"invoice.view_any"})
    assert loader == [clerk]
    assert codes_of(_user(None, "x", authenticated=False)) == frozenset()


def test_viewsets_are_checked_by_action():
    view = SimpleNamespace(
        action="duplicate",
        permission_codes=viewset_codes("course", duplicate="replicate"),
    )
    assert required_code(view, _request(None, "POST")) == "course.replicate"
    assert viewset_codes("site") == {
        "list": "site.view_any",
        "retrieve": "site.view",
        "create": "site.create",
        "update": "site.update",
        "partial_update": "site.update",
        "destroy": "site.delete",
    }


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("role", "office", "own"),
    [
        ("admin", True, False),
        ("staff", True, False),
        ("teacher", False, True),
        ("student", False, True),
        ("parent", False, True),
    ],
)
def test_office_and_own_data_roles(role, office, own):
    user = _user(role)
    assert is_office(user) is office
    assert ReadsOwn().has_permission(_request(user), None) is own
    assert not is_office(_user(role, authenticated=False))
```

```python
class ForbiddenError(EtqanError):
    """A 403 that names its field: the caller may use this route but not to
    do this (Plan 12 §4.2, a staff user granting what they don't hold)."""

    def __init__(self, message: str, field: str | None = None):
        self.field = field
        super().__init__(message=message, code="forbidden")
```

```python
# Admins and staff run the academy (Plan 12 R-5): past a route's permission
# check they see all of its data, where teachers, students and parents see
# only their own.
OFFICE_ROLES = frozenset({"admin", "staff"})
OWN_DATA_ROLES = frozenset({"teacher", "student", "parent"})
# DRF viewset actions and the verb each needs (spec §4.1).
VIEWSET_VERBS = {
    "list": "view_any",
    "retrieve": "view",
    "create": "create",
    "update": "update",
    "partial_update": "update",
    "destroy": "delete",
}
_LOADER: dict = {}
```

In `backend/etqan/platform/permissions.py`, replace:

```python

        return role_of(request.user) is not None and request.method in SAFE_METHODS
```

with:

```python
    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        return role_of(request.user) is not None and request.method in SAFE_METHODS


class ReadsOwn(BasePermission):
    """A teacher, student or parent: their routes' scopes leave them only
    their own rows. Compose: `[HasCode | (ReadOnly & ReadsOwn)]`."""

    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        return role_of(request.user) in OWN_DATA_ROLES


def is_office(user) -> bool:
    """An admin or a staff account (R-5): sees every row of what it may view."""
    return role_of(user) in OFFICE_ROLES


def register_code_loader(loader) -> None:
    """``etqan.access`` hands over ``loader(user) -> Iterable[str]`` when it
    starts: the platform never imports a business module."""
    _LOADER["codes"] = loader


def codes_of(user) -> frozenset[str]:
    """The permission codes ``user`` holds, loaded once per user object: a
    request's ``request.user`` is one object, so one query per request."""
    if role_of(user) is None:
        return frozenset()
    codes = getattr(user, "_etqan_codes", None)
    if codes is None:
        loader = _LOADER.get("codes")
        codes = frozenset(loader(user)) if loader else frozenset()
        user._etqan_codes = codes  # noqa: SLF001
    return codes


def viewset_codes(resource: str, **verbs: str) -> dict[str, str]:
    """A viewset's codes by action: the standard ones, plus ``verbs`` for its
    extra actions (``duplicate="replicate"``)."""
    return {
        action: f"{resource}.{verb}"
        for action, verb in {**VIEWSET_VERBS, **verbs}.items()
    }


def required_code(view, request) -> str | tuple[str, ...] | None:
    """The code ``view`` declares for this request: by viewset action, else by
    method (HEAD as GET). A tuple means any one of them. None: admins only."""
    codes = getattr(view, "permission_codes", None) or {}
    action = getattr(view, "action", None)
    if action:
        return codes.get(action)
    method = "GET" if request.method == "HEAD" else request.method
    return codes.get(method)


class HasCode(BasePermission):
    """Plan 12 §4.1: admins always pass; a staff account passes when one of
    its active roles holds the code the view declares; nobody else ever
    does. Views declare ``permission_codes`` (method or action → code)."""

    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        role = role_of(request.user)
        if role == "admin":
            return True
        if role != "staff":
            return False
        code = required_code(view, request)
        wanted = (code,) if isinstance(code, str) else code or ()
        held = codes_of(request.user)
        return any(c in held for c in wanted)
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

- **The registry (§3.3):** the codes are unique; every label exists in both languages; 12 verbs, with 6 for `role` and 12 + `supervise` for `session`; `session.supervise` is defined but not in use; four greyed pages and one widget in use; the payload's shape.
- **Roles (§3.2):** created active, with codes sorted and deduplicated; an unknown code is refused; each name is required, at most 60 characters, and unique in either language whatever the case (a race too); renamed, recoded, retired and restored; an unknown field and an unknown role are refused; `member_count` counts active holders.
- **Codes (§4.3):** an admin holds every code with no query; staff hold the union of their active roles in one query; a code gone from the registry counts for nothing; a restored role counts again; nobody else holds any.
- **Escalation (§4.2):** a staff user adds only codes it holds, while keeping and removing are allowed; an admin adds any.
- **The Supervisor preset (§3.4):** its 20 codes; it is in every academy and never in the public schema; it is seeded once and keeps an academy's edits; `create_academy` ensures it inside the new schema.
- **The staff account type (§3.1):** it is invited, needs an email and has no profile.

Create `backend/etqan/access/tests/__init__.py`: an empty file.

Create `backend/etqan/access/tests/test_registry.py`:

```python
"""Plan 12a §3.3: the permission registry."""

from etqan.access import registry


def test_codes_are_unique_and_well_formed():
    codes = [
        *registry.RESOURCE_CODES,
        *(entry.code for entry in (*registry.PAGES, *registry.WIDGETS)),
    ]
    assert len(codes) == len(set(codes)) == len(registry.CODES)
    resources = [r.code for r in registry.RESOURCES]
    assert len(resources) == len(set(resources)) == 22
    for code in codes:
        head, _, verb = code.partition(".")
        assert head, code
        assert verb, code


def test_every_label_exists_in_both_languages():
    for resource in registry.RESOURCES:
        assert resource.label_en, resource.code
        assert resource.label_ar, resource.code
        for verb in resource.verbs:
            assert all(registry.VERB_LABELS[verb]), verb
    for entry in (*registry.PAGES, *registry.WIDGETS):
        assert entry.label_en, entry.code
        assert entry.label_ar, entry.code


def test_resources_carry_the_12_verbs_and_the_role_resource_6():
    assert len(registry.ALL_VERBS) == 12
    by_code = {r.code: r for r in registry.RESOURCES}
    assert by_code["role"].verbs == (
        "view",
        "view_any",
        "create",
        "update",
        "delete",
        "delete_any",
    )
    assert by_code["session"].verbs == (*registry.ALL_VERBS, "supervise")
    for code, resource in by_code.items():
        if code not in ("role", "session"):
            assert resource.verbs == registry.ALL_VERBS, code


def test_in_use_verbs_are_the_resources_own_and_supervise_waits_for_12b():
    for resource in registry.RESOURCES:
        assert set(resource.in_use) <= set(resource.verbs), resource.code
    assert "session.supervise" in registry.CODES
    assert "session.supervise" not in registry.IN_USE
    assert registry.IN_USE <= registry.CODES


def test_pages_are_listed_greyed_and_one_widget_is_in_use():
    assert [p.code for p in registry.PAGES] == [
        "page.quick_translate",
        "page.themes",
        "page.two_factor",
        "page.system_status",
    ]
    assert not any(p.in_use for p in registry.PAGES)
    assert [w.code for w in registry.WIDGETS if w.in_use] == ["widget.revenue_stats"]
    assert len(registry.WIDGETS) == 5


def test_the_payload_lists_verbs_resources_pages_and_widgets():
    body = registry.payload()
    assert body["verbs"][0] == {"code": "view", "label_en": "view", "label_ar": "عرض"}
    assert body["verbs"][-1]["code"] == "supervise"
    tag = next(r for r in body["resources"] if r["code"] == "tag")
    assert tag == {
        "code": "tag",
        "label_en": "Tags",
        "label_ar": "الوسوم",
        "verbs": list(registry.ALL_VERBS),
        "in_use": ["view_any", "create", "update"],
    }
    assert body["pages"][0] == {
        "code": "page.quick_translate",
        "label_en": "Quick translate",
        "label_ar": "الترجمة السريعة",
        "in_use": False,
    }
    assert [w["code"] for w in body["widgets"] if w["in_use"]] == [
        "widget.revenue_stats"
    ]
```

```python
"""Plan 12a §3.2, §3.4, §4.2, §4.3: roles, the Supervisor preset and the
codes a user holds."""

import importlib
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from django.apps import apps
from django.db import IntegrityError
from django.db import connection
from django.db import transaction
from django.test.utils import CaptureQueriesContext
from django_tenants.utils import schema_context
from django_tenants.utils import tenant_context

from etqan.access import presets
from etqan.access import registry
from etqan.access import services
from etqan.access.models import StaffRole
from etqan.identity.models import User
from etqan.platform.exceptions import ForbiddenError
from etqan.platform.exceptions import NotFoundError
from etqan.platform.exceptions import ValidationError
from etqan.platform.permissions import codes_of

pytestmark = pytest.mark.django_db
preset_migration = importlib.import_module(
    "etqan.access.migrations.0002_supervisor_preset"
)


def user(role="staff", name="Sara"):
    return User.objects.create_user(
        email=f"{name.lower()}@x.test", full_name=name, role=role
    )


def role(name, *codes, active=True, members=()):
    made = StaffRole.objects.create(
        name_en=name, name_ar=f"{name} ع", permissions=list(codes), is_active=active
    )
    made.members.add(*members)
    return made


def reads(queries) -> list[str]:
    """The queries a block ran, less django-tenants' ``SET search_path``."""
    return [q["sql"] for q in queries if not q["sql"].startswith("SET search_path")]


def refused(error, field, call, *args, **kwargs):
    with pytest.raises(error) as exc:
        call(*args, **kwargs)
    assert exc.value.field == field, exc.value.message
    return exc.value.message


class TestCodes:
    def test_an_admin_holds_every_code_without_a_query(self):
        boss = user("admin", "Boss")
        with CaptureQueriesContext(connection) as queries:
            assert services.codes_for(boss) == registry.CODES
        assert reads(queries) == []

    def test_staff_hold_the_union_of_their_active_roles_in_one_query(self):
        sara = user()
        role("Clerk", "invoice.view_any", "invoice.view", members=[sara])
        role("Reader", "student.view_any", "invoice.view", members=[sara])
        role("Retired", "payslip.view_any", active=False, members=[sara])
        role("Others", "course.view_any", members=[user(name="Omar")])
        with CaptureQueriesContext(connection) as queries:
            held = services.codes_for(sara)
        assert held == {"invoice.view_any", "invoice.view", "student.view_any"}
        assert len(reads(queries)) == 1

    def test_a_retired_role_counts_again_once_restored(self):
        sara = user()
        clerk = role("Clerk", "invoice.view", active=False, members=[sara])
        assert services.codes_for(sara) == frozenset()
        services.update_role(clerk, fields={"is_active": True}, by=None)
        assert services.codes_for(sara) == {"invoice.view"}

    def test_a_code_no_longer_in_the_registry_counts_for_nothing(self):
        sara = user()
        role("Old", "invoice.view", members=[sara])
        StaffRole.objects.filter(name_en="Old").update(
            permissions=["invoice.view", "gone.view"]
        )
        assert services.codes_for(sara) == {"invoice.view"}

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("kind", ["teacher", "student", "parent"])
    def test_other_account_types_hold_nothing(self, kind):
        person = user(kind, "Person")
        role("Mixed", "invoice.view", members=[person])
        assert services.codes_for(person) == frozenset()

    def test_the_platform_loads_them_through_this_app(self):
        sara = user()
        role("Clerk", "invoice.view", members=[sara])
        assert codes_of(sara) == {"invoice.view"}

            name_ar=" محاسب ",

    @pytest.mark.parametrize(

    )

        refused(
            ValidationError,

            by=None,
        )

    @pytest.mark.parametrize(
        ("field", "value"),

    )

        names = {"name_en": "New", "name_ar": "جديد", field: value}
        refused(

            **names,

            by=None,
        )

    def test_a_name_over_60_characters_is_refused(self):

    def test_members_count_active_holders_only(self):
        sara, omar = user(), user(name="Omar")

class TestEscalation:
    """§4.2: a staff user adds only codes it holds itself."""

    def test_staff_create_roles_only_from_codes_they_hold(self):
        sara = user()
        role("Mine", "role.create", "invoice.view", members=[sara])
        made = services.create_role(
            name_ar="ب", name_en="B", permissions=["invoice.view"], by=sara
        )
        assert made.permissions == ["invoice.view"]
        message = refused(
            ForbiddenError,
            "permissions",
            services.create_role,
            name_ar="ج",
            name_en="C",
            permissions=["invoice.view", "payslip.view"],
            by=sara,
        )
        assert message == "You can only add permissions you hold yourself."
        assert not StaffRole.objects.filter(name_en="C").exists()

    def test_staff_keep_or_remove_codes_they_lack_but_never_add_one(self):
        sara = user()
        role("Mine", "role.update", "invoice.view", members=[sara])
        theirs = role("Theirs", "payslip.view", "payslip.update")
        services.update_role(
            theirs, fields={"permissions": ["payslip.view", "invoice.view"]}, by=sara
        )
        assert services.get_role(theirs.pk).permissions == [
            "invoice.view",
            "payslip.view",
        ]
        refused(
            ForbiddenError,
            "permissions",
            services.update_role,
            theirs,
            fields={"permissions": ["payslip.view", "payslip.update"]},
            by=sara,
        )

    def test_admins_add_any_code(self):
        boss = user("admin", "Boss")
        made = services.create_role(
            name_ar="د", name_en="D", permissions=sorted(registry.CODES), by=boss
        )
        assert len(made.permissions) == len(registry.CODES)


class TestSupervisorPreset:
    def test_it_holds_what_the_spec_lists_and_nothing_on_money_or_settings(self):
        codes = set(presets.SUPERVISOR["permissions"])
        assert codes <= registry.CODES
        for resource in (
            "student",
            "teacher",
            "parent",
            "family",
            "subscription",
            "session",
            "attendance",
            "session_report",
        ):
            assert {f"{resource}.view", f"{resource}.view_any"} <= codes
        assert {"session.update", "attendance.update", "session_report.update"} <= codes
        assert "session.supervise" in codes
        assert len(codes) == 20
        for head in ("invoice", "payment", "payslip", "payroll_rate", "site", "staff"):
            assert not any(code.startswith(f"{head}.") for code in codes), head
        assert not any("settings" in code or code.startswith("role.") for code in codes)

    def test_every_academy_starts_with_it(self, tenants):
        for academy in (tenants.main, tenants.other):
            with tenant_context(academy):
                preset = StaffRole.objects.get(name_en="Supervisor")
                assert preset.name_ar == "مشرف"
                assert preset.permissions == presets.SUPERVISOR["permissions"]
                assert preset.is_active

    def test_the_public_schema_gets_no_role(self):
        StaffRole.objects.all().delete()
        preset_migration.forwards(
            apps, SimpleNamespace(connection=SimpleNamespace(schema_name="public"))
        )
        assert not StaffRole.objects.exists()
        with schema_context("public"):
            assert "access_staffrole" not in connection.introspection.table_names()

    def test_it_is_seeded_once_and_keeps_an_academys_edits(self):
        preset = StaffRole.objects.get(name_en="Supervisor")
        preset.name_en = "SUPERVISOR"
        preset.permissions = ["session.view"]
        preset.save()
        editor = SimpleNamespace(connection=connection)
        preset_migration.forwards(apps, editor)
        assert services.ensure_supervisor_preset() is False
        assert StaffRole.objects.count() == 1
        assert StaffRole.objects.get().permissions == ["session.view"]
        StaffRole.objects.all().delete()
        assert services.ensure_supervisor_preset() is True
        assert services.ensure_supervisor_preset() is False
        assert StaffRole.objects.count() == 1
```

```python
"""Plan 12a §3.1: the staff account type."""

import pytest
from django.core import mail

from etqan.identity import services
from etqan.identity.models import User
from etqan.platform.exceptions import ValidationError

pytestmark = pytest.mark.django_db


def test_staff_is_an_account_type():
    assert User.Role.STAFF == "staff"
    assert ("staff", "Staff") in User.Role.choices


def test_a_staff_member_is_created_invited_and_without_a_profile(
    django_capture_on_commit_callbacks,
):
    with django_capture_on_commit_callbacks(execute=True):
        sara = services.create_person("staff", full_name="Sara", email="Sara@X.test")
    assert (sara.role, sara.email, sara.has_usable_password()) == (
        "staff",
        "Sara@x.test",
        False,
    )
    assert services.account_state(sara) == "invited"
    assert services.get_profile_types(sara.pk) == []
    assert services.get_person("staff", sara.pk) == sara

def test_a_staff_member_needs_an_email_and_has_no_profile():
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError) as exc:
        services.create_person("staff", full_name="Sara")
    assert (exc.value.field, exc.value.message) == (
        "email",
        "A staff member needs an email address.",
    )
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError) as exc:
        services.create_person(
            "staff", full_name="Sara", email="s@x.test", profile={"notes": "x"}
        )
    assert exc.value.field == "notes"
    assert not User.objects.filter(role="staff").exists()
```

```python
from django.apps import AppConfig


class AccessConfig(AppConfig):
    default_auto_field = "django.db.models.BigAutoField"
    name = "etqan.access"
    label = "access"
    verbose_name = "Staff roles and permissions"

    def ready(self):
        # The platform's HasCode asks this app for a user's codes; the
        # platform never imports a business module.
        from etqan.access import services  # noqa: PLC0415
        from etqan.platform.permissions import register_code_loader  # noqa: PLC0415

        register_code_loader(services.codes_for)
```

Create `backend/etqan/access/models.py`:

```python
from django.conf import settings
from django.db import models
from django.db.models.functions import Lower


class StaffRole(models.Model):
    """A set of permission codes an academy gives its staff (Plan 12 §3.2).

    Never deleted: a retired role (``is_active=False``) keeps its members but
    grants nothing. ``permissions`` holds registry codes only (the services
    refuse any other)."""

    name_ar = models.CharField(max_length=60)
    name_en = models.CharField(max_length=60)
    permissions = models.JSONField(default=list, blank=True)
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    members = models.ManyToManyField(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, blank=True, related_name="staff_roles"
    )
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ("name_en", "id")
        constraints = [
            models.UniqueConstraint(
                Lower("name_en"), name="access_staffrole_name_en_ci_unique"
            ),
            models.UniqueConstraint(
                Lower("name_ar"), name="access_staffrole_name_ar_ci_unique"
            ),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"StaffRole<{self.name_en}>"
```

Create `backend/etqan/access/presets.py`:

```python
"""The Supervisor preset (Plan 12 §3.4). Plain data plus one seeding
function that works on the real ``StaffRole`` model and on the migration's
historical one alike, so the data migration and ``create_academy`` share it
(and never import each other)."""

from django.db.models import Q

_READ = ("view", "view_any")
SUPERVISOR = {
    "name_ar": "مشرف",
    "name_en": "Supervisor",
    "permissions": sorted(
        [
            *(
                f"{resource}.{verb}"
                for resource in (
                    "student",
                    "teacher",
                    "parent",
                    "family",
                    "subscription",
                    "session",
                    "attendance",
                    "session_report",
                )
                for verb in _READ
            ),
            "session.update",
            "attendance.update",
            "session_report.update",
            # Plan 12b switches it on; the preset holds it from the start.
            "session.supervise",
        ]
    ),
}


def seed(model) -> bool:
    """Add the preset to the current academy unless a role already has either
    of its names, whatever the case (idempotent by name). True if added."""
    clash = model.objects.filter(
        Q(name_en__iexact=SUPERVISOR["name_en"])
        | Q(name_ar__iexact=SUPERVISOR["name_ar"])
    )
    if clash.exists():
        return False
    model.objects.create(**SUPERVISOR)
    return True
```

Create `backend/etqan/access/registry.py`:

```python
"""The permission registry (Plan 12 §3.3): every code a staff role may hold,
defined once, in code. Roles are data, sets of these codes (R-2).

A resource permission is ``<resource>.<verb>``. Each resource marks which of
its verbs are in use: a route declares it (``HasCode``) or a screen checks
it. The others are stored and shown, greyed "not used yet" (R-1).
``etqan.access.tests.test_routes`` keeps ``in_use`` equal to the codes the
routes declare."""

from dataclasses import dataclass

# TutorHamster's 12 verbs: (code, English, Arabic).
VERBS = (
    ("view", "view", "عرض"),
    ("view_any", "view all", "عرض الكل"),
    ("create", "create", "إضافة"),
    ("update", "update", "تعديل"),
    ("restore", "restore", "استرجاع"),
    ("restore_any", "restore all", "استرجاع الكل"),
    ("replicate", "replicate", "استنساخ"),
    ("reorder", "reorder", "إعادة ترتيب"),
    ("delete", "delete", "حذف"),
    ("delete_any", "delete all", "حذف الكل"),
    ("force_delete", "force delete", "إجبار الحذف"),
    ("force_delete_any", "force delete all", "إجبار حذف أي"),
)
# Plan 12b's session supervision: defined now, in use when 12b ships.
SUPERVISE = ("supervise", "supervise", "إشراف")
ALL_VERBS = tuple(code for code, _, _ in VERBS)
# The role resource has TutorHamster's 6 verbs only (RBAC-003).
ROLE_VERBS = ("view", "view_any", "create", "update", "delete", "delete_any")
VERB_LABELS = {code: (en, ar) for code, en, ar in (*VERBS, SUPERVISE)}

READ = ("view", "view_any")
EDIT = ("view", "view_any", "create", "update")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Resource:
    code: str
    label_en: str
    label_ar: str
    in_use: tuple[str, ...]
    verbs: tuple[str, ...] = ALL_VERBS


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Entry:
    """A page or widget permission: one code, no verbs."""

    code: str
    label_en: str
    label_ar: str
    in_use: bool = False


RESOURCES = (
    Resource("student", "Students", "الطلاب", EDIT),
    Resource("teacher", "Teachers", "المعلمون", EDIT),
    Resource("parent", "Parents", "أولياء الأمور", EDIT),
    Resource("family", "Families", "العائلات", EDIT),
    Resource("tag", "Tags", "الوسوم", ("view_any", "create", "update")),
    Resource("course", "Courses", "الدورات", (*EDIT, "delete", "replicate")),
    Resource("package", "Packages", "الباقات", (*EDIT, "delete", "replicate")),
    Resource("subscription", "Subscriptions", "الاشتراكات", (*EDIT, "delete")),
    Resource("session", "Sessions", "الحصص", EDIT, verbs=(*ALL_VERBS, SUPERVISE[0])),
    Resource("attendance", "Attendance", "الحضور", ("update",)),
    Resource("session_report", "Session reports", "تقارير الحصص", (*READ, "update")),
    Resource("invoice", "Invoices", "الفواتير", EDIT),
    Resource("payment", "Payments", "المدفوعات", ("view_any", "create", "delete")),
    Resource(
        "payroll_rate",
        "Payroll rates",
        "أسعار الرواتب",
        ("view_any", "create", "update", "delete"),
    ),
    Resource(
        "payroll_adjustment",
        "Payroll adjustments",
        "تسويات الرواتب",
        ("view_any", "create", "update", "delete"),
    ),
    Resource("payslip", "Payslips", "كشوف الرواتب", EDIT),
    Resource(
        "academy_settings", "Academy settings", "إعدادات الأكاديمية", ("view", "update")
    ),
    Resource(
        "notification_settings",
        "Notification settings",
        "إعدادات الإشعارات",
        ("view", "update"),
    ),
    Resource(
        "site", "Site pages and branding", "صفحات الموقع والهوية", (*EDIT, "delete")
    ),
    Resource("inquiry", "Inquiries", "الاستفسارات", ("view_any", "update")),
    Resource("staff", "Staff", "الموظفون", EDIT),
    Resource("role", "Roles", "الأدوار", EDIT, verbs=ROLE_VERBS),
)
# TutorHamster's vendor-only pages (R-7): listed for parity, never in use.
PAGES = (
    Entry("page.quick_translate", "Quick translate", "الترجمة السريعة"),
    Entry("page.themes", "Themes", "السمات"),
    Entry("page.two_factor", "Two-factor login", "تسجيل الدخول بخطوتين"),
    Entry("page.system_status", "System status", "حالة النظام"),
)
# TutorHamster's five stats widgets; each gates the matching card where the
# dashboard has one (revenue: the billing summary, Plan 12a D1).
WIDGETS = (
    Entry("widget.students_stats", "Students stats", "إحصائيات الطلاب"),
    Entry("widget.subscriptions_stats", "Subscriptions stats", "إحصائيات الاشتراكات"),
    Entry("widget.revenue_stats", "Revenue stats", "إحصائيات الإيرادات", in_use=True),
    Entry("widget.sessions_stats", "Sessions stats", "إحصائيات الحصص"),
    Entry("widget.students_overview", "Students overview", "نظرة عامة على الطلاب"),
)

RESOURCE_CODES = tuple(
    f"{resource.code}.{verb}" for resource in RESOURCES for verb in resource.verbs
)
CODES = frozenset((*RESOURCE_CODES, *(entry.code for entry in (*PAGES, *WIDGETS))))
IN_USE = frozenset(
    (
        *(f"{r.code}.{verb}" for r in RESOURCES for verb in r.in_use),
        *(entry.code for entry in (*PAGES, *WIDGETS) if entry.in_use),
    )
)


def _entry(entry: Entry) -> dict:
    return {
        "code": entry.code,
        "label_en": entry.label_en,
        "label_ar": entry.label_ar,
        "in_use": entry.in_use,
    }


def payload() -> dict:
    """The registry as ``GET access/registry/`` answers it (spec §5.1)."""
    return {
        "verbs": [
            {"code": code, "label_en": en, "label_ar": ar}
            for code, (en, ar) in VERB_LABELS.items()
        ],
        "resources": [
            {
                "code": r.code,
                "label_en": r.label_en,
                "label_ar": r.label_ar,
                "verbs": list(r.verbs),
                "in_use": [verb for verb in r.verbs if verb in r.in_use],
            }
            for r in RESOURCES
        ],
        "pages": [_entry(entry) for entry in PAGES],
        "widgets": [_entry(entry) for entry in WIDGETS],
    }
```

Create `backend/etqan/access/services.py`:

```python
"""Public API for the access module (Plan 12a). All inter-module calls
enter here; the rules live here and nowhere else."""

from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError as DjangoValidationError
from django.db import IntegrityError
from django.db import transaction
from django.db.models import Count
from django.db.models import Q
from django.db.models import QuerySet

from etqan.access import presets
from etqan.access import registry
from etqan.access.models import StaffRole
from etqan.platform.exceptions import ForbiddenError
from etqan.platform.exceptions import NotFoundError
from etqan.platform.exceptions import ValidationError
from etqan.platform.permissions import codes_of
from etqan.platform.permissions import role_of
from etqan.platform.validators import from_django

ROLE_FIELDS = frozenset({"name_ar", "name_en", "permissions", "is_active"})
NAMES = ("name_en", "name_ar")


def codes_for(user) -> frozenset[str]:
    """The platform's code loader (``HasCode``, ``me/``): an admin holds every
    code (R-4) without a query; a staff account the union of its active
    roles' codes, in one query; everyone else nothing (§4.3)."""
    role = role_of(user)
    if role == "admin":
        return registry.CODES
    if role != "staff":
        return frozenset()
    held = (
        StaffRole.objects.filter(members=user, is_active=True)
        .order_by()
        .values_list("permissions", flat=True)
    )
    return frozenset(code for codes in held for code in codes) & registry.CODES


def ensure_supervisor_preset() -> bool:
    """Seed the Supervisor preset into the current academy; idempotent."""
    return presets.seed(StaffRole)


# ── Roles ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────


def roles_queryset() -> QuerySet[StaffRole]:
    """Roles by English name, each annotated with ``member_count``: how many
    active staff accounts hold it."""
    count = Count("members", filter=Q(members__is_active=True), distinct=True)
    return StaffRole.objects.annotate(member_count=count).order_by("name_en", "id")


def get_role(role_id: int) -> StaffRole:
    try:
        return roles_queryset().get(pk=role_id)
    except StaffRole.DoesNotExist:
        raise NotFoundError("Role", role_id) from None


def _clean_codes(codes, *, current: frozenset[str], by) -> list[str]:
    """Registry codes only, deduplicated and sorted. A staff ``by`` may only
    add codes it holds itself (§4.2); keeping or removing is always allowed."""
    if not isinstance(codes, list | tuple) or not all(
        isinstance(code, str) for code in codes
    ):
        raise ValidationError("Choose permissions from the list.", field="permissions")
    wanted = frozenset(codes)
    unknown = sorted(wanted - registry.CODES)
    if unknown:
        raise ValidationError(f"Unknown permission: {unknown[0]}.", field="permissions")
    if role_of(by) == "staff" and not (wanted - current) <= codes_of(by):
        raise ForbiddenError(
            "You can only add permissions you hold yourself.", field="permissions"
        )
    return sorted(wanted)


def _check_role(role: StaffRole) -> None:
    """Trimmed names, unique in either language whatever the case (400 on
    that name), and the model's own limits."""
    for field in NAMES:
        value = (getattr(role, field) or "").strip()
        setattr(role, field, value)
        if not value:
            raise ValidationError("Enter a name.", field=field)
        clash = StaffRole.objects.filter(**{f"{field}__iexact": value})
        if clash.exclude(pk=role.pk).exists():
            raise ValidationError("A role with this name already exists.", field=field)
    try:
        role.full_clean(validate_unique=False, validate_constraints=False)
    except DjangoValidationError as exc:
        raise from_django(exc) from None


def _save_role(role: StaffRole) -> None:
    """Save, mapping a unique-constraint race to the pre-check's 400; the
    nested atomic keeps the failed insert from poisoning the request."""
    try:
        with transaction.atomic():
            role.save()
    except IntegrityError:
        for field in NAMES:
            clash = StaffRole.objects.filter(
                **{f"{field}__iexact": getattr(role, field)}
            )
            if clash.exclude(pk=role.pk).exists():
                raise ValidationError(
                    "A role with this name already exists.", field=field
                ) from None
        raise


@transaction.atomic
def create_role(*, name_ar: str, name_en: str, permissions, by) -> StaffRole:
    """A new, active role. ``by`` is the user asking (None for seeds)."""
    role = StaffRole(
        name_ar=name_ar,
        name_en=name_en,
        permissions=_clean_codes(permissions, current=frozenset(), by=by),
    )
    _check_role(role)
    _save_role(role)
    return role


@transaction.atomic
def update_role(role: StaffRole, *, fields: dict, by) -> StaffRole:
    """Rename a role, change its codes, retire or restore it. Nothing is ever
    deleted: a retired role keeps its members and grants nothing."""
    unknown = sorted(set(fields) - ROLE_FIELDS)
    if unknown:
        raise ValidationError("Unknown field.", field=unknown[0])
    if "permissions" in fields:
        role.permissions = _clean_codes(
            fields["permissions"], current=frozenset(role.permissions), by=by
        )
    for key in ("name_ar", "name_en", "is_active"):
        if key in fields:
            setattr(role, key, fields[key])
    _check_role(role)
    _save_role(role)
    return role
```

Create `backend/etqan/access/migrations/0002_supervisor_preset.py`:

```python
"""Plan 12a: every academy starts with the Supervisor preset (§3.4).

access is a tenant app only, but the migration still checks: the public
schema never gets a role. Idempotent by name."""

from django.db import migrations
from django_tenants.utils import get_public_schema_name

from etqan.access import presets


def forwards(apps, schema_editor):
    if schema_editor.connection.schema_name == get_public_schema_name():
        return
    presets.seed(apps.get_model("access", "StaffRole"))


class Migration(migrations.Migration):
    dependencies = [("access", "0001_initial")]
    operations = [migrations.RunPython(forwards, migrations.RunPython.noop)]
```

### Task 3: The access API: the registry, roles and staff, with the escalation guards

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/etqan/access/api/{__init__,urls,views,serializers,payloads,filters}.py`, `backend/etqan/platform/params.py`
- Modify: `backend/etqan/access/services.py`, `backend/etqan/identity/api/people_filters.py`, `backend/config/api_router.py`, `backend/pyproject.toml`, `backend/conftest.py`
- Test: `backend/etqan/access/tests/test_staff.py`, `backend/etqan/access/tests/test_access_api.py` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 2's role services, `codes_for` and `StaffRole`;
  - Task 1's `HasCode`, `codes_of` and `ForbiddenError`;
  - `identity.services`: `create_person`, `update_person`, `activate`, `deactivate`, `people_queryset("staff")` and `account_state`.
- Produces:
  - `etqan.platform.params.as_int(value) -> int | None`; `people_filters` imports it, and still exports it to `people_views`.
  - `etqan.access.services`:
    - `STAFF_FIELDS = {full_name, email, is_active, role_ids}`;
    - `staff_queryset()`, with roles prefetched by English name;
    - `get_staff(user_id)` (NotFoundError → 404, never an admin);
    - `create_staff(*, full_name, email, role_ids, by)`;
    - `update_staff(user, *, fields, by)`;
    - the private `_roles_to_assign(role_ids, *, kept, by)`.
  - Routes:
    - `GET access/registry/`;
    - `GET/POST access/roles/` (paged; `?active=`) and `GET/PATCH access/roles/<id>/`;
    - `GET/POST access/staff/` (paged; `?search=`, `?role=`, `?active=`) and `GET/PATCH access/staff/<id>/`.
  - `payloads.role_row(role)`, `payloads.staff_row(user)`, `filters.filter_roles` and `filters.filter_staff`.
  - The root fixture `staff_for(*codes, **fields)`: an `APIClient` signed in as a new staff account holding exactly these codes through one active role (no codes: no role).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

- **Services:**
  - a staff account holds its roles and gets Plan 1's invite;
  - unknown or junk role ids, and a retired role, are refused before anything is written;
  - the email is required and unique;
  - name, email and roles change together;
  - a retired role already held is kept;
  - deactivating ends the sessions and reactivating restores the login;
  - an unknown field is refused, and admins and other people are never staff;
  - each §4.2 guard, with its field (D5).
- **API:**
  - fresh reads, and row keys exactly as §5.1 lists them;
  - errors name their field;
  - paging, filters and junk;
  - no PUT or DELETE;
  - the staff dialogs may read the roles list;
  - every route refuses teachers, students, parents and visitors;
  - another academy's roles and staff are never reachable or assignable (`until_pk_exceeds`);
  - the roles and staff lists stay flat as roles and members grow.

```python
    """`staff_for("invoice.view_any", …)` → an APIClient logged in as a new
    staff account (Plan 12a) holding exactly these codes through one active
```

Create `backend/etqan/access/tests/test_access_api.py`:

```python
"""Plan 12a §5.1: /api/v1/access/ — the registry, roles and staff; fresh
reads, no PUT or DELETE, fields named in errors, never another academy's."""

import pytest
from django.db import connection
from django.db.models import Max
from django.test.utils import CaptureQueriesContext
from django_tenants.utils import tenant_context
from rest_framework.test import APIClient

from etqan.access import registry
from etqan.access import services
from etqan.access.models import StaffRole
from etqan.identity import services as identity_services
from etqan.identity.models import User
from etqan.scheduling.tests.conftest import until_pk_exceeds

pytestmark = pytest.mark.django_db
A = "/api/v1/access/"
ROLE_KEYS = {
    "id",
    "name_ar",
    "name_en",
    "permissions",
    "is_active",
    "members",
    "created_at",
}
STAFF_KEYS = {"id", "full_name", "email", "is_active", "roles", "invited", "created_at"}


@pytest.fixture
def admin(api_for):
    return api_for("admin")


def role(name, *codes, active=True):
    return StaffRole.objects.create(
        name_en=name, name_ar=f"{name} ع", permissions=list(codes), is_active=active
    )


def staff(name, *roles, active=True):
    user = identity_services.create_person(
        "staff", full_name=name, email=f"{name.lower().replace(' ', '')}@x.test"
    )
    user.staff_roles.set(roles)
    if not active:
        identity_services.deactivate(user, by=None)
    return user


def reads(queries) -> int:
    return len([q for q in queries if not q["sql"].startswith("SET search_path")])


class TestRegistry:
    def test_it_lists_what_a_role_can_hold(self, admin):
        body = admin.get(f"{A}registry/").json()
        assert body == registry.payload()
        assert admin.post(f"{A}registry/", {}).status_code == 405


class TestRoles:
    def test_create_answers_with_a_fresh_read(self, admin):
        body = {
            "name_ar": "محاسب",
            "name_en": "Accountant",
            "permissions": ["invoice.view_any", "invoice.create"],
        }
        resp = admin.post(f"{A}roles/", body, format="json")
        assert resp.status_code == 201, resp.json()
        row = resp.json()
        assert set(row) == ROLE_KEYS
        assert (row["name_en"], row["is_active"], row["members"]) == (
            "Accountant",
            True,
            0,
        )
        assert row["permissions"] == ["invoice.create", "invoice.view_any"]
        assert admin.get(f"{A}roles/{row['id']}/").json() == row

    def test_the_list_is_paged_counted_and_filtered(self, admin):
        clerk = role("Clerk", "invoice.view")
        role("Old", active=False)
        staff("Sara", clerk)
        staff("Omar", clerk, active=False)
        rows = admin.get(f"{A}roles/").json()["results"]
        assert [(r["name_en"], r["members"]) for r in rows] == [
            ("Clerk", 1),
            ("Old", 0),
            ("Supervisor", 0),
        ]
        names = [
            r["name_en"] for r in admin.get(f"{A}roles/?active=false").json()["results"]
        ]
        assert names == ["Old"]
        for junk in ("x", "", "1"):
            assert admin.get(f"{A}roles/?active={junk}").json()["count"] == 3

    def test_errors_name_their_field(self, admin):
        cases = [
            ({"name_ar": "أ", "name_en": "A", "permissions": ["x.fly"]}, "permissions"),
            ({"name_ar": "أ", "name_en": "supervisor"}, "name_en"),
            ({"name_ar": "مشرف", "name_en": "B"}, "name_ar"),
            ({"name_ar": "أ", "name_en": "x" * 61}, "name_en"),
            ({"name_en": "C"}, "name_ar"),
        ]
        for body, field in cases:
            resp = admin.post(f"{A}roles/", body, format="json")
            assert resp.status_code == 400, body
            assert list(resp.json()) == [field], body
        assert StaffRole.objects.count() == 1

    def test_retire_restore_rename_and_recode(self, admin):
        clerk = role("Clerk", "invoice.view")
        url = f"{A}roles/{clerk.pk}/"
        resp = admin.patch(url, {"is_active": False}, format="json")
        assert resp.json()["is_active"] is False
        body = {
            "is_active": True,
            "name_en": "Cashier",
            "permissions": ["payment.create"],
        }
        row = admin.patch(url, body, format="json").json()
        assert (row["is_active"], row["name_en"], row["permissions"]) == (
            True,
            "Cashier",
            ["payment.create"],
        )

    def test_there_is_no_put_and_no_delete(self, admin):
        clerk = role("Clerk")
        url = f"{A}roles/{clerk.pk}/"
        assert admin.put(url, {"name_en": "X"}, format="json").status_code == 405
        assert admin.delete(url).status_code == 405
        assert StaffRole.objects.filter(pk=clerk.pk).exists()
        assert admin.get(f"{A}roles/999999/").status_code == 404
        assert admin.patch(f"{A}roles/999999/", {}, format="json").status_code == 404

    def test_a_staff_editor_adds_only_codes_it_holds(self, staff_for):
        editor = staff_for("role.update", "role.create", "invoice.view")
        clerk = role("Clerk", "payslip.view")
        resp = editor.patch(
            f"{A}roles/{clerk.pk}/",
            {"permissions": ["payslip.view", "payslip.update"]},
            format="json",
        )
        assert resp.status_code == 403
        assert list(resp.json()) == ["permissions"]
        resp = editor.patch(
            f"{A}roles/{clerk.pk}/",
            {"permissions": ["payslip.view", "invoice.view"]},
            format="json",
        )
        assert resp.status_code == 200
        body = {"name_ar": "ب", "name_en": "B", "permissions": ["payslip.view"]}
        resp = editor.post(f"{A}roles/", body, format="json")
        assert resp.status_code == 403
        assert not StaffRole.objects.filter(name_en="B").exists()

    def test_the_list_is_flat_as_roles_and_members_grow(self, admin):
        def count():
            with CaptureQueriesContext(connection) as queries:
                assert admin.get(f"{A}roles/").status_code == 200
            return reads(queries)

        one = count()
        for n in range(4):
            made = role(f"R{n}", "invoice.view")
            staff(f"S{n}", made)
            staff(f"T{n}", made)
        assert count() == one

        )

        ]

    def test_errors_name_their_field(self, admin):

            ({"full_name": "S", "email": "not-an-email"}, "email"),
            ({"email": "s@x.test"}, "full_name"),
            ({"full_name": "S", "email": "s@x.test", "role_ids": [999999]}, "role_ids"),
            ({"full_name": "S", "email": "s@x.test", "role_ids": [old.pk]}, "role_ids"),
        ]
        for body, field in cases:
            resp = admin.post(f"{A}staff/", body, format="json")
            assert resp.status_code == 400, body
            assert list(resp.json()) == [field], body
        assert User.objects.filter(role="staff").count() == 1

    def test_the_list_searches_and_filters_and_ignores_junk(self, admin):
        clerk, reader = role("Clerk"), role("Reader")
        staff("Sara Ali", clerk)
        staff("Omar", reader)
        staff("Nour", clerk, reader, active=False)

        def names(query=""):
            rows = admin.get(f"{A}staff/?{query}").json()["results"]
            return [row["full_name"] for row in rows]

        assert names() == ["Nour", "Omar", "Sara Ali"]
        assert names("search=ali") == ["Sara Ali"]
        assert names("search=omar@x") == ["Omar"]
        assert names(f"role={clerk.pk}") == ["Nour", "Sara Ali"]
        assert names("active=false") == ["Nour"]
        for junk in (
            "role=x",
            "role=-1",
            "role=%C2%B2",
            f"role={'1' * 5000}",
            "active=x",
        ):
            assert names(junk) == ["Nour", "Omar", "Sara Ali"], junk
        assert names("role=999999") == []
```
