```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize("kind", ["teacher", "parent", "student"])
def test_every_access_route_refuses_other_account_types(api_for, kind):
    client = api_for(kind)
    clerk = role("Clerk")
    sara = staff("Sara", clerk)
    for method, url in (
        ("get", f"{A}registry/"),
        ("get", f"{A}roles/"),
        ("post", f"{A}roles/"),
        ("get", f"{A}roles/{clerk.pk}/"),
        ("patch", f"{A}roles/{clerk.pk}/"),
        ("get", f"{A}staff/"),
        ("post", f"{A}staff/"),
        ("get", f"{A}staff/{sara.pk}/"),
        ("patch", f"{A}staff/{sara.pk}/"),
    ):
        assert getattr(client, method)(url, {}, format="json").status_code == 403
    assert APIClient().get(f"{A}roles/").status_code == 403


def test_the_staff_dialogs_may_read_the_roles_to_pick_from(staff_for):
    assert staff_for("staff.create").get(f"{A}roles/").status_code == 200
    assert staff_for("staff.update").get(f"{A}roles/").status_code == 200
    assert staff_for("staff.view_any").get(f"{A}roles/").status_code == 403


def test_another_academys_roles_and_staff_are_never_reachable(admin, tenants):
    clerk = role("Clerk")
    sara = staff("Sara", clerk)
    ceiling = max(
        StaffRole.objects.aggregate(top=Max("pk"))["top"],
        User.objects.aggregate(top=Max("pk"))["top"],
    )
    with tenant_context(tenants.other):
        theirs = until_pk_exceeds(StaffRole, ceiling, lambda: role("Theirs"))
        layla = until_pk_exceeds(User, ceiling, lambda: staff("Layla", theirs))
    assert min(theirs.pk, layla.pk) > ceiling
    assert admin.get(f"{A}roles/{theirs.pk}/").status_code == 404
    assert admin.patch(f"{A}roles/{theirs.pk}/", {}, format="json").status_code == 404
    assert admin.get(f"{A}staff/{layla.pk}/").status_code == 404
    names = [r["name_en"] for r in admin.get(f"{A}roles/").json()["results"]]
    assert names == ["Clerk", "Supervisor"]
    assert [r["full_name"] for r in admin.get(f"{A}staff/").json()["results"]] == [
        "Sara"
    ]
    resp = admin.patch(f"{A}staff/{sara.pk}/", {"role_ids": [theirs.pk]}, format="json")
    assert list(resp.json()) == ["role_ids"]
    assert admin.get(f"{A}staff/?role={theirs.pk}").json()["count"] == 0
    with tenant_context(tenants.other):
        assert [r.name_en for r in services.get_staff(layla.pk).staff_roles.all()] == [
            "Theirs"
        ]
```

```python
"""Plan 12a §4.2: staff accounts, their roles, and the escalation guards."""

import pytest
from django.contrib.sessions.backends.db import SessionStore
from django.contrib.sessions.models import Session
from django.core import mail

from etqan.access import services
from etqan.access.models import StaffRole
from etqan.identity import services as identity_services
from etqan.identity.models import User
from etqan.platform.exceptions import ForbiddenError
from etqan.platform.exceptions import NotFoundError
from etqan.platform.exceptions import ValidationError

pytestmark = pytest.mark.django_db


def role(name, *codes, active=True):
    return StaffRole.objects.create(
        name_en=name, name_ar=f"{name} ع", permissions=list(codes), is_active=active
    )


def staff(name="Sara", *roles):
    user = identity_services.create_person(
        "staff", full_name=name, email=f"{name.lower()}@x.test"
    )
    user.staff_roles.set(roles)
    return user


def roles_of(user):
    return sorted(r.name_en for r in services.get_staff(user.pk).staff_roles.all())


def refused(error, field, call, *args, **kwargs):
    with pytest.raises(error) as exc:
        call(*args, **kwargs)
    assert exc.value.field == field, exc.value.message
    return exc.value.message


class TestCreate:
    def test_a_staff_account_holds_its_roles_and_is_invited(
        self, django_capture_on_commit_callbacks
    ):
        clerk, reader = role("Clerk", "invoice.view"), role("Reader")
        with django_capture_on_commit_callbacks(execute=True):
            sara = services.create_staff(
                full_name="Sara",
                email="sara@x.test",
                role_ids=[reader.pk, clerk.pk, reader.pk],
                by=None,
            )
        assert (sara.role, identity_services.account_state(sara)) == (
            "staff",
            "invited",
        )
        assert roles_of(sara) == ["Clerk", "Reader"]
        assert mail.outbox[-1].to == ["sara@x.test"]
        assert "/app/reset-password?uid=" in mail.outbox[-1].body

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("ids", [[999999], ["x"], None])
    def test_unknown_roles_are_refused_and_nothing_is_written(self, ids):
        refused(
            ValidationError,
            "role_ids",
            services.create_staff,
            full_name="Sara",
            email="sara@x.test",
            role_ids=ids,
            by=None,
        )
        assert not User.objects.filter(role="staff").exists()

    def test_a_retired_role_cannot_be_given(self):
        old = role("Old", active=False)
        message = refused(
            ValidationError,
            "role_ids",
            services.create_staff,
            full_name="Sara",
            email="sara@x.test",
            role_ids=[old.pk],
            by=None,
        )
        assert message == "A retired role can't be added."

    def test_email_is_required_and_unique(self):
        staff("Omar")
        for email in ("", "OMAR@x.test"):
            refused(
                ValidationError,
                "email",
                services.create_staff,
                full_name="Sara",
                email=email,
                role_ids=[],
                by=None,
            )

        sara = staff("Sara", clerk)

        sara = staff("Sara", old)
        old.is_active = False

    def test_deactivating_blocks_login_and_ends_sessions(self, client):
        sara = staff("Sara")
        store = SessionStore()
        store["_auth_user_id"] = str(sara.pk)
        store.create()
        services.update_staff(sara, fields={"is_active": False}, by=None)
        assert not User.objects.get(pk=sara.pk).is_active

    def test_an_unknown_field_is_refused(self):
        refused(
            ValidationError,
            "role",
            services.update_staff,
            staff(),
            fields={"role": "admin"},
            by=None,
        )

    def test_admins_and_other_people_are_not_staff(self):
        boss = User.objects.create_user(email="b@x.test", role="admin")
        pupil = identity_services.create_person("student", full_name="Zaid")
        for user in (boss, pupil):
            with pytest.raises(NotFoundError):
                services.get_staff(user.pk)
        assert [u.pk for u in services.staff_queryset()] == []


class TestEscalation:
    """§4.2: each guard names its field, and nothing is written."""

    def test_staff_grant_only_roles_whose_codes_they_hold(self):
        mine = role("Mine", "staff.update", "invoice.view")
        small = role("Small", "invoice.view")
        big = role("Big", "invoice.view", "payslip.view")
        sara, omar = staff("Sara", mine), staff("Omar")
        services.update_staff(omar, fields={"role_ids": [small.pk]}, by=sara)
        assert roles_of(omar) == ["Small"]
        message = refused(
            ForbiddenError,
            "role_ids",
            services.update_staff,
            omar,
            fields={"role_ids": [small.pk, big.pk]},
            by=sara,
        )
        assert (
            message == "You can only grant roles whose permissions you hold yourself."
        )
        refused(
            ForbiddenError,
            "role_ids",
            services.create_staff,
            full_name="Nour",
            email="nour@x.test",
            role_ids=[big.pk],
            by=sara,
        )
        assert roles_of(omar) == ["Small"]
        assert not User.objects.filter(email="nour@x.test").exists()

    def test_staff_keep_a_role_they_could_not_grant(self):
        mine, big = role("Mine", "staff.update"), role("Big", "payslip.view")
        sara, omar = staff("Sara", mine), staff("Omar")
        omar.staff_roles.set([big])
        services.update_staff(
            omar, fields={"role_ids": [big.pk], "full_name": "Omar B."}, by=sara
        )
        assert roles_of(omar) == ["Big"]
        services.update_staff(omar, fields={"role_ids": []}, by=sara)
        assert roles_of(omar) == []

    def test_staff_never_change_their_own_roles(self):
        mine, other = role("Mine", "staff.update"), role("Other")
        sara = staff("Sara", mine)
        message = refused(
            ForbiddenError,
            "role_ids",
            services.update_staff,
            sara,
            fields={"role_ids": [mine.pk, other.pk]},
            by=sara,
        )
        assert message == "You can't change your own roles."
        # Sending their roles unchanged, with a new name, is fine.
        services.update_staff(
            sara, fields={"role_ids": [mine.pk], "full_name": "Sara A."}, by=sara
        )
        assert services.get_staff(sara.pk).full_name == "Sara A."

    def test_staff_never_deactivate_themselves(self):
        sara = staff("Sara", role("Mine", "staff.update"))
        message = refused(
            ForbiddenError,
            "is_active",
            services.update_staff,
            sara,
            fields={"is_active": False, "full_name": "Gone"},
            by=sara,
        )
        assert message == "You can't deactivate yourself."
        assert services.get_staff(sara.pk).full_name == "Sara"
        assert User.objects.get(pk=sara.pk).is_active

    def test_admins_grant_any_role(self):
        boss = User.objects.create_user(email="b@x.test", role="admin")
        big = role("Big", "payslip.view", "role.update")
        omar = staff("Omar")
        services.update_staff(omar, fields={"role_ids": [big.pk]}, by=boss)
        assert roles_of(omar) == ["Big"]
```

Create `backend/etqan/access/api/filters.py`:

```python
from django.db.models import Q

from etqan.platform.params import as_int

_BOOL = {"true": True, "false": False}


def filter_roles(queryset, params):
    """``?active=true|false``; anything else is ignored."""
    if (active := params.get("active")) in _BOOL:
        queryset = queryset.filter(is_active=_BOOL[active])
    return queryset


def filter_staff(queryset, params):
    """``?search=`` on the name or email, ``?role=<id>``, ``?active=``; junk
    is ignored, never a 500."""
    if search := params.get("search", "").strip():
        queryset = queryset.filter(
            Q(full_name__icontains=search) | Q(email__icontains=search)
        )
    if (role := as_int(params.get("role", ""))) is not None:
        queryset = queryset.filter(staff_roles=role)
    if (active := params.get("active")) in _BOOL:
        queryset = queryset.filter(is_active=_BOOL[active])
    return queryset
```

Create `backend/etqan/access/api/payloads.py`:

```python
"""JSON shapes for the access routes (Plan 12a §5.1)."""

from etqan.identity import services as identity_services


def role_row(role) -> dict:
    """``role`` comes from ``services.roles_queryset`` (``member_count``)."""
    return {
        "id": role.id,
        "name_ar": role.name_ar,
        "name_en": role.name_en,
        "permissions": sorted(role.permissions),
        "is_active": role.is_active,
        "members": role.member_count,
        "created_at": role.created_at,
    }


def staff_row(user) -> dict:
    """``user`` comes from ``services.staff_queryset`` (roles prefetched)."""
    return {
        "id": user.id,
        "full_name": user.full_name,
        "email": user.email,
        "is_active": user.is_active,
        "roles": [
            {"id": role.id, "name_ar": role.name_ar, "name_en": role.name_en}
            for role in user.staff_roles.all()
        ],
        "invited": identity_services.account_state(user) == "invited",
        "created_at": user.date_joined,
    }
```

Create `backend/etqan/access/api/serializers.py`:

```python
from rest_framework import serializers


def _codes():
    return serializers.ListField(
        child=serializers.CharField(max_length=64), max_length=1000, required=False
    )


def _role_ids():
    return serializers.ListField(
        child=serializers.IntegerField(min_value=1), max_length=100, required=False
    )


class RoleCreateSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    name_ar = serializers.CharField(max_length=60)
    name_en = serializers.CharField(max_length=60)
    permissions = _codes()


class RoleUpdateSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    """PATCH only (§5.1): no PUT, and no DELETE: a role is retired instead."""

    name_ar = serializers.CharField(max_length=60, required=False)
    name_en = serializers.CharField(max_length=60, required=False)
    permissions = _codes()
    is_active = serializers.BooleanField(required=False)


class StaffCreateSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    full_name = serializers.CharField(max_length=255)
    email = serializers.EmailField()
    role_ids = _role_ids()


class StaffUpdateSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    full_name = serializers.CharField(max_length=255, required=False)
    email = serializers.EmailField(required=False)
    is_active = serializers.BooleanField(required=False)
    role_ids = _role_ids()
```

```python
"""Staff and roles admin (Plan 12a §5.1). Thin: parse, call one service,
re-read and arrange a payload. No PUT and no DELETE."""

from rest_framework import generics
from rest_framework import status
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from etqan.access import registry
from etqan.access import services
from etqan.access.api.filters import filter_roles
from etqan.access.api.filters import filter_staff
from etqan.access.api.payloads import role_row
from etqan.access.api.payloads import staff_row
from etqan.access.api.serializers import RoleCreateSerializer
from etqan.access.api.serializers import RoleUpdateSerializer
from etqan.access.api.serializers import StaffCreateSerializer
from etqan.access.api.serializers import StaffUpdateSerializer
from etqan.platform.permissions import HasCode


class RegistryView(APIView):
    """What a role can hold: the Roles editor's matrix."""

    permission_classes = [HasCode]
    permission_codes = {"GET": "role.view_any"}

    def get(self, request):
        return Response(registry.payload())


class RoleListCreateView(generics.GenericAPIView):
    permission_classes = [HasCode]
    # The staff dialogs' roles picker reads the list too (D2).
    permission_codes = {
        "GET": ("role.view_any", "staff.create", "staff.update"),
        "POST": "role.create",
    }

    def get(self, request):
        roles = filter_roles(services.roles_queryset(), request.query_params)
        page = self.paginate_queryset(roles)
        return self.get_paginated_response([role_row(role) for role in page])

    def post(self, request):
        body = RoleCreateSerializer(data=request.data)
        body.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        data = body.validated_data
        role = services.create_role(
            name_ar=data["name_ar"],
            name_en=data["name_en"],
            permissions=data.get("permissions", []),
            by=request.user,
        )
        return Response(
            role_row(services.get_role(role.pk)), status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED
        )


class RoleDetailView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [HasCode]
    permission_codes = {"GET": "role.view", "PATCH": "role.update"}

    def get(self, request, pk):
        return Response(role_row(services.get_role(pk)))

    def patch(self, request, pk):
        role = services.get_role(pk)
        body = RoleUpdateSerializer(data=request.data, partial=True)
        body.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        services.update_role(role, fields=dict(body.validated_data), by=request.user)
        return Response(role_row(services.get_role(pk)))


class StaffListCreateView(generics.GenericAPIView):
    permission_classes = [HasCode]
    permission_codes = {"GET": "staff.view_any", "POST": "staff.create"}

    def get(self, request):
        people = filter_staff(services.staff_queryset(), request.query_params)
        page = self.paginate_queryset(people)
        return self.get_paginated_response([staff_row(user) for user in page])

    def post(self, request):
        body = StaffCreateSerializer(data=request.data)
        body.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        data = body.validated_data
        user = services.create_staff(
            full_name=data["full_name"],
            email=data["email"],
            role_ids=data.get("role_ids", []),
            by=request.user,
        )
        return Response(
            staff_row(services.get_staff(user.pk)), status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED
        )


class StaffDetailView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [HasCode]
    permission_codes = {"GET": "staff.view", "PATCH": "staff.update"}

    def get(self, request, pk):
        return Response(staff_row(services.get_staff(pk)))

    def patch(self, request, pk):
        user = services.get_staff(pk)
        body = StaffUpdateSerializer(data=request.data, partial=True)
        body.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        services.update_staff(user, fields=dict(body.validated_data), by=request.user)
        return Response(staff_row(services.get_staff(pk)))
```

```python
# ── Staff (§4.2) ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

STAFF_FIELDS = frozenset({"full_name", "email", "is_active", "role_ids"})
USER_FIELDS = ("full_name", "email")


def staff_queryset() -> QuerySet:
    """Staff accounts by name, each with its roles (one query for all rows).
    Admins are never here: only admins see admins (§4.2)."""
    roles = Prefetch("staff_roles", queryset=StaffRole.objects.order_by("name_en"))
    return identity_services.people_queryset("staff").prefetch_related(roles)


def get_staff(user_id: int):
    user = staff_queryset().filter(pk=user_id).first()
    if user is None:
        raise NotFoundError("Staff", user_id)
    return user


def _is_staff(user) -> bool:
    return role_of(user) == "staff"


def _roles_to_assign(role_ids, *, kept: set[int], by) -> list[StaffRole]:
    """The roles ``role_ids`` names, all of this academy. A retired role is
    refused unless the account already has it (``kept``). A staff ``by``
    grants a role only if it holds every code in it itself (§4.2)."""
    try:
        wanted = list(dict.fromkeys(int(pk) for pk in role_ids))
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        raise ValidationError("Choose roles from the list.", field="role_ids") from None
    found = {role.pk: role for role in StaffRole.objects.filter(pk__in=wanted)}
    if len(found) != len(wanted):
        raise ValidationError("Choose roles from the list.", field="role_ids")
    added = [found[pk] for pk in wanted if pk not in kept]
    if any(not role.is_active for role in added):
        raise ValidationError("A retired role can't be added.", field="role_ids")
    if _is_staff(by):
        held = codes_of(by)
        if any(not set(role.permissions) <= held for role in added):
            raise ForbiddenError(
                "You can only grant roles whose permissions you hold yourself.",
                field="role_ids",
            )
    return [found[pk] for pk in wanted]


@transaction.atomic
def create_staff(*, full_name: str, email: str, role_ids, by):
    """A staff account holding ``role_ids``, invited by email (Plan 1's
    invite flow, sent after the request commits)."""
    roles = _roles_to_assign(role_ids, kept=set(), by=by)
    user = identity_services.create_person("staff", full_name=full_name, email=email)
    user.staff_roles.set(roles)
    return user


@transaction.atomic
def update_staff(user, *, fields: dict, by):
    """Edit a staff account's name and email, its roles, or deactivate or
    reactivate it. A staff ``by`` never changes its own roles or deactivates
    itself (§4.2); everything is checked before anything is written."""
    unknown = sorted(set(fields) - STAFF_FIELDS)
    if unknown:
        raise ValidationError("Unknown field.", field=unknown[0])
    current = {role.pk for role in user.staff_roles.all()}
    roles = None
    if "role_ids" in fields:
        roles = _roles_to_assign(fields["role_ids"], kept=current, by=by)
    oneself = by is not None and by.pk == user.pk
    if oneself and roles is not None and {role.pk for role in roles} != current:
        raise ForbiddenError("You can't change your own roles.", field="role_ids")
    active = fields.get("is_active", user.is_active)
    if oneself and not active:
        raise ForbiddenError("You can't deactivate yourself.", field="is_active")
    changes = {key: fields[key] for key in USER_FIELDS if key in fields}
    if changes:
        identity_services.update_person(user, fields=changes)
    if roles is not None:
        user.staff_roles.set(roles)
    if active != user.is_active:
        if active:
            identity_services.activate(user)
        else:
            # Signs them out everywhere, as for a teacher (§4.2).
            identity_services.deactivate(user, by=by)
    return user
```

```python
_TAGGED = {"student": StudentProfile, "teacher": TeacherProfile}


def as_int(value: str) -> int | None:
    """An id from a query parameter, or None for junk: never a 500. ``int()``
    itself decides, inside ``try``: ``isdigit()`` passes "²" and
    ``isdecimal()`` passes a 5000-digit string, and ``int()`` raises
    ValueError on both. A negative number is junk too."""
    try:
        number = int(value)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None
    return number if number >= 0 else None
```


Create `backend/etqan/platform/params.py`:

```python
"""Query-string values, parsed so that junk never becomes a 500."""


def as_int(value: str) -> int | None:
    """An id from a query parameter, or None for junk: never a 500. ``int()``
    itself decides, inside ``try``: ``isdigit()`` passes "²" and
    ``isdecimal()`` passes a 5000-digit string, and ``int()`` raises
    ValueError on both. A negative number is junk too."""
    try:
        number = int(value)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None
    return number if number >= 0 else None
```

### Task 4: Enforcement: every route declares its code, and `me/` carries the codes

**Files:**
- Modify: `backend/etqan/identity/api/people_views.py`, `backend/etqan/identity/api/views.py`, `backend/etqan/identity/api/serializers.py`, `backend/etqan/catalogue/api/views.py`, `backend/etqan/academy/api/views.py`, `backend/etqan/notifications/api/views.py`, `backend/etqan/site/api/admin_views.py`, `backend/etqan/scheduling/api/views.py`, `backend/etqan/scheduling/api/session_views.py`, `backend/etqan/billing/api/views.py`, `backend/etqan/payroll/api/views.py`
- Delete: `backend/etqan/site/permissions.py`
- Test: `backend/etqan/access/tests/test_routes.py`, `backend/etqan/access/tests/test_me.py` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1's `HasCode`, `ReadsOwn`, `viewset_codes` and `codes_of`; Task 2's registry; Task 3's routes and `staff_for`.
- Produces:
  - every view's `permission_codes` as D2's table sets it out;
  - `me/` (and the login answer) gains `permissions` (a sorted list) and `is_super_admin`, also in `MeResponseSerializer`.

The route table (D2) as `ROUTES`, `ADMIN_ONLY`, `OPEN_READS` and `SELF_SERVICE`, and the tests that hold every route to it (D3):
- no handler is left out or declares a different code;
- the registry's in-use set equals the table's codes;
- 403 without the code (every other code held) and not-403 with it alone;
- 403 with no role or a retired one;
- admin accounts are for admins only;
- admins pass everything.

`me/` for each account type, the login answer, and the one-query and flat-count rules.

Create `backend/etqan/access/tests/test_me.py`:

```python
"""Plan 12a §4.3: me/ carries the caller's codes and whether they are the
academy's super admin; the dashboard's can() reads them."""

import pytest
from allauth.account.models import EmailAddress
from django.db import connection
from django.test.utils import CaptureQueriesContext

from etqan.access import registry
from etqan.access.models import StaffRole

pytestmark = pytest.mark.django_db
ME = "/api/v1/identity/me/"


def reads(queries) -> int:
    return len([q for q in queries if not q["sql"].startswith("SET search_path")])


def test_an_admin_holds_every_registry_code(api_for):
    body = api_for("admin").get(ME).json()
    assert body["permissions"] == sorted(registry.CODES)
    assert body["is_super_admin"] is True


def test_staff_hold_their_active_roles_codes(staff_for):
    sara = staff_for("invoice.view_any", "invoice.view")
    StaffRole.objects.create(
        name_en="Old", name_ar="قديم", permissions=["payslip.view"], is_active=False
    ).members.add(sara.user)
    body = sara.get(ME).json()
    assert body["permissions"] == ["invoice.view", "invoice.view_any"]
    assert (body["role"], body["is_super_admin"], body["profiles"]) == (
        "staff",
        False,
        [],
    )
    assert body["profile"] == {}
    assert staff_for().get(ME).json()["permissions"] == []


@pytest.mark.parametrize("role", ["teacher", "student", "parent"])
def test_everyone_else_holds_nothing(api_for, role):
    body = api_for(role).get(ME).json()
    assert (body["permissions"], body["is_super_admin"]) == ([], False)


def test_login_answers_with_the_codes_too(api_for, staff_for):
    sara = staff_for("invoice.view")
    sara.user.set_password("pw-12345678")
    sara.user.save()

    EmailAddress.objects.create(
        user=sara.user, email=sara.user.email, primary=True, verified=True
    )
    resp = (
        api_for("admin")
        .__class__()
        .post(
            "/api/v1/identity/login/",
            {"email": sara.user.email, "password": "pw-12345678"},
            format="json",
        )
    )
    assert resp.status_code == 200, resp.json()
    assert resp.json()["permissions"] == ["invoice.view"]


def test_me_is_flat_as_a_staff_accounts_roles_grow(staff_for):
    sara = staff_for("invoice.view")

    def count():
        with CaptureQueriesContext(connection) as queries:
            assert sara.get(ME).status_code == 200
        return reads(queries)

    one = count()
    for n in range(4):
        StaffRole.objects.create(
            name_en=f"R{n}", name_ar=f"د{n}", permissions=["course.view_any"]
        ).members.add(sara.user)
    assert count() == one
    assert sara.get(ME).json()["permissions"] == ["course.view_any", "invoice.view"]


def test_one_query_loads_the_codes_for_the_whole_request(staff_for):
    """§4.1: the permission check loads them once, and nothing in the
    request loads them again."""
    sara = staff_for("invoice.view_any")
    with CaptureQueriesContext(connection) as queries:
        assert sara.get("/api/v1/billing/invoices/").status_code == 200
    loads = [q for q in queries if '"access_staffrole"' in q["sql"]]
    assert len(loads) == 1
```

Create `backend/etqan/access/tests/test_routes.py`:

```python
"""Plan 12a §4.1: every /api/v1/ route declares its code (the plan's route
table, D2), and HasCode enforces it.

``ROUTES`` is the route table: each authenticated route that is not
self-service, with the code a staff account needs. ``ADMIN_ONLY`` declares
no code, so staff never pass. ``SELF_SERVICE`` lists the views exempt from a
code, each with its reason. The coverage test walks the URL patterns and
fails on any handler none of the three names."""

from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from django.urls import URLResolver
from django.urls import get_resolver
from django.urls import resolve

from etqan.access import registry
from etqan.platform.permissions import required_code

N = 999999  # an id no academy has: past the check, a 404 or a 400

ROUTES = [
    # The people routes, in etqan.identity.
    ("GET", "/api/v1/people/students/", "student.view_any"),
    ("POST", "/api/v1/people/students/", "student.create"),

    # Sessions, attendance and reports, in etqan.scheduling.
    ("POST", "/api/v1/schedule/generate/", "session.create"),

    ("POST", f"/api/v1/sessions/{N}/cancel/", "session.update"),
    ("POST", f"/api/v1/sessions/{N}/restore/", "session.update"),
    ("POST", f"/api/v1/sessions/{N}/attendance/", "attendance.update"),
    ("GET", f"/api/v1/sessions/{N}/report/", "session_report.view"),
    ("PUT", f"/api/v1/sessions/{N}/report/", "session_report.update"),
    ("GET", "/api/v1/reports/missing/", "session_report.view_any"),
    # Billing.
    ("GET", "/api/v1/billing/invoices/", "invoice.view_any"),
    ("POST", "/api/v1/billing/invoices/", "invoice.create"),
    ("GET", f"/api/v1/billing/invoices/{N}/", "invoice.view"),
    ("PATCH", f"/api/v1/billing/invoices/{N}/", "invoice.update"),
    ("POST", f"/api/v1/billing/invoices/{N}/void/", "invoice.update"),
    ("GET", f"/api/v1/billing/invoices/{N}/payments/", "payment.view_any"),
    ("POST", f"/api/v1/billing/invoices/{N}/payments/", "payment.create"),
    ("DELETE", f"/api/v1/billing/payments/{N}/", "payment.delete"),
    ("GET", "/api/v1/billing/summary/", "widget.revenue_stats"),
    ("GET", "/api/v1/billing/payers/", "invoice.create"),
    # Payroll.
    ("GET", "/api/v1/payroll/rates/", "payroll_rate.view_any"),
    ("POST", "/api/v1/payroll/rates/", "payroll_rate.create"),
    ("PATCH", f"/api/v1/payroll/rates/{N}/", "payroll_rate.update"),
    ("DELETE", f"/api/v1/payroll/rates/{N}/", "payroll_rate.delete"),
    ("GET", "/api/v1/payroll/adjustments/", "payroll_adjustment.view_any"),
    ("POST", "/api/v1/payroll/adjustments/", "payroll_adjustment.create"),
    ("PATCH", f"/api/v1/payroll/adjustments/{N}/", "payroll_adjustment.update"),
    ("DELETE", f"/api/v1/payroll/adjustments/{N}/", "payroll_adjustment.delete"),
    ("GET", "/api/v1/payroll/payslips/", "payslip.view_any"),
    ("POST", "/api/v1/payroll/payslips/generate/", "payslip.create"),
    ("GET", f"/api/v1/payroll/payslips/{N}/", "payslip.view"),
    ("POST", f"/api/v1/payroll/payslips/{N}/issue/", "payslip.update"),
    ("POST", f"/api/v1/payroll/payslips/{N}/mark-paid/", "payslip.update"),
    # Staff and roles, in etqan.access.
    ("GET", "/api/v1/access/registry/", "role.view_any"),
    ("GET", "/api/v1/access/roles/", ("role.view_any", "staff.create", "staff.update")),
    ("POST", "/api/v1/access/roles/", "role.create"),
    ("GET", f"/api/v1/access/roles/{N}/", "role.view"),
    ("PATCH", f"/api/v1/access/roles/{N}/", "role.update"),
    ("GET", "/api/v1/access/staff/", "staff.view_any"),
    ("POST", "/api/v1/access/staff/", "staff.create"),
    ("GET", f"/api/v1/access/staff/{N}/", "staff.view"),
    ("PATCH", f"/api/v1/access/staff/{N}/", "staff.update"),
]
# Only admins create, edit or see admins (§4.2): these declare no code.
ADMIN_ONLY = [
    ("GET", "/api/v1/people/admins/"),
    ("POST", "/api/v1/people/admins/"),
    ("GET", f"/api/v1/people/admins/{N}/"),
    ("PATCH", f"/api/v1/people/admins/{N}/"),
    ("POST", f"/api/v1/people/admins/{N}/deactivate/"),
]
# Routes every signed-in user may also read without the code: the forms of
# any screen need the academy's currency and country.
OPEN_READS = {("GET", "/api/v1/academy/settings/")}
# Views exempt from a code: the caller's own account, sign-in, their own
# notifications, the public site, and the API docs.
SELF_SERVICE = {
    "CsrfView": "sets the CSRF cookie before sign-in",
    "ResendVerificationView": "sign-up and sign-in flow, anonymous",
    "VerifyEmailView": "sign-up and sign-in flow, anonymous",
    "LoginView": "signs in",
    "LogoutView": "signs the caller out",
    "MeView": "the caller's own account",
    "ChangePasswordView": "the caller's own password",
    "PasswordResetView": "anonymous password reset",
    "PasswordResetConfirmView": "anonymous password reset",
    "EmailListCreateView": "the caller's own email addresses",
    "EmailDeleteView": "the caller's own email addresses",
    "EmailPrimaryView": "the caller's own email addresses",
    "NotificationListView": "the caller's own notifications",
    "UnreadCountView": "the caller's own notifications",
    "ReadAllView": "the caller's own notifications",
    "ReadView": "the caller's own notifications",
    "SiteView": "the public site",
    "BrandingView": "the public site",
    "PageView": "the public site",
    "InquiryCreateView": "the public site's contact form",
    "SpectacularAPIView": "the API docs (DocsPermission)",
    "SpectacularSwaggerView": "the API docs (DocsPermission)",
    "SpectacularRedocView": "the API docs (DocsPermission)",
}


def _handlers():
    """Every (route, method) the API serves, and its view."""

    def walk(resolver, prefix=""):
        for pattern in resolver.url_patterns:
            # Joined as ``resolve().route`` joins them: a regex's "^" dropped.
            route = prefix + str(pattern.pattern).removeprefix("^")
            if isinstance(pattern, URLResolver):
                yield from walk(pattern, route)
            else:
                yield route, pattern.callback

    for route, callback in walk(get_resolver()):
        if not route.startswith("api/v1/"):
            continue
        cls = callback.cls
        allowed = {m for m in cls.http_method_names if m not in ("head", "options")}
        actions = getattr(callback, "actions", None)
        methods = (
            set(actions) & allowed
            if actions
            else {m for m in allowed if hasattr(cls, m)}
        )
        for method in methods:
            yield route, method.upper(), callback


def _declared(method: str, path: str):
    """The code the view behind ``path`` declares for ``method``."""
    match = resolve(path)
    callback = match.func
    view = callback.cls()
    view.kwargs, view.args = match.kwargs, ()
    actions = getattr(callback, "actions", None)
    if actions:
        view.action = actions[method.lower()]
    return match.route, required_code(view, SimpleNamespace(method=method))


def test_every_route_declares_its_code_or_is_named_exempt():
    served = set()
    for route, method, callback in _handlers():
        if callback.cls.__name__ in SELF_SERVICE:
            continue
        served.add((route, method))
    tabled = {}
    for method, path, code in ROUTES:
        route, declared = _declared(method, path)
        assert declared == code, (method, path)
        tabled[(route, method)] = code
    for method, path in ADMIN_ONLY:
        route, declared = _declared(method, path)
        assert declared is None, (method, path)
        tabled.setdefault((route, method), None)
    assert sorted(served - set(tabled)) == []
    assert sorted(set(tabled) - served) == []


def test_every_code_in_the_table_is_in_the_registry_and_in_use():
    """The registry's in-use set is exactly what the routes check."""
    used = set()
    for _, _, code in ROUTES:
        used.update((code,) if isinstance(code, str) else code)
    assert used <= registry.CODES
    assert used == registry.IN_USE


def _call(client, method, path):
    return getattr(client, method.lower())(path, {}, format="json")


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("method", "path", "code"), ROUTES)
def test_staff_pass_with_the_code_and_get_403_without_it(staff_for, method, path, code):
    codes = (code,) if isinstance(code, str) else code
    others = sorted(registry.CODES - set(codes))
    without = _call(staff_for(*others), method, path)
    if (method, path) in OPEN_READS:
        assert without.status_code == 200
    else:
        assert without.status_code == 403, without.content
    for one in codes:
        assert _call(staff_for(one), method, path).status_code != 403


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("method", "path", "code"), ROUTES)
def test_a_staff_account_with_no_role_or_a_retired_one_gets_nothing(
    staff_for, method, path, code
):
    if (method, path) in OPEN_READS:
        return
    assert _call(staff_for(), method, path).status_code == 403
    codes = (code,) if isinstance(code, str) else code
    retired = staff_for(*codes)
    retired.user.staff_roles.update(is_active=False)
    assert _call(retired, method, path).status_code == 403


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("method", "path"), ADMIN_ONLY)
def test_admin_accounts_are_for_admins_only(api_for, staff_for, method, path):
    everything = staff_for(*sorted(registry.CODES))
    assert _call(everything, method, path).status_code == 403
    assert _call(api_for("admin"), method, path).status_code != 403


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("method", "path", "code"), ROUTES)
def test_admins_pass_every_route(api_for, method, path, code):
    assert _call(api_for("admin"), method, path).status_code != 403
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run them to verify they fail**

Run (from `backend/`): `.venv/bin/pytest etqan/access/tests/test_routes.py etqan/access/tests/test_me.py -q`

Expected: FAIL — 130 failed, 257 passed:
- the coverage test finds every handler without a code;
- a staff account holding a row's code still gets 403 on every pre-existing route;
- `me/` has no `permissions`.

The 257 that pass are guards, named here:
- `test_admins_pass_every_route` (124) and `test_admin_accounts_are_for_admins_only` (5): admins already pass, and staff already can't;
- `test_a_staff_account_with_no_role_or_a_retired_one_gets_nothing` (117): every staff account is refused today;
- `test_every_code_in_the_table_is_in_the_registry_and_in_use`: the table and Task 2's registry already agree;
- `test_staff_pass_with_the_code_and_get_403_without_it` for Task 3's nine `access/` rows and the `academy/settings/` read (10).

```python
def _person_codes(kwargs: dict, **verbs: str) -> dict[str, str]:
    """``people/<kind>/`` needs ``<role>.<verb>``; ``people/admins/`` declares
    no code, so it stays for admins only (Plan 12 §4.2)."""
    role = KINDS[kwargs["kind"]]
    if role == "admin":
        return {}
    return {method: f"{role}.{verb}" for method, verb in verbs.items()}


def people_in_scope(request, role: str):
    return scope_for(request.user, services.people_queryset(role))
```

### Task 5: Data scope: staff see the academy's data for what they may view (R-5)

**Files:**
- Modify: `backend/etqan/identity/scopes.py`, `backend/etqan/catalogue/scopes.py`, `backend/etqan/billing/scopes.py`, `backend/etqan/payroll/scopes.py`, `backend/etqan/scheduling/scopes.py`, `backend/etqan/billing/api/views.py`, `backend/etqan/payroll/api/views.py`, `backend/etqan/scheduling/api/views.py`, `backend/etqan/scheduling/api/session_views.py`, `backend/etqan/scheduling/api/payloads.py`, `backend/etqan/scheduling/services/reports.py`, `backend/etqan/scheduling/services/attendance.py`
- Test: `backend/etqan/{identity,scheduling,billing,payroll}/tests/test_staff_scope.py` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1's `is_office`; Task 3's `staff_for`; Task 4's route codes.
- Produces:
  - each view's `_is_admin` helper becomes `_is_office(request)`;
  - the CSV refusal reads "CSV export is for admins and staff only.";
  - `can_read_report(user, session)` is true for any office user;
  - an office user may clear attendance.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Past a route's code, a staff account:
- lists every student with the office fields, and every course, retired ones too;
- lists every subscription with its price and notes, and every session with its staff-only fields and `has_report`;
- exports sessions, invoices and payslips as CSV;
- clears a student's attendance;
- reads any report and the missing ones;
- lists every invoice with its notes and every payment with `recorded_by`;
- sees every payslip, drafts included.

Create `backend/etqan/billing/tests/test_staff_scope.py`:

```python
"""Plan 12 R-5: a staff account past a billing route's code sees every
invoice and payment of the academy, with the office's fields, and exports
CSV, as an admin does."""

from datetime import date

import pytest

from etqan.billing import services
from etqan.scheduling.tests.conftest import make_student

pytestmark = pytest.mark.django_db
URL = "/api/v1/billing/invoices/"


def invoice(student, **fields):
    return services.create_invoice(
        student_id=student.id,
        amount_minor=1000,
        due_on=date(2026, 6, 8),
        description="June",
        notes="Office note",
        by=None,
        **fields,
    )


def test_staff_list_every_invoice_with_its_notes_and_export_them(staff_for, world):
    invoice(world.student)
    invoice(make_student("Aisha"))
    clerk = staff_for("invoice.view_any")
    rows = clerk.get(URL).json()["results"]
    assert sorted(row["student"]["full_name"] for row in rows) == ["Aisha", "Yusuf"]
    assert {row["notes"] for row in rows} == {"Office note"}
    resp = clerk.get(f"{URL}?format=csv")
    assert resp.status_code == 200
    assert resp["Content-Type"].startswith("text/csv")


def test_staff_read_one_invoice_and_its_payments_in_full(staff_for, world):
    made = invoice(world.student)
    services.add_payment(
        services.invoices_queryset().get(pk=made.pk),
        amount_minor=400,
        paid_on=date(2026, 6, 1),
        method="cash",
        by=None,
    )
    detail = staff_for("invoice.view").get(f"{URL}{made.pk}/").json()
    assert detail["notes"] == "Office note"
    payments = staff_for("payment.view_any").get(f"{URL}{made.pk}/payments/").json()
    assert [p["amount_minor"] for p in payments] == [400]
    assert "recorded_by" in payments[0]
```

Create `backend/etqan/identity/tests/test_staff_scope.py`:

```python
"""Plan 12 R-5: a staff account past a people or catalogue route's code sees
everyone and everything of the academy, as an admin does."""

import pytest

from etqan.catalogue import services as catalogue_services
from etqan.identity import services

pytestmark = pytest.mark.django_db


def test_staff_see_every_student_with_the_office_fields(staff_for):
    services.create_person("student", full_name="Yusuf", profile={"notes": "Shy"})
    services.create_person("student", full_name="Aisha")
    rows = staff_for("student.view_any").get("/api/v1/people/students/").json()
    names = [row["user"]["full_name"] for row in rows["results"]]
    assert names == ["Aisha", "Yusuf"]
    assert rows["results"][1]["profile"]["notes"] == "Shy"
    csv = staff_for("student.view_any").get("/api/v1/people/students/?format=csv")
    assert csv.status_code == 200


def test_staff_see_retired_courses_too(staff_for):
    catalogue_services.create_course(name_ar="أ", name_en="Old", is_active=False)
    catalogue_services.create_course(name_ar="ب", name_en="New")
    rows = staff_for("course.view_any").get("/api/v1/catalogue/courses/").json()
    assert sorted(row["name_en"] for row in rows["results"]) == ["New", "Old"]
```

Create `backend/etqan/payroll/tests/test_staff_scope.py`:

```python
"""Plan 12 R-5: a staff account past a payslip route's code sees every
payslip, drafts included, with the office's fields, and exports CSV."""

from datetime import date

import pytest

from etqan.payroll import services
from etqan.payroll.models import Payslip
from etqan.payroll.tests.conftest import adjust
from etqan.scheduling.tests.conftest import make_teacher

pytestmark = pytest.mark.django_db
PAYSLIPS = "/api/v1/payroll/payslips/"


def test_staff_see_every_payslip_drafts_included_and_export_them(staff_for, world):
    maryam = make_teacher("Maryam")
    adjust(world.teacher, "bonus", 500, on=date(2026, 6, 15))
    adjust(maryam, "bonus", 300, on=date(2026, 6, 20))
    services.generate(2026, 6)
    Payslip.objects.update(notes="Bank transfer")
    clerk = staff_for("payslip.view_any")
    rows = clerk.get(PAYSLIPS).json()["results"]
    assert sorted(row["teacher"]["full_name"] for row in rows) == ["Bilal", "Maryam"]
    assert {row["status"] for row in rows} == {"draft"}
    assert {row["notes"] for row in rows} == {"Bank transfer"}
    assert clerk.get(f"{PAYSLIPS}?format=csv").status_code == 200
    draft = Payslip.objects.get(teacher=maryam.teacher_profile)
    detail = staff_for("payslip.view").get(f"{PAYSLIPS}{draft.pk}/")
    assert detail.status_code == 200
```

Create `backend/etqan/scheduling/tests/test_staff_scope.py`:

```python
"""Plan 12 R-5: a staff account past a scheduling route's code sees every
subscription and session of the academy, with the office's fields, exports
CSV, clears attendance and reads reports, as an admin does."""

from datetime import UTC
from datetime import date
from datetime import datetime

import pytest

from etqan.scheduling import services
from etqan.scheduling.models import Session
from etqan.scheduling.tests.conftest import make_student
from etqan.scheduling.tests.conftest import make_teacher
from etqan.scheduling.tests.conftest import two_slots

pytestmark = pytest.mark.django_db
JUNE_1, JUNE_2 = date(2026, 6, 1), date(2026, 6, 2)


@pytest.fixture
def two(subscribe, world, clock):
    """Yusuf with Bilal (Mondays and Wednesdays) and Aisha with Maryam
    (Tuesdays): both first sessions marked present and Yusuf's reported; on
    4 June, Aisha's report is a day late."""
    mine = subscribe(slots=two_slots(), notes="Pays in cash")
    maryam = make_teacher("Maryam")
    world.course.teachers.add(maryam.teacher_profile)
    theirs = subscribe(
        student_id=make_student("Aisha").id,
        teacher_id=maryam.id,
        slots=[{"weekdays": [1], "start_time": "10:00"}],
    )
    clock.set(datetime(2026, 6, 3, 9, tzinfo=UTC))
    first = Session.objects.get(subscription=mine, occurs_on=JUNE_1)
    second = Session.objects.get(subscription=theirs, occurs_on=JUNE_2)
    services.mark_attendance(first, by=world.teacher, student_attendance="present")
    services.mark_attendance(second, by=maryam, student_attendance="present")
    services.write_report(first, by=world.teacher, behaviour=5, participation=4)
    clock.set(datetime(2026, 6, 4, 9, tzinfo=UTC))
    return first, second


def test_staff_list_every_subscription_with_its_price(staff_for, two):
    rows = staff_for("subscription.view_any").get("/api/v1/subscriptions/").json()
    assert sorted(row["student"]["full_name"] for row in rows["results"]) == [
        "Aisha",
        "Yusuf",
    ]
    assert all("price_minor" in row for row in rows["results"])
    first, _ = two
    detail = staff_for("subscription.view").get(
        f"/api/v1/subscriptions/{first.subscription_id}/"
    )
    assert detail.json()["notes"] == "Pays in cash"


def test_staff_list_every_session_and_export_them(staff_for, two):
    clerk = staff_for("session.view_any")
    rows = clerk.get("/api/v1/sessions/").json()["results"]
    assert len({row["teacher"]["id"] for row in rows}) == 2
    assert all("notes" in row and "has_report" in row for row in rows)
    resp = clerk.get("/api/v1/sessions/?format=csv")
    assert resp.status_code == 200
    assert resp["Content-Type"].startswith("text/csv")
    first, _ = two
    detail = staff_for("session.view").get(f"/api/v1/sessions/{first.pk}/").json()
    assert (detail["has_report"], detail["cancel_reason"]) == (True, "")


def test_staff_holding_attendance_update_clear_a_mark(staff_for, two):
    first, _ = two
    resp = staff_for("attendance.update").post(
        f"/api/v1/sessions/{first.pk}/attendance/",
        {"student_attendance": "not_set"},
        format="json",
    )
    assert resp.status_code == 200, resp.json()
    first.refresh_from_db()
    assert (first.status, first.student_attendance) == ("scheduled", "not_set")


def test_staff_read_every_report_and_the_missing_ones(staff_for, two):
    first, second = two
    report = staff_for("session_report.view").get(
        f"/api/v1/sessions/{first.pk}/report/"
    )
    assert (report.status_code, report.json()["behaviour"]) == (200, 5)
    missing = staff_for("session_report.view_any").get("/api/v1/reports/missing/")
    assert [row["id"] for row in missing.json()["results"]] == [second.pk]
```

### Task 6: Dev seeds: the preset in every academy, Sara Supervisor in `demo`

**Files:**
- Modify: `backend/etqan/tenants/management/commands/seed_dev.py`
- Test: `backend/etqan/tenants/tests/test_seed_dev.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2's `ensure_supervisor_preset` and `roles_queryset`; Task 3's `update_staff` and `staff_queryset`; `identity.services.create_person(..., invite=False)` and `get_user_by_email`.
- Produces: `seed_dev.STAFF = {"demo": ("Sara Supervisor", "sara@demo.test")}` and `seed_staff(spec)`, called from `seed_academy` after `seed_families`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Two runs of `seed_dev` leave:
- `demo` with the preset and Sara Supervisor holding it (staff, with no password);
- `other` with the preset only.

A retired preset makes `seed_staff` print a `skip:` line and add no one.

In `backend/etqan/tenants/tests/test_seed_dev.py`, replace:

```python
        assert "skip: Omar family — Yusuf Omar is already in another family." in out
        assert [f.name for f in identity.families_queryset()] == ["Renamed"]
```

with:

```python
        assert "skip: Omar family — Yusuf Omar is already in another family." in out
        assert [f.name for f in identity.families_queryset()] == ["Renamed"]


@pytest.mark.django_db
@override_settings(DEBUG=True)
def test_seed_dev_gives_demo_sara_supervisor_once_and_other_the_preset_only(
    capsys, monkeypatch
):
    from etqan.access import services as access  # noqa: PLC0415

    def state():
        return (
            [role.name_en for role in access.roles_queryset()],
            [
                (
                    user.full_name,
                    user.email,
                    [r.name_en for r in user.staff_roles.all()],
                )
                for user in access.staff_queryset()
            ],
        )

    call_command("seed_dev")
    call_command("seed_dev")
    connection.set_schema_to_public()
    demo = Academy.objects.get(subdomain="demo")
    other = Academy.objects.get(subdomain="other")
    with tenant_context(demo):
        assert state() == (
            ["Supervisor"],
            [("Sara Supervisor", "sara@demo.test", ["Supervisor"])],
        )
        sara = User.objects.get(email="sara@demo.test")
        assert (sara.role, sara.has_usable_password()) == ("staff", False)
    with tenant_context(other):
        assert state() == (["Supervisor"], [])
        access.roles_queryset().update(is_active=False)
        monkeypatch.setitem(seed_dev.STAFF, "other", ("Nour", "nour@other.test"))
        capsys.readouterr()
        seed_dev.seed_staff(seed_dev.STAFF["other"])
        assert state()[1] == []
    assert "skip: Nour — the Supervisor role is missing or retired" in (
        capsys.readouterr().out
    )
```

```python
# Plan 12a (spec §7): demo's staff member holding the Supervisor preset.
# Every academy has the preset; the other academy gets no staff.
STAFF = {"demo": ("Sara Supervisor", "sara@demo.test")}


def seed_staff(spec: tuple[str, str] | None) -> None:
    """The Supervisor preset in every academy, and Sara in demo, once: an
    account with her email already there is left alone. Like the seeded
    teachers, she gets no invite (an admin sends one from People → Staff)."""
    access_services.ensure_supervisor_preset()
    if spec is None:
        return
    full_name, email = spec
    try:
        identity_services.get_user_by_email(email)
    except NotFoundError:
        pass
    else:
        return
    preset = access_services.roles_queryset().filter(name_en="Supervisor").first()
    if preset is None or not preset.is_active:
        print(f"skip: {full_name} — the Supervisor role is missing or retired")  # noqa: T201
        return
    user = identity_services.create_person(
        "staff", full_name=full_name, email=email, invite=False
    )
    access_services.update_staff(user, fields={"role_ids": [preset.pk]}, by=None)


def seed_subscriptions(specs: list[dict]) -> None:
    """Idempotent: an academy that already has subscriptions is left alone.
```

**Interfaces:**

Create `dashboard/src/features/access/api.test.ts`:

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { api } from "@/lib/api";
import { accessApi } from "./api";

vi.mock("@/lib/api", async (orig) => {
	const actual = await orig<typeof import("@/lib/api")>();
	const ok = () => Promise.resolve({ data: { id: 1 } });
	return {
		...actual,
		api: { get: vi.fn(ok), post: vi.fn(ok), patch: vi.fn(ok) },
	};
});

describe("accessApi", () => {
	beforeEach(() => vi.clearAllMocks());

	it("reads the registry", async () => {
		await accessApi.registry();
		expect(api.get).toHaveBeenCalledWith("access/registry/");
	});

	it("lists, reads, creates and patches roles", async () => {
		await accessApi.roles({ active: "", page: 2 });
		expect(api.get).toHaveBeenCalledWith("access/roles/", {
			params: { page: "2" },
		});
		await accessApi.role(3);
		expect(api.get).toHaveBeenCalledWith("access/roles/3/");
		const body = { name_ar: "م", name_en: "M", permissions: ["invoice.view"] };
		await accessApi.createRole(body);
		expect(api.post).toHaveBeenCalledWith("access/roles/", body);
		await accessApi.updateRole(3, { is_active: false });
		expect(api.patch).toHaveBeenCalledWith("access/roles/3/", {
			is_active: false,
		});
	});

	it("lists, creates and patches staff", async () => {
		await accessApi.staff({ search: "sara", role: 1, active: "" });
		expect(api.get).toHaveBeenCalledWith("access/staff/", {
			params: { search: "sara", role: "1" },
		});
		const body = { full_name: "S", email: "s@x.test", role_ids: [1] };
		await accessApi.createStaff(body);
		expect(api.post).toHaveBeenCalledWith("access/staff/", body);
		await accessApi.updateStaff(7, { role_ids: [] });
		expect(api.patch).toHaveBeenCalledWith("access/staff/7/", {
			role_ids: [],
		});
	});
});
```

```tsx
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { renderHook, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { REGISTRY, roleRow, staffRow } from "@/test/access-fixtures";
import { accessApi } from "./api";
import {
	useCreateRole,
	useCreateStaff,
	useRegistry,
	useRole,
	useUpdateRole,
	useUpdateStaff,
} from "./queries";

vi.mock("./api", async (orig) => {
	const actual = await orig<typeof import("./api")>();
	return {
		...actual,
		accessApi: {
			...actual.accessApi,
			registry: vi.fn(),
			role: vi.fn(),
			createRole: vi.fn(),
			updateRole: vi.fn(),
			createStaff: vi.fn(),
			updateStaff: vi.fn(),
		},
	};
});

function spiedWrapper() {
	const client = new QueryClient({
		defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false }, mutations: { retry: false } },
	});
	const spy = vi.spyOn(client, "invalidateQueries");
	const wrap = ({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) => (
		<QueryClientProvider client={client}>{children}</QueryClientProvider>
	);
	return { wrap, spy };
}

describe("access queries", () => {
	beforeEach(() => vi.clearAllMocks());

	it("asks for the registry once, and a role only with an id", async () => {
		vi.mocked(accessApi.registry).mockResolvedValue(REGISTRY);
		const { wrap } = spiedWrapper();
		const { result } = renderHook(() => useRegistry(), { wrapper: wrap });
		await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.data).toEqual(REGISTRY));
		renderHook(() => useRole(undefined), { wrapper: wrap });
		expect(accessApi.role).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
	});

	it("refreshes both lists and me/ after any save", async () => {
		vi.mocked(accessApi.createRole).mockResolvedValue(roleRow({ id: 2 }));
		vi.mocked(accessApi.updateRole).mockResolvedValue(roleRow({ id: 2 }));
		vi.mocked(accessApi.createStaff).mockResolvedValue(staffRow({ id: 5 }));
		vi.mocked(accessApi.updateStaff).mockResolvedValue(staffRow({ id: 5 }));
		const { wrap, spy } = spiedWrapper();
		const createRole = renderHook(() => useCreateRole(), { wrapper: wrap });
		const updateRole = renderHook(() => useUpdateRole(), { wrapper: wrap });
		const createStaff = renderHook(() => useCreateStaff(), { wrapper: wrap });
		const updateStaff = renderHook(() => useUpdateStaff(), { wrapper: wrap });
		await createRole.result.current.mutateAsync({
			name_ar: "م",
			name_en: "M",
			permissions: [],
		});
		await updateRole.result.current.mutateAsync({
			id: 2,
			body: { is_active: false },
		});
		await createStaff.result.current.mutateAsync({
			full_name: "S",
			email: "s@x.test",
			role_ids: [],
		});
		await updateStaff.result.current.mutateAsync({
			id: 5,
			body: { is_active: false },
		});
		const keys = spy.mock.calls.map(([filters]) => filters?.queryKey);
		expect(keys).toEqual([
			["access"],
			["me"],
			["access"],
			["me"],
			["access"],
			["me"],
			["access"],
			["me"],
		]);
	});
});
```

Create `dashboard/src/features/identity/permissions.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { staffMe } from "@/test/access-fixtures";
import { ADMINS_ONLY, CanProvider, can, useCan } from "./permissions";
import type { Me } from "./schemas";

const person = (role: Me["role"]): Me => ({
	id: 1,
	email: "x@b.com",
	full_name: "X",
	role,
	profiles: [],
	// A teacher is never listed codes; if one were, it still would not count.
	permissions: ["invoice.view"],
});

function Probe({ code }: { code: string }) {
	const allowed = useCan();
	return <p>{allowed(code) ? "yes" : "no"}</p>;
}

describe("can", () => {
	it("lets an admin do everything, admins-only screens included", () => {
		expect(can(person("admin"), "invoice.create")).toBe(true);
		expect(can(person("admin"), ADMINS_ONLY)).toBe(true);
	});

	it("lets a staff account do only what me/ lists", () => {
		const sara = staffMe("invoice.view");
		expect(can(sara, "invoice.view")).toBe(true);
		expect(can(sara, "invoice.create")).toBe(false);
		expect(can(sara, ADMINS_ONLY)).toBe(false);
		expect(can({ ...sara, permissions: undefined }, "invoice.view")).toBe(
			false,
		);
	});

	it("never lets anyone else, or nobody, through", () => {
		for (const role of ["teacher", "student", "parent"] as const) {
			expect(can(person(role), "invoice.view")).toBe(false);
		}
		expect(can(undefined, "invoice.view")).toBe(false);
	});
});

describe("useCan", () => {
	it("reads the signed-in user inside the shell's provider", () => {
		render(
			<CanProvider me={staffMe("invoice.view")}>
				<Probe code="invoice.view" />
				<Probe code="invoice.create" />
			</CanProvider>,
		);
		expect(screen.getAllByText(/yes|no/).map((p) => p.textContent)).toEqual([
			"yes",
			"no",
		]);
	});

	it("hides nothing from a teacher: their own actions are checked by role", () => {
		render(
			<CanProvider me={person("teacher")}>
				<Probe code="attendance.update" />
			</CanProvider>,
		);
		expect(screen.getByText("yes")).toBeInTheDocument();
	});

	it("hides nothing outside the shell: the server decides", () => {
		render(<Probe code="invoice.create" />);
		expect(screen.getByText("yes")).toBeInTheDocument();
	});
});
```

```ts
import type {
	AccessRole,
	Registry,
	StaffMember,
} from "@/features/access/schemas";
import type { Me } from "@/features/identity/schemas";

const VERBS = [
	["view", "view", "عرض"],
	["view_any", "view all", "عرض الكل"],
	["create", "create", "إضافة"],
	["update", "update", "تعديل"],
	["restore", "restore", "استرجاع"],
	["restore_any", "restore all", "استرجاع الكل"],
	["replicate", "replicate", "استنساخ"],
	["reorder", "reorder", "إعادة ترتيب"],
	["delete", "delete", "حذف"],
	["delete_any", "delete all", "حذف الكل"],
	["force_delete", "force delete", "إجبار الحذف"],
	["force_delete_any", "force delete all", "إجبار حذف أي"],
] as const;
const ALL = VERBS.map(([code]) => code);

/** A registry cut down to two resources, one page and two widgets. */
export const REGISTRY: Registry = {
	verbs: VERBS.map(([code, label_en, label_ar]) => ({
		code,
		label_en,
		label_ar,
	})),
	resources: [
		{
			code: "invoice",
			label_en: "Invoices",
			label_ar: "الفواتير",
			verbs: [...ALL],
			in_use: ["view", "view_any", "create", "update"],
		},
		{
			code: "role",
			label_en: "Roles",
			label_ar: "الأدوار",
			verbs: ["view", "view_any", "create", "update", "delete", "delete_any"],
			in_use: ["view", "view_any", "create", "update"],
		},
	],
	pages: [
		{
			code: "page.themes",
			label_en: "Themes",
			label_ar: "السمات",
			in_use: false,
		},
	],
	widgets: [
		{
			code: "widget.revenue_stats",
			label_en: "Revenue stats",
			label_ar: "إحصائيات الإيرادات",
			in_use: true,
		},
		{
			code: "widget.students_stats",
			label_en: "Students stats",
			label_ar: "إحصائيات الطلاب",
			in_use: false,
		},
	],
};

/** A roles-admin row: an active role with no codes unless ``fields`` says. */
export function roleRow(fields: Partial<AccessRole> & Pick<AccessRole, "id">) {
	return {
		name_ar: `دور ${fields.id}`,
		name_en: `Role ${fields.id}`,
		permissions: [],
		is_active: true,
		members: 0,
		created_at: "2026-09-01T10:00:00Z",
		...fields,
	} satisfies AccessRole;
}

export const SUPERVISOR = roleRow({
	id: 1,
	name_ar: "مشرف",
	name_en: "Supervisor",
	permissions: ["session.view", "session.view_any"],
	members: 1,
});

/** A People → Staff row: Sara, active, holding the Supervisor preset. */
export function staffRow(
	fields: Partial<StaffMember> & Pick<StaffMember, "id">,
) {
	return {
		full_name: `Staff ${fields.id}`,
		email: `staff${fields.id}@demo.test`,
		is_active: true,
		roles: [{ id: 1, name_ar: "مشرف", name_en: "Supervisor" }],
		invited: false,
		created_at: "2026-09-01T10:00:00Z",
		...fields,
	} satisfies StaffMember;
}

/** A signed-in staff account holding exactly ``codes``. */
export function staffMe(...codes: string[]): Me {
	return {
		id: 50,
		email: "sara@demo.test",
		full_name: "Sara Supervisor",
		role: "staff",
		profiles: [],
		permissions: codes,
		is_super_admin: false,
	};
}
```

Create `dashboard/src/features/access/api.ts`:

```ts
import { api, clean, type Paginated, type QueryParams } from "@/lib/api";
import type {
	AccessRole,
	Registry,
	RoleChange,
	RoleInput,
	StaffChange,
	StaffInput,
	StaffMember,
} from "./schemas";

export const accessApi = {
	registry: async () => (await api.get<Registry>("access/registry/")).data,
	roles: async (params: QueryParams) =>
		(
			await api.get<Paginated<AccessRole>>("access/roles/", {
				params: clean(params),
			})
		).data,
	role: async (id: number) =>
		(await api.get<AccessRole>(`access/roles/${id}/`)).data,
	createRole: async (body: RoleInput) =>
		(await api.post<AccessRole>("access/roles/", body)).data,
	updateRole: async (id: number, body: RoleChange) =>
		(await api.patch<AccessRole>(`access/roles/${id}/`, body)).data,
	staff: async (params: QueryParams) =>
		(
			await api.get<Paginated<StaffMember>>("access/staff/", {
				params: clean(params),
			})
		).data,
	createStaff: async (body: StaffInput) =>
		(await api.post<StaffMember>("access/staff/", body)).data,
	updateStaff: async (id: number, body: StaffChange) =>
		(await api.patch<StaffMember>(`access/staff/${id}/`, body)).data,
};
```

Create `dashboard/src/features/access/queries.ts`:

```ts
import {
	keepPreviousData,
	useMutation,
	useQuery,
	useQueryClient,
} from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { meQueryKey } from "@/features/identity/queries";
import type { QueryParams } from "@/lib/api";
import { accessApi } from "./api";
import type { RoleChange, RoleInput, StaffChange, StaffInput } from "./schemas";

export const accessKey = ["access"] as const;
export const rolesKey = [...accessKey, "roles"] as const;
export const staffKey = [...accessKey, "staff"] as const;

/** Fixed for the life of a build: asked once. */
export function useRegistry() {
	return useQuery({
		queryKey: [...accessKey, "registry"],
		queryFn: accessApi.registry,
		staleTime: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY,
	});
}

export function useRoles(
	params: QueryParams,
	options: { enabled?: boolean } = {},
) {
	return useQuery({
		queryKey: [...rolesKey, "list", params],
		queryFn: () => accessApi.roles(params),
		placeholderData: keepPreviousData,
		enabled: options.enabled ?? true,
	});
}

export function useRole(id: number | undefined) {
	return useQuery({
		queryKey: [...rolesKey, "detail", id],
		queryFn: () => accessApi.role(id as number),
		enabled: id !== undefined,
	});
}

export function useStaff(params: QueryParams) {
	return useQuery({
		queryKey: [...staffKey, "list", params],
		queryFn: () => accessApi.staff(params),
		placeholderData: keepPreviousData,
	});
}

/** A role's or a staff account's save can change the rows of both lists
 * (names, member counts) and the caller's own codes: refresh them all. */
function useRefresh() {
	const qc = useQueryClient();
	return () => {
		qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: accessKey });
		qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: meQueryKey });
	};
}

export function useCreateRole() {
	const refresh = useRefresh();
	return useMutation({
		mutationFn: (body: RoleInput) => accessApi.createRole(body),
		onSuccess: refresh,
	});
}

export function useUpdateRole() {
	const refresh = useRefresh();
	return useMutation({
		mutationFn: ({ id, body }: { id: number; body: RoleChange }) =>
			accessApi.updateRole(id, body),
		onSuccess: refresh,
	});
}

export function useCreateStaff() {
	const refresh = useRefresh();
	return useMutation({
		mutationFn: (body: StaffInput) => accessApi.createStaff(body),
		onSuccess: refresh,
	});
}

export function useUpdateStaff() {
	const refresh = useRefresh();
	return useMutation({
		mutationFn: ({ id, body }: { id: number; body: StaffChange }) =>
			accessApi.updateStaff(id, body),
		onSuccess: refresh,
	});
}
```

Create `dashboard/src/features/access/schemas.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from "zod";

/** A code with its labels, as the registry lists it (Plan 12a §5.1). */
export interface Labelled {
	code: string;
	label_en: string;
	label_ar: string;
}
/** A resource and its verbs; `in_use` are those a route or screen checks. */
export interface RegistryResource extends Labelled {
	verbs: string[];
	in_use: string[];
}
/** A page or widget permission: one code, no verbs. */
export interface RegistryEntry extends Labelled {
	in_use: boolean;
}
export interface Registry {
	verbs: Labelled[];
	resources: RegistryResource[];
	pages: RegistryEntry[];
	widgets: RegistryEntry[];
}

/** A row of the roles admin (`GET access/roles/`). */
export interface AccessRole {
	id: number;
	name_ar: string;
	name_en: string;
	permissions: string[];
	is_active: boolean;
	/** How many active staff accounts hold it. */
	members: number;
	created_at: string;
}
export interface RoleInput {
	name_ar: string;
	name_en: string;
	permissions: string[];
}
/** A PATCH to one role: rename, recode, or retire (`false`) and restore. */
export type RoleChange = Partial<RoleInput> & { is_active?: boolean };
export type RoleRef = Pick<AccessRole, "id" | "name_ar" | "name_en">;

/** A row of People → Staff (`GET access/staff/`). */
export interface StaffMember {
	id: number;
	full_name: string;
	email: string;
	is_active: boolean;
	roles: RoleRef[];
	/** Invited and never signed in yet. */
	invited: boolean;
	created_at: string;
}
export interface StaffInput {
	full_name: string;
	email: string;
	role_ids: number[];
}
export type StaffChange = Partial<StaffInput> & { is_active?: boolean };

const name = z
	.string()
	.trim()
	.min(1, "access.roles.errors.nameRequired")
	.max(60, "access.roles.errors.nameTooLong");
export const roleFormSchema = z.object({
	name_ar: name,
	name_en: name,
	permissions: z.array(z.string()),
});
export type RoleFormValues = z.infer<typeof roleFormSchema>;

export const staffFormSchema = z.object({
	full_name: z
		.string()
		.trim()
		.min(1, "access.staff.errors.nameRequired")
		.max(255, "access.staff.errors.nameTooLong"),
	email: z.email("access.staff.errors.emailInvalid"),
	role_ids: z.array(z.number()),
	is_active: z.boolean(),
});
export type StaffFormValues = z.infer<typeof staffFormSchema>;

/** A role's or registry entry's name in the reader's language. */
export function localName(
	item: { name_ar: string; name_en: string },
	language: string,
): string {
	return language === "ar" ? item.name_ar : item.name_en;
}

/** A registry label in the reader's language. */
export function localLabel(item: Labelled, language: string): string {
	return language === "ar" ? item.label_ar : item.label_en;
}
```

Create `dashboard/src/features/identity/permissions.tsx`:

```tsx
import { createContext, type ReactNode, useContext } from "react";
import type { Me } from "./schemas";

/** Never a registry code, so only an admin passes it: People → Admins
 * (only admins see admins, Plan 12 §4.2). */
export const ADMINS_ONLY = "admins_only";

/**
 * Plan 12a §6.1: whether `me` may use what `code` guards. An admin always may
 * (R-4); a staff account when `me/` listed the code; nobody else. The server
 * still checks every route: this only hides what would be refused.
 */
export function can(me: Me | undefined, code: string): boolean {
	if (me?.role === "admin") return true;
	return me?.role === "staff" && (me.permissions ?? []).includes(code);
}

const CanContext = createContext<((code: string) => boolean) | null>(null);
const allowAll = () => true;

/** The app shell's: every screen inside it hides the office actions `me`
 * can't use. Only a staff account is ever narrowed: an admin can do
 * everything, and a teacher's, student's or parent's own actions (marking
 * attendance, writing a report) are checked by role on the server, never
 * by a code, so a shared component hides nothing from them. */
export function CanProvider({
	me,
	children,
}: {
	me: Me | undefined;
	children: ReactNode;
}) {
	return (
		<CanContext.Provider
			value={(code) => (me?.role === "staff" ? can(me, code) : true)}
		>
			{children}
		</CanContext.Provider>
	);
}

/**
 * Whether the signed-in user may use an office action, inside the app
 * shell. Outside it (a printed page, a component's unit test) nothing is
 * hidden: the server decides.
 */
export function useCan(): (code: string) => boolean {
	return useContext(CanContext) ?? allowAll;
}
```

Create `dashboard/src/features/shell/PermissionGate.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import {
	createMemoryHistory,
	createRootRoute,
	createRoute,
	createRouter,
	Outlet,
	RouterProvider,
} from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import "@/lib/i18n";
import { ADMINS_ONLY, newOrView } from "@/features/identity/permissions";
import type { Me } from "@/features/identity/schemas";
import { staffMe } from "@/test/access-fixtures";
import { PermissionGate } from "./PermissionGate";

const admin: Me = {
	id: 1,
	email: "a@b.com",
	full_name: "Amal",
	role: "admin",
	profiles: [],
};

function renderAt(me: Me, path: string) {
	const client = new QueryClient();
	client.setQueryData(["me"], me);
	const rootRoute = createRootRoute({
		component: () => (
			<PermissionGate>
				<Outlet />
			</PermissionGate>
		),
	});
	const home = createRoute({
		getParentRoute: () => rootRoute,
		path: "/",
		component: () => <p>home</p>,
	});
	const invoices = createRoute({
		getParentRoute: () => rootRoute,
		path: "/invoices",
		staticData: { permission: "invoice.view_any" },
		component: () => <p>invoices</p>,
	});
	const student = createRoute({
		getParentRoute: () => rootRoute,
		path: "/students/$personId",
		staticData: { permission: newOrView("personId", "student") },
		component: () => <p>student</p>,
	});
	const admins = createRoute({
		getParentRoute: () => rootRoute,
		path: "/admins",
		staticData: { permission: ADMINS_ONLY },
		component: () => <p>admins</p>,
	});
	const router = createRouter({
		routeTree: rootRoute.addChildren([home, invoices, student, admins]),
		history: createMemoryHistory({ initialEntries: [path] }),
	});
	return render(
		<QueryClientProvider client={client}>
			<RouterProvider router={router} />
		</QueryClientProvider>,
	);
}

const NO_ACCESS = "You don't have access to this page.";

describe("PermissionGate", () => {
	it("renders a screen whose code the staff account holds", async () => {
		renderAt(staffMe("invoice.view_any"), "/invoices");
		expect(await screen.findByText("invoices")).toBeInTheDocument();
	});

	it("says so, with a way home, when the code is missing", async () => {
		renderAt(staffMe("invoice.view"), "/invoices");
		expect(
			await screen.findByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: NO_ACCESS }),
		).toBeInTheDocument();
		expect(screen.queryByText("invoices")).toBeNull();
		expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "Back to home" })).toHaveAttribute(
			"href",
			"/",
		);
	});

	it("reads a detail screen's code off its params", async () => {
		renderAt(staffMe("student.view"), "/students/7");
		expect(await screen.findByText("student")).toBeInTheDocument();
	});

	it("asks for create on a detail screen's new page", async () => {
		renderAt(staffMe("student.view"), "/students/new");
		expect(
			await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: NO_ACCESS }),
		).toBeInTheDocument();
	});

	it("keeps admins-only screens from every staff account", async () => {
		renderAt(staffMe("staff.view_any", "role.view_any"), "/admins");
		expect(
			await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: NO_ACCESS }),
		).toBeInTheDocument();
	});

	it("lets an admin open everything, and anyone open a screen with no code", async () => {
		renderAt(admin, "/admins");
		expect(await screen.findByText("admins")).toBeInTheDocument();
	});

	it("gates nothing that names no code", async () => {
		renderAt(staffMe(), "/");
		expect(await screen.findByText("home")).toBeInTheDocument();
	});
});
```

```tsx
describe("/website", () => {
	async function landing(me: Me) {
		const queryClient = new QueryClient();
		queryClient.setQueryData(["me"], me);
		const beforeLoad = WebsiteIndex.options.beforeLoad as (options: {
			context: { queryClient: QueryClient };
		}) => Promise<void>;
		try {
			await beforeLoad({ context: { queryClient } });
		} catch (thrown) {
			return (thrown as { options: { to: string } }).options.to;
```

```ts
import { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { type AnyRoute, createRouter } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { routeTree } from "@/routeTree.gen";

/** Plan 12a §6.1: every office screen names the code it needs, as every API
 * route does (the backend's route table). A layout or a redirect is not a
 * screen. */
const OFFICE =
	/^\/_authed\/(scheduling|billing|payroll|people|catalogue|website|settings)\//;

const router = createRouter({
	routeTree,
	context: { queryClient: new QueryClient() },
});
const screens = (Object.values(router.routesById) as AnyRoute[]).filter(
	(route) =>
		OFFICE.test(route.id) &&
		route.options.component &&
		Object.keys(route.children ?? {}).length === 0,
);

describe("office screens", () => {
	it("finds every office screen", () => {
		expect(screens.length).toBeGreaterThan(30);
	});

	it.each(
		screens.map((route) => [route.id, route] as const),
	)("%s names its code", (_, route) => {
		expect(route.options.staticData?.permission).toBeTruthy();
	});
});
```

```tsx
 * (only admins see admins, Plan 12 §4.2). */
export const ADMINS_ONLY = "admins_only";

/** The code an office screen needs: fixed, or read off its route params. */
export type ScreenPermission =
	| string
	| ((params: Record<string, string>) => string);

declare module "@tanstack/react-router" {
	interface StaticDataRouteOption {
		/** Plan 12a: what a staff account needs to open this screen. */
		permission?: ScreenPermission;
	}
}

/** A detail screen that also creates: its "new" page needs
 * `<resource>.create`, one record `<resource>.view`. */
export function newOrView(param: string, resource: string): ScreenPermission {
	return (params) =>
		params[param] === "new" ? `${resource}.create` : `${resource}.view`;
}

export function screenCode(
	permission: ScreenPermission,
	params: Record<string, string>,
): string {
	return typeof permission === "function" ? permission(params) : permission;
}
```

```tsx
import { Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { ShieldBan } from "lucide-react";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import { usePageTitle } from "@/features/branding";
import { Card, CardContent, EmptyState, PageContainer, PageHeader } from "@/ui";

/** Plan 12a §6.1: a screen opened without the code it needs. */
export function NoAccess() {
	const { t } = useTranslation();
	usePageTitle(t("access.noAccess.title"));
	return (
		<PageContainer>
			<PageHeader title={t("access.noAccess.title")} />
			<Card>
				<CardContent>
					<EmptyState
						icon={ShieldBan}
						title={t("access.noAccess.body")}
						action={
							<Link
								to="/"
								className="font-medium text-primary-text underline-offset-4 hover:underline"
							>
								{t("access.noAccess.home")}
							</Link>
						}
					/>
				</CardContent>
			</Card>
		</PageContainer>
	);
}
```

Create `dashboard/src/features/shell/PermissionGate.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useMatches } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { can, screenCode } from "@/features/identity/permissions";
import { useMe } from "@/features/identity/queries";
import { NoAccess } from "./NoAccess";

/**
 * Plan 12a §6.1: every office screen names the code it needs
 * (`staticData.permission`); opened without it, the page says so instead
 * of rendering. The layouts' `requireOffice` has already sent teachers,
 * students and parents home, and the server refuses the data anyway.
 */
export function PermissionGate({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
	const { data: me } = useMe();
	const needed = useMatches({
		select: (matches) =>
			matches
				.map((match) =>
					match.staticData.permission
						? screenCode(
								match.staticData.permission,
								match.params as Record<string, string>,
							)
						: "",
				)
				.filter(Boolean)
				.join(" "),
	});
	if (needed && needed.split(" ").some((code) => !can(me, code))) {
		return <NoAccess />;
	}
	return <>{children}</>;
}
```


```tsx
import { can } from "@/features/identity/permissions";
import { meQueryOptions } from "@/features/identity/queries";
import { WEBSITE_TABS } from "@/features/shell/nav";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/_authed/website/")({
	// The first tab the user may open (Plan 12a); none: the first, which
	// then says they have no access.
	beforeLoad: async ({ context }) => {
		const me = await context.queryClient.ensureQueryData(meQueryOptions);
		const tab =
			WEBSITE_TABS.find((one) => can(me, one.code)) ?? WEBSITE_TABS[0];
		throw redirect({ to: tab.to });
```
