### Task 9: Dashboard: Settings → Roles, the list and the matrix editor

**Files:**
- Create: `dashboard/src/features/access/RolesList.tsx`, `dashboard/src/features/access/RoleEditor.tsx`, `dashboard/src/routes/_authed/settings.roles.index.tsx`, `dashboard/src/routes/_authed/settings.roles.$roleId.tsx`
- Modify: `dashboard/src/features/access/index.ts`, `dashboard/src/features/shell/nav.ts`, `dashboard/src/locales/{en,ar}/common.json`, `dashboard/src/routeTree.gen.ts` (regenerated)
- Test: `dashboard/src/features/access/RolesList.test.tsx`, `dashboard/src/features/access/RoleEditor.test.tsx` (new); `dashboard/src/features/shell/nav.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 7's access hooks, schemas and fixtures; Task 8's `newOrView`, `useCan` and `requireOffice`.
- Produces:
  - `RolesList()`, `RoleActiveToggle({role})`, `RoleEditor({roleId})` and `allCodes(registry)`;
  - the routes `/settings/roles` (`role.view_any`) and `/settings/roles/$roleId` (`newOrView("roleId", "role")`);
  - the nav item Settings → Roles (`nav.roles`, `role.view_any`);
  - i18n `access.roles.*`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

- The list:
  - shows the name in the reader's language, the members and the status, with Edit, Retire and Restore;
  - filters by status from page 1;
  - hides Add and Retire from staff without the codes.
- The editor:
  - shows each resource's verbs, greying those not used yet;
  - selects a row and then everything, and saves the codes sorted;
  - adds a role and returns to the list;
  - asks for both names;
  - shows the server's 403 on `permissions`;
  - is read-only for staff who may see but not change;
  - says so when the role can't be loaded.
- The nav lists Roles last in Settings.

Create `dashboard/src/features/access/RoleEditor.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { AxiosError, AxiosHeaders } from "axios";
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import "@/lib/i18n";
import { CanProvider } from "@/features/identity/permissions";
import { REGISTRY, roleRow, staffMe } from "@/test/access-fixtures";
import { renderWithRouter } from "@/test/render";
import { accessApi } from "./api";
import { RoleEditor } from "./RoleEditor";

vi.mock("./api", async (orig) => {
	const actual = await orig<typeof import("./api")>();
	return {
		...actual,
		accessApi: {
			...actual.accessApi,
			registry: vi.fn(),
			role: vi.fn(),
			createRole: vi.fn(),
			updateRole: vi.fn(),
		},
	};
});

const CLERK = roleRow({
	id: 2,
	name_ar: "محاسب",
	name_en: "Accountant",
	permissions: ["invoice.view_any"],
});

function forbidden(field: string, message: string) {
	const response = {
		status: 403,
		data: { [field]: [message] },
		statusText: "Forbidden",
		headers: {},
		config: { headers: new AxiosHeaders() },
	};
	return new AxiosError("403", "ERR", undefined, undefined, response);
}

function invoices() {
	return screen.getByRole("group", { name: "Invoices" });
}

describe("RoleEditor", () => {
	beforeEach(() => {
		vi.clearAllMocks();
		vi.mocked(accessApi.registry).mockResolvedValue(REGISTRY);
		vi.mocked(accessApi.role).mockResolvedValue(CLERK);
		vi.mocked(accessApi.createRole).mockResolvedValue(CLERK);
		vi.mocked(accessApi.updateRole).mockResolvedValue(CLERK);
	});

	it("shows each resource's verbs, greying those not used yet", async () => {
		renderWithRouter(<RoleEditor roleId="2" />);
		await screen.findByRole("group", { name: "Invoices" });
		const row = invoices();
		expect(
			within(row).getByRole("checkbox", { name: "view all" }),
		).toBeChecked();
		expect(
			within(row).getByRole("checkbox", { name: "view" }),
		).not.toBeChecked();
		expect(
			within(row).getByRole("checkbox", { name: "restore all (not used yet)" }),
		).toBeEnabled();
		expect(within(row).getAllByRole("checkbox")).toHaveLength(13);
		const roles = screen.getByRole("group", { name: "Roles" });
		expect(within(roles).getAllByRole("checkbox")).toHaveLength(7);
		expect(screen.getByLabelText(/^Name \(English\)/)).toHaveValue(
			"Accountant",
		);
	});

	it("selects a whole row, then every permission, and saves the codes sorted", async () => {
		renderWithRouter(<RoleEditor roleId="2" />);
		await screen.findByRole("group", { name: "Invoices" });
		await userEvent.click(
			screen.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Select all of Roles" }),
		);
		expect(
			screen.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Select all of Roles" }),
		).toBeChecked();
		await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save role" }));
		await waitFor(() =>
			expect(accessApi.updateRole).toHaveBeenCalledWith(2, {
				name_ar: "محاسب",
				name_en: "Accountant",
				permissions: [
					"invoice.view_any",
					"role.create",
					"role.delete",
					"role.delete_any",
					"role.update",
					"role.view",
					"role.view_any",
				],
			}),
		);
		expect(await screen.findByText("Role saved.")).toBeInTheDocument();
		await userEvent.click(
			screen.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Select every permission" }),
		);
		await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("tab", { name: "Widgets" }));
		expect(
			screen.getByRole("checkbox", {
				name: "Students stats (not used yet)",
			}),
		).toBeChecked();
		await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("tab", { name: "Pages" }));
		await userEvent.click(
			screen.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Themes (not used yet)" }),
		);
		await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save role" }));
		const body = vi.mocked(accessApi.updateRole).mock.calls.at(-1)?.[1];
		expect(body?.permissions).toHaveLength(12 + 6 + 2);
		expect(body?.permissions).not.toContain("page.themes");
	});

	it("adds a role and goes back to the list", async () => {
		renderWithRouter(<RoleEditor roleId="new" />, {
			extraPaths: ["/settings/roles"],
		});
		await userEvent.type(
			await screen.findByLabelText(/^Name \(Arabic\)/),
			" محاسب ",
		);
		await userEvent.type(
			screen.getByLabelText(/^Name \(English\)/),
			"Accountant",
		);
		await userEvent.click(
			within(invoices()).getByRole("checkbox", { name: "create" }),
		);
		await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save role" }));
		await waitFor(() =>
			expect(accessApi.createRole).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
				name_ar: "محاسب",
				name_en: "Accountant",
				permissions: ["invoice.create"],
			}),
		);
		expect(await screen.findByText("at /settings/roles")).toBeInTheDocument();
		expect(accessApi.role).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
	});

	it("asks for both names", async () => {
		renderWithRouter(<RoleEditor roleId="new" />);
		await userEvent.click(
			await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Save role" }),
		);
		expect(await screen.findAllByText("Enter a name.")).toHaveLength(2);
		expect(accessApi.createRole).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
	});

	it("shows the server's refusal of a code the editor lacks", async () => {
		vi.mocked(accessApi.updateRole).mockRejectedValue(
			forbidden(
				"permissions",
				"You can only add permissions you hold yourself.",
			),
		);
		renderWithRouter(<RoleEditor roleId="2" />);
		await screen.findByRole("group", { name: "Invoices" });
		await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save role" }));
		expect(
			await screen.findByText(
				"You can only add permissions you hold yourself.",
			),
		).toBeInTheDocument();
	});

	it("is read-only for staff who may see but not change roles", async () => {
		renderWithRouter(
			<CanProvider me={staffMe("role.view")}>
				<RoleEditor roleId="2" />
			</CanProvider>,
		);
		await screen.findByRole("group", { name: "Invoices" });
		expect(
			screen.getByText("You can see this role but not change it."),
		).toBeInTheDocument();
		expect(screen.getByLabelText(/^Name \(English\)/)).toBeDisabled();
		expect(
			within(invoices()).getByRole("checkbox", { name: "view" }),
		).toBeDisabled();
		expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: "Save role" })).toBeNull();
	});

	it("says so when the role can't be loaded", async () => {
		vi.mocked(accessApi.role).mockRejectedValue(new Error("x"));
		renderWithRouter(<RoleEditor roleId="2" />);
		expect(
			await screen.findByText("Couldn't load this role."),
		).toBeInTheDocument();
	});
});
```

Create `dashboard/src/features/access/RolesList.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import "@/lib/i18n";
import { CanProvider } from "@/features/identity/permissions";
import i18n from "@/lib/i18n";
import { roleRow, SUPERVISOR, staffMe } from "@/test/access-fixtures";
import { renderWithRouter } from "@/test/render";
import { accessApi } from "./api";
import { RolesList } from "./RolesList";
import type { AccessRole } from "./schemas";

vi.mock("./api", async (orig) => {
	const actual = await orig<typeof import("./api")>();
	return {
		...actual,
		accessApi: { ...actual.accessApi, roles: vi.fn(), updateRole: vi.fn() },
	};
});

const page = (results: AccessRole[]) => ({
	count: results.length,
	next: null,
	previous: null,
	results,
});
const CLERK = roleRow({
	id: 2,
	name_ar: "محاسب",
	name_en: "Accountant",
	is_active: false,
	members: 0,
});
const PATHS = ["/settings/roles/$roleId"];

describe("RolesList", () => {
	beforeEach(async () => {
		vi.clearAllMocks();
		await i18n.changeLanguage("en");
		vi.mocked(accessApi.roles).mockResolvedValue(page([SUPERVISOR, CLERK]));
		vi.mocked(accessApi.updateRole).mockResolvedValue(CLERK);
	});

	it("lists each role's name, members and status, with its actions", async () => {
		renderWithRouter(<RolesList />, { extraPaths: PATHS });
		const row = await screen.findByRole("row", { name: /Supervisor/ });
		expect(
			within(row)
				.getAllByRole("cell")
				.slice(0, 3)
				.map((cell) => cell.textContent),
		).toEqual(["Supervisor", "1", "Active"]);
		expect(
			within(row).getByRole("link", { name: "Edit Supervisor" }),
		).toHaveAttribute("href", "/settings/roles/1");
		expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "Add role" })).toHaveAttribute(
			"href",
			"/settings/roles/new",
		);
		const retired = screen.getByRole("row", { name: /Accountant/ });
		expect(within(retired).getByText("Retired")).toBeInTheDocument();
	});

	it("names roles in the reader's language", async () => {
		await i18n.changeLanguage("ar");
		renderWithRouter(<RolesList />, { extraPaths: PATHS });
		expect(
			await screen.findByRole("row", { name: /مشرف/ }),
		).toBeInTheDocument();
		await i18n.changeLanguage("en");
	});

	it("filters by status from the first page", async () => {
		renderWithRouter(<RolesList />, { extraPaths: PATHS });
		await screen.findByRole("row", { name: /Supervisor/ });
		await userEvent.selectOptions(
			screen.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Status" }),
			"false",
		);
		await waitFor(() =>
			expect(accessApi.roles).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith({
				page: 1,
				active: "false",
			}),
		);
	});

	it("retires and restores, keeping the members", async () => {
		renderWithRouter(<RolesList />, { extraPaths: PATHS });
		await userEvent.click(
			await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Restore Accountant" }),
		);
		await waitFor(() =>
			expect(accessApi.updateRole).toHaveBeenCalledWith(2, {
				is_active: true,
			}),
		);
		expect(await screen.findByText("Role restored.")).toBeInTheDocument();
		await userEvent.click(
			screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Retire Supervisor" }),
		);
		expect(accessApi.updateRole).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(1, {
			is_active: false,
		});
	});

	it("hides Add and Retire from staff without the codes", async () => {
		renderWithRouter(
			<CanProvider me={staffMe("role.view_any")}>
				<RolesList />
			</CanProvider>,
			{ extraPaths: PATHS },
		);
		const row = await screen.findByRole("row", { name: /Supervisor/ });
		expect(
			within(row).getByRole("link", { name: "Edit Supervisor" }),
		).toBeVisible();
		expect(within(row).queryByRole("button")).toBeNull();
		expect(screen.queryByRole("link", { name: "Add role" })).toBeNull();
	});
});
```

Create `dashboard/src/features/access/RoleEditor.tsx`:

```tsx
import { zodResolver } from "@hookform/resolvers/zod";
import { useNavigate } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useState } from "react";
import { useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import { useCan } from "@/features/identity/permissions";
import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";
import { useFieldError } from "@/lib/field-error";
import { applyServerErrors } from "@/lib/form-errors";
import {
	Alert,
	AlertDescription,
	Button,
	Checkbox,
	Field,
	FormError,
	Input,
	Label,
	Spinner,
	toast,
} from "@/ui";
import { useCreateRole, useRegistry, useRole, useUpdateRole } from "./queries";
import {
	type AccessRole,
	localLabel,
	type Registry,
	type RegistryEntry,
	type RoleFormValues,
	roleFormSchema,
} from "./schemas";

const TABS = ["resources", "pages", "widgets"] as const;
type Tab = (typeof TABS)[number];

/** Every code the registry lists: what "Select every permission" holds. */
export function allCodes(registry: Registry): string[] {
	return [
		...registry.resources.flatMap((r) => r.verbs.map((v) => `${r.code}.${v}`)),
		...registry.pages.map((p) => p.code),
		...registry.widgets.map((w) => w.code),
	];
}

/** One permission's checkbox. A code no route or screen checks yet is shown
 * greyed and says so, but can still be held (R-1). */
function Code({
	code,
	label,
	inUse,
	held,
	disabled,
	onChange,
}: {
	code: string;
	label: string;
	inUse: boolean;
	held: boolean;
	disabled: boolean;
	onChange: (on: boolean) => void;
}) {
	const { t } = useTranslation();
	const id = `role-code-${code}`;
	return (
		<div
			className={cn(
				"flex items-center gap-2",
				!inUse && "text-muted-foreground",
			)}
		>
			<Checkbox
				id={id}
				checked={held}
				disabled={disabled}
				onCheckedChange={onChange}
			/>
			<Label htmlFor={id} className="font-normal">
				{inUse ? label : `${label} (${t("access.roles.notUsed")})`}
			</Label>
		</div>
	);
}

/** Settings → Roles → one role (Plan 12a §6.1): its names and TutorHamster's
 * tabs Resources · Pages · Widgets, with "Select all" for the whole role
 * and for each resource. `roleId` is "new" to add one. */
export function RoleEditor({ roleId }: { roleId: string }) {
	const { t } = useTranslation();
	const creating = roleId === "new";
	const registry = useRegistry();
	const role = useRole(creating ? undefined : Number(roleId));
	if (registry.isError || role.isError) {
		return (
			<Alert variant="destructive">
				<AlertDescription>{t("access.roles.loadRoleError")}</AlertDescription>
			</Alert>
		);
	}
	if (!registry.data || (!creating && !role.data)) {
		return (
			<div className="flex justify-center py-6">
				<Spinner />
			</div>
		);
	}
	return (
		<RoleForm
			key={role.data?.id ?? "new"}
			registry={registry.data}
			role={role.data}
		/>
	);
}

function RoleForm({
	registry,
	role,
}: {
	registry: Registry;
	role: AccessRole | undefined;
}) {
	const { t, i18n } = useTranslation();
	const can = useCan();
	const navigate = useNavigate();
	const create = useCreateRole();
	const update = useUpdateRole();
	const fieldError = useFieldError();
	const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>("resources");
	const editable = can(role ? "role.update" : "role.create");
	const {
		register,
		handleSubmit,
		setError,
		setValue,
		watch,
		formState: { errors, isSubmitting },
	} = useForm<RoleFormValues>({
		resolver: zodResolver(roleFormSchema),
		defaultValues: {
			name_ar: role?.name_ar ?? "",
			name_en: role?.name_en ?? "",
			permissions: role?.permissions ?? [],
		},
	});
	const held = new Set(watch("permissions"));
	const every = allCodes(registry);
	const language = i18n.language;
	const verbLabel = (verb: string) => {
		const found = registry.verbs.find((v) => v.code === verb);
		return found ? localLabel(found, language) : verb;
	};

	function toggle(codes: readonly string[], on: boolean) {
		const next = new Set(held);
		for (const code of codes) {
			if (on) next.add(code);
			else next.delete(code);
		}
		setValue("permissions", [...next].sort(), { shouldDirty: true });
	}

	async function onSubmit(values: RoleFormValues) {
		const body = {
			name_ar: values.name_ar.trim(),
			name_en: values.name_en.trim(),
			permissions: values.permissions,
		};
		try {
			if (role) await update.mutateAsync({ id: role.id, body });
			else await create.mutateAsync(body);
			toast({ description: t("access.roles.saved"), variant: "success" });
			if (!role) navigate({ to: "/settings/roles" });
		} catch (error) {
			applyServerErrors(error, setError);
		}
	}

	const entries = (list: RegistryEntry[]) => (
		<div className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-lg border border-border p-3">
			{list.map((entry) => (
				<Code
					key={entry.code}
					code={entry.code}
					label={localLabel(entry, language)}
					inUse={entry.in_use}
					held={held.has(entry.code)}
					disabled={!editable}
					onChange={(on) => toggle([entry.code], on)}
				/>
			))}
		</div>
	);

	return (
		<form
			onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)}
			className="flex flex-col gap-4"
			noValidate
		>
			{editable ? null : (
				<Alert>
					<AlertDescription>{t("access.roles.readOnly")}</AlertDescription>
				</Alert>
			)}
			<div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
				<Field
					id="role-name-ar"
					label={t("access.roles.nameAr")}
					error={fieldError(errors.name_ar?.message)}
					required
				>
					<Input dir="rtl" disabled={!editable} {...register("name_ar")} />
				</Field>
				<Field
					id="role-name-en"
					label={t("access.roles.nameEn")}
					error={fieldError(errors.name_en?.message)}
					required
				>
					<Input dir="ltr" disabled={!editable} {...register("name_en")} />
				</Field>
			</div>
			<Code
				code="all"
				label={t("access.roles.selectAllRole")}
				inUse
				held={every.every((code) => held.has(code))}
				disabled={!editable}
				onChange={(on) => toggle(every, on)}
			/>
			<div
				role="tablist"
				aria-label={t("access.roles.permissions")}
				className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 border-b border-border pb-2"
			>
				{TABS.map((one) => (
					<button
						key={one}
						type="button"
						role="tab"
						id={`role-tab-${one}`}
						aria-selected={tab === one}
						aria-controls={`role-panel-${one}`}
						onClick={() => setTab(one)}
						className="rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground hover:bg-secondary aria-selected:bg-secondary aria-selected:text-foreground"
					>
						{t(`access.roles.tabs.${one}`)}
					</button>
				))}
			</div>
			<div
				role="tabpanel"
				id={`role-panel-${tab}`}
				aria-labelledby={`role-tab-${tab}`}
				className="flex flex-col gap-3"
			>
				{tab === "resources"
					? registry.resources.map((resource) => {
							const label = localLabel(resource, language);
							const codes = resource.verbs.map((v) => `${resource.code}.${v}`);
							return (
								<fieldset
									key={resource.code}
									className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-lg border border-border p-3"
								>
									<legend className="px-1 text-sm font-semibold">
										{label}
									</legend>
									<div className="flex items-center gap-2">
										<Checkbox
											id={`role-row-${resource.code}`}
											aria-label={t("access.roles.selectAllRow", {
												name: label,
											})}
											checked={codes.every((code) => held.has(code))}
											disabled={!editable}
											onCheckedChange={(on) => toggle(codes, on)}
										/>
										<span aria-hidden="true" className="text-sm">
											{t("access.roles.selectAll")}
										</span>
									</div>
									<div className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-5 gap-y-2">
										{resource.verbs.map((verb) => (
											<Code
												key={verb}
												code={`${resource.code}.${verb}`}
												label={verbLabel(verb)}
												inUse={resource.in_use.includes(verb)}
												held={held.has(`${resource.code}.${verb}`)}
												disabled={!editable}
												onChange={(on) =>
													toggle([`${resource.code}.${verb}`], on)
												}
											/>
										))}
									</div>
								</fieldset>
							);
						})
					: null}
				{tab === "pages" ? entries(registry.pages) : null}
				{tab === "widgets" ? entries(registry.widgets) : null}
			</div>
			{errors.permissions ? (
				<FormError>{fieldError(errors.permissions.message)}</FormError>
			) : null}
			{errors.root?.server ? (
				<Alert variant="destructive">
					<AlertDescription>
						{fieldError(errors.root.server.message)}
					</AlertDescription>
				</Alert>
			) : null}
			{editable ? (
				<div className="flex justify-end">
					<Button type="submit" disabled={isSubmitting}>
						{t("access.roles.save")}
					</Button>
				</div>
			) : null}
		</form>
	);
}
```

Create `dashboard/src/features/access/RolesList.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { KeyRound } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import { Pager } from "@/components/Pager";
import { useCan } from "@/features/identity/permissions";
import type { QueryParams } from "@/lib/api";
import { errorText } from "@/lib/form-errors";
import {
	Alert,
	AlertDescription,
	Button,
	buttonVariants,
	Card,
	CardContent,
	EmptyState,
	Select,
	Spinner,
	toast,
} from "@/ui";
import { useRoles, useUpdateRole } from "./queries";
import { type AccessRole, localName } from "./schemas";

const PAGE_SIZE = 25;

/** Retire an active role or restore a retired one; its members keep it. */
export function RoleActiveToggle({ role }: { role: AccessRole }) {
	const { t, i18n } = useTranslation();
	const update = useUpdateRole();
	const retiring = role.is_active;
	return (
		<Button
			size="sm"
			variant="outline"
			disabled={update.isPending}
			aria-label={t(
				retiring ? "access.roles.retireName" : "access.roles.restoreName",
				{ name: localName(role, i18n.language) },
			)}
			onClick={() =>
				update.mutate(
					{ id: role.id, body: { is_active: !retiring } },
					{
						onSuccess: () =>
							toast({
								description: t(
									retiring
										? "access.roles.retiredDone"
										: "access.roles.restoredDone",
								),
								variant: "success",
							}),
						onError: (error) =>
							toast({
								description: errorText(error, t),
								variant: "destructive",
							}),
					},
				)
			}
		>
			{t(retiring ? "access.roles.retire" : "access.roles.restore")}
		</Button>
	);
}

/** Settings → Roles (Plan 12a §6.1): each role's name in the reader's
 * language, its members and status, with Edit, Retire and Restore. */
export function RolesList() {
	const { t, i18n } = useTranslation();
	const can = useCan();
	const [params, setParams] = useState<QueryParams>({ page: 1 });
	const { data, isPending, isError } = useRoles(params);
	const rows = data?.results ?? [];
	const page = Number(params.page ?? 1);
	const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((data?.count ?? 0) / PAGE_SIZE));

	return (
		<div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
			<div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
				<Select
					aria-label={t("access.roles.columns.status")}
					className="w-auto"
					value={String(params.active ?? "")}
					onChange={(e) => setParams({ page: 1, active: e.target.value })}
				>
					<option value="">{t("access.roles.anyStatus")}</option>
					<option value="true">{t("access.roles.status.active")}</option>
					<option value="false">{t("access.roles.status.retired")}</option>
				</Select>
				{can("role.create") ? (
					<Link
						to="/settings/roles/$roleId"
						params={{ roleId: "new" }}
						className={buttonVariants({ size: "sm" })}
					>
						{t("access.roles.add")}
					</Link>
				) : null}
			</div>
			{isError ? (
				<Alert variant="destructive">
					<AlertDescription>{t("access.roles.loadError")}</AlertDescription>
				</Alert>
			) : isPending ? (
				<div className="flex justify-center py-6">
					<Spinner />
				</div>
			) : rows.length === 0 ? (
				<Card>
					<CardContent>
						<EmptyState icon={KeyRound} title={t("access.roles.empty")} />
					</CardContent>
				</Card>
			) : (
				<div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-lg border border-border">
					<table className="w-full text-sm">
						<thead className="bg-secondary text-start text-muted-foreground">
							<tr>
								{(["name", "members", "status"] as const).map((key) => (
									<th
										key={key}
										scope="col"
										className="p-3 text-start font-medium"
									>
										{t(`access.roles.columns.${key}`)}
									</th>
								))}
								<th scope="col" className="p-3">
									<span className="sr-only">
										{t("access.roles.columns.actions")}
									</span>
								</th>
							</tr>
						</thead>
						<tbody>
							{rows.map((role) => {
								const name = localName(role, i18n.language);
								return (
									<tr key={role.id} className="border-t border-border">
										<td className="p-3 font-medium">{name}</td>
										<td className="p-3">{role.members}</td>
										<td className="p-3">
											{t(
												role.is_active
													? "access.roles.status.active"
													: "access.roles.status.retired",
											)}
										</td>
										<td className="p-3">
											<div className="flex flex-wrap justify-end gap-1">
												<Link
													to="/settings/roles/$roleId"
													params={{ roleId: String(role.id) }}
													aria-label={t("access.roles.editName", { name })}
													className={buttonVariants({
														size: "sm",
														variant: "outline",
													})}
												>
													{t("access.roles.edit")}
												</Link>
												{can("role.update") ? (
													<RoleActiveToggle role={role} />
												) : null}
											</div>
										</td>
									</tr>
								);
							})}
						</tbody>
					</table>
				</div>
			)}
			<Pager
				page={page}
				pages={pages}
				onChange={(next) => setParams({ ...params, page: next })}
			/>
		</div>
	);
}
```

Create `dashboard/src/routes/_authed/settings.roles.$roleId.tsx`:

```tsx
import { createFileRoute, Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import { RoleEditor } from "@/features/access";
import { usePageTitle } from "@/features/branding";
import { newOrView } from "@/features/identity/permissions";
import { requireOffice } from "@/features/identity/require-admin";
import { PageContainer, PageHeader } from "@/ui";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/_authed/settings/roles/$roleId")({
	staticData: { permission: newOrView("roleId", "role") },
	beforeLoad: ({ context }) => requireOffice(context),
	component: function RoleRoute() {
		const { t } = useTranslation();
		const { roleId } = Route.useParams();
		const title =
			roleId === "new" ? t("access.roles.new") : t("access.roles.editTitle");
		usePageTitle(title);
		return (
			<PageContainer>
				<Link
					to="/settings/roles"
					className="text-sm font-medium text-primary-text underline-offset-4 hover:underline"
				>
					{t("access.roles.back")}
				</Link>
				<PageHeader title={title} />
				<RoleEditor roleId={roleId} />
			</PageContainer>
		);
	},
});
```

Create `dashboard/src/routes/_authed/settings.roles.index.tsx`:

```tsx
import { createFileRoute } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import { RolesList } from "@/features/access";
import { usePageTitle } from "@/features/branding";
import { requireOffice } from "@/features/identity/require-admin";
import { PageContainer, PageHeader } from "@/ui";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/_authed/settings/roles/")({
	staticData: { permission: "role.view_any" },
	beforeLoad: ({ context }) => requireOffice(context),
	component: function RolesRoute() {
		const { t } = useTranslation();
		usePageTitle(t("access.roles.title"));
		return (
			<PageContainer>
				<PageHeader
					title={t("access.roles.title")}
					description={t("access.roles.subtitle")}
				/>
				<RolesList />
			</PageContainer>
		);
	},
});
```

### Task 10: Dashboard: People → Staff, with invites and the roles picker

**Files:**
- Create: `dashboard/src/features/access/StaffList.tsx`, `dashboard/src/features/access/StaffDialog.tsx`, `dashboard/src/routes/_authed/people.staff.tsx`
- Modify: `dashboard/src/features/access/index.ts`, `dashboard/src/features/shell/nav.ts`, `dashboard/src/locales/{en,ar}/common.json` (with D9's "المديرون"), `dashboard/src/routeTree.gen.ts` (regenerated)
- Test: `dashboard/src/features/access/StaffList.test.tsx`, `dashboard/src/features/access/StaffDialog.test.tsx` (new); `dashboard/src/features/shell/nav.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 7's staff and roles hooks, schemas and fixtures; Task 8's `useCan`.
- Produces:
  - `StaffList()` and `StaffDialog({member?})`;
  - the route `/people/staff` (`staff.view_any`) under the People layout;
  - the nav item People → Staff (`nav.staff`, `staff.view_any`);
  - i18n `access.staff.*`, and `nav.admins` in Arabic "المديرون".

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

- The list:
  - shows each member's email, roles (or "No roles") and status, and "Invited";
  - searches and filters by role and status from page 1;
  - hides Add and Edit without the codes;
  - closes a dialog before the deactivated member leaves the Active-filtered list: submitted with Enter, the dialog is gone and the page is not left inert.
- The dialog:
  - adds a member with the active roles chosen, and says the invite went;
  - keeps a retired role already held (marked "(retired)") and deactivates;
  - checks the name and email before asking the server;
  - puts the server's 403 on `role_ids`;
  - disables the roles and Active on the caller's own account, with a note.
- The nav lists Staff after Teachers.

Create `dashboard/src/features/access/StaffDialog.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { AxiosError, AxiosHeaders } from "axios";
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import "@/lib/i18n";
import { identityApi } from "@/features/identity/api";
import { roleRow, SUPERVISOR, staffMe, staffRow } from "@/test/access-fixtures";
import { renderWithRouter } from "@/test/render";
import { accessApi } from "./api";
import { StaffDialog } from "./StaffDialog";

vi.mock("./api", async (orig) => {
	const actual = await orig<typeof import("./api")>();
	return {
		...actual,
		accessApi: {
			...actual.accessApi,
			roles: vi.fn(),
			createStaff: vi.fn(),
			updateStaff: vi.fn(),
		},
	};
});
vi.mock("@/features/identity/api", async (orig) => {
	const actual = await orig<typeof import("@/features/identity/api")>();
	return { ...actual, identityApi: { ...actual.identityApi, me: vi.fn() } };
});

const OLD = roleRow({ id: 3, name_en: "Old desk", is_active: false });
const CLERK = roleRow({ id: 2, name_en: "Accountant" });
const SARA = staffRow({
	id: 7,
	full_name: "Sara",
	email: "sara@demo.test",
	roles: [{ id: 3, name_ar: "قديم", name_en: "Old desk" }],
});

function refused(status: number, field: string, message: string) {
	const response = {
		status,
		data: { [field]: [message] },
		statusText: "",
		headers: {},
		config: { headers: new AxiosHeaders() },
	};
	return new AxiosError(String(status), "ERR", undefined, undefined, response);
}

describe("StaffDialog", () => {
	beforeEach(() => {
		vi.clearAllMocks();
		vi.mocked(identityApi.me).mockResolvedValue({
			id: 1,
			email: "admin@demo.test",
			full_name: "Admin",
			role: "admin",
			profiles: [],
		});
		vi.mocked(accessApi.roles).mockResolvedValue({
			count: 3,
			next: null,
			previous: null,
			results: [CLERK, OLD, SUPERVISOR],
		});
		vi.mocked(accessApi.createStaff).mockResolvedValue(SARA);
		vi.mocked(accessApi.updateStaff).mockResolvedValue(SARA);
	});

	it("adds a staff member with the active roles chosen, and says the invite went", async () => {
		const user = userEvent.setup();
		renderWithRouter(<StaffDialog />);
		await user.click(
			await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Add staff member" }),
		);
		const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog");
		await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText(/^Full name/), " Nour ");
		await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText(/^Email/), "nour@demo.test");
		await user.click(
			await within(dialog).findByRole("checkbox", { name: "Supervisor" }),
		);
		expect(
			within(dialog).queryByRole("checkbox", { name: /Old desk/ }),
		).toBeNull();
		expect(
			within(dialog).queryByRole("checkbox", { name: "Active" }),
		).toBeNull();
		await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole("button", { name: "Save" }));
		await waitFor(() =>
			expect(accessApi.createStaff).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
				full_name: "Nour",
				email: "nour@demo.test",
				role_ids: [1],
			}),
		);
		expect(
			await screen.findByText("Invite sent to nour@demo.test."),
		).toBeInTheDocument();
		await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByRole("dialog")).toBeNull());
	});

	it("keeps a retired role already held, and deactivates", async () => {
		const user = userEvent.setup();
		renderWithRouter(<StaffDialog member={SARA} />);
		await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Edit Sara" }));
		const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog");
		const old = await within(dialog).findByRole("checkbox", {
			name: "Old desk (retired)",
		});
		expect(old).toBeChecked();
		await user.click(
			within(dialog).getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Accountant" }),
		);
		await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Active" }));
		await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole("button", { name: "Save" }));
		await waitFor(() =>
			expect(accessApi.updateStaff).toHaveBeenCalledWith(7, {
				full_name: "Sara",
				email: "sara@demo.test",
				role_ids: [3, 2],
				is_active: false,
			}),
		);
		expect(await screen.findByText("Staff member saved.")).toBeInTheDocument();
	});

	it("checks the name and email before asking the server", async () => {
		const user = userEvent.setup();
		renderWithRouter(<StaffDialog />);
		await user.click(
			await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Add staff member" }),
		);
		const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog");
		await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText(/^Email/), "not-an-email");
		await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole("button", { name: "Save" }));
		expect(
			await within(dialog).findByText("Enter a name."),
		).toBeInTheDocument();
		expect(
			within(dialog).getByText("Enter a valid email address."),
		).toBeInTheDocument();
		expect(accessApi.createStaff).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
	});

	it("puts the server's refusals on their fields", async () => {
		vi.mocked(accessApi.createStaff).mockRejectedValue(
			refused(
				403,
				"role_ids",
				"You can only grant roles whose permissions you hold yourself.",
			),
		);
		const user = userEvent.setup();
		renderWithRouter(<StaffDialog />);
		await user.click(
			await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Add staff member" }),
		);
		const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog");
		await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText(/^Full name/), "Nour");
		await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText(/^Email/), "nour@demo.test");
		await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole("button", { name: "Save" }));
		expect(
			await within(dialog).findByText(
				"You can only grant roles whose permissions you hold yourself.",
			),
		).toBeInTheDocument();
		expect(screen.getByRole("dialog")).toBeInTheDocument();
	});

	it("never lets anyone change their own roles or deactivate themselves", async () => {
		vi.mocked(identityApi.me).mockResolvedValue({ ...staffMe(), id: 7 });
		const user = userEvent.setup();
		renderWithRouter(<StaffDialog member={SARA} />);
		await waitFor(() => expect(identityApi.me).toHaveBeenCalled());
		await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Edit Sara" }));
		const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog");
		expect(
			await within(dialog).findByText(
				"You can't change your own roles or deactivate yourself.",
			),
		).toBeInTheDocument();
		expect(
			await within(dialog).findByRole("checkbox", { name: "Accountant" }),
		).toBeDisabled();
		expect(
			within(dialog).getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Active" }),
		).toBeDisabled();
	});
});
```

Create `dashboard/src/features/access/StaffList.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import "@/lib/i18n";
import { identityApi } from "@/features/identity/api";
import { CanProvider } from "@/features/identity/permissions";
import { SUPERVISOR, staffMe, staffRow } from "@/test/access-fixtures";
import { renderWithRouter } from "@/test/render";
import { accessApi } from "./api";
import { StaffList } from "./StaffList";
import type { StaffMember } from "./schemas";

vi.mock("./api", async (orig) => {
	const actual = await orig<typeof import("./api")>();
	return {
		...actual,
		accessApi: {
			...actual.accessApi,
			staff: vi.fn(),
			roles: vi.fn(),
			updateStaff: vi.fn(),
		},
	};
});
vi.mock("@/features/identity/api", async (orig) => {
	const actual = await orig<typeof import("@/features/identity/api")>();
	return { ...actual, identityApi: { ...actual.identityApi, me: vi.fn() } };
});

const page = (results: StaffMember[]) => ({
	count: results.length,
	next: null,
	previous: null,
	results,
});
const SARA = staffRow({ id: 7, full_name: "Sara", email: "sara@demo.test" });
const NOUR = staffRow({
	id: 8,
	full_name: "Nour",
	email: "nour@demo.test",
	roles: [],
	is_active: false,
	invited: true,
});

function lastParams() {
	return vi.mocked(accessApi.staff).mock.calls.at(-1)?.[0];
}

describe("StaffList", () => {
	beforeEach(() => {
		vi.clearAllMocks();
		vi.mocked(identityApi.me).mockResolvedValue({
			id: 1,
			email: "admin@demo.test",
			full_name: "Admin",
			role: "admin",
			profiles: [],
		});
		vi.mocked(accessApi.staff).mockResolvedValue(page([NOUR, SARA]));
		vi.mocked(accessApi.roles).mockResolvedValue({
			count: 1,
			next: null,
			previous: null,
			results: [SUPERVISOR],
		});
		vi.mocked(accessApi.updateStaff).mockResolvedValue({
			...SARA,
			is_active: false,
		});
	});

	it("lists each member's email, roles and status", async () => {
		renderWithRouter(<StaffList />);
		const sara = await screen.findByRole("row", { name: /Sara/ });
		expect(within(sara).getByText("sara@demo.test")).toBeInTheDocument();
		expect(within(sara).getByRole("listitem")).toHaveTextContent("Supervisor");
		expect(within(sara).getByText("Active")).toBeInTheDocument();
		const nour = screen.getByRole("row", { name: /Nour/ });
		expect(within(nour).getByText("No roles")).toBeInTheDocument();
		expect(within(nour).getByText("Inactive")).toBeInTheDocument();
		expect(within(nour).getByText("Invited")).toBeInTheDocument();
	});

	it("searches and filters by role and status, from the first page", async () => {
		const user = userEvent.setup();
		renderWithRouter(<StaffList />);
		await screen.findByRole("row", { name: /Sara/ });
		await user.type(screen.getByRole("searchbox"), "sa");
		await waitFor(() => expect(lastParams()).toMatchObject({ search: "sa" }));
		await user.selectOptions(
			screen.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Roles" }),
			await screen.findByRole("option", { name: "Supervisor" }),
		);
		await user.selectOptions(
			screen.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Status" }),
			"false",
		);
		await waitFor(() =>
			expect(lastParams()).toEqual({
				page: 1,
				search: "sa",
				role: "1",
				active: "false",
			}),
		);
	});

	it("closes the dialog before a deactivated member leaves the Active list", async () => {
		vi.mocked(accessApi.staff).mockImplementation(async (params) =>
			page(
				params.active === "true" &&
					vi.mocked(accessApi.updateStaff).mock.calls.length > 0
					? []
					: [SARA],
			),
		);
		const user = userEvent.setup();
		renderWithRouter(<StaffList />);
		await user.selectOptions(
			await screen.findByRole("combobox", { name: "Status" }),
			"true",
		);
		await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Edit Sara" }));
		const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog");
		await user.click(
			await within(dialog).findByRole("checkbox", { name: "Active" }),
		);
		// Enter from the name field: no click to hold the dialog up.
		await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText(/^Full name/), "{Enter}");
		expect(await screen.findByText("Staff member saved.")).toBeInTheDocument();
		await waitFor(() =>
			expect(screen.queryByRole("row", { name: /Sara/ })).toBeNull(),
		);
		expect(screen.queryByRole("dialog")).toBeNull();
		expect(document.body.style.pointerEvents).not.toBe("none");
	});

	it("hides Add and Edit from staff without the codes", async () => {
		renderWithRouter(
			<CanProvider me={staffMe("staff.view_any")}>
				<StaffList />
			</CanProvider>,
		);
		await screen.findByRole("row", { name: /Sara/ });
		expect(
			screen.queryByRole("button", { name: "Add staff member" }),
		).toBeNull();
		expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: "Edit Sara" })).toBeNull();
	});
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run them to verify they fail**

Run (from `dashboard/`): `npx pnpm@10 exec vitest run src/features/access/StaffList.test.tsx src/features/access/StaffDialog.test.tsx src/features/shell/nav.test.ts`

Expected: FAIL — `Failed to resolve import "./StaffList"` and `Failed to resolve import "./StaffDialog"`, and `nav.test.ts`'s order and grouping (there is no Staff item yet).

One test is a guard once the list exists: "closes the dialog before a deactivated member leaves the Active list" also passes in jsdom with a plain `setOpen(false)`. It holds the behaviour; `flushSync` is there for the browser (Plan 11's finding).

Create `dashboard/src/features/access/StaffDialog.tsx`:

```tsx
import { zodResolver } from "@hookform/resolvers/zod";
import { useState } from "react";
import { flushSync } from "react-dom";
import { useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import { useMe } from "@/features/identity/queries";
import { useFieldError } from "@/lib/field-error";
import { applyServerErrors } from "@/lib/form-errors";
import {
	Alert,
	AlertDescription,
	Button,
	Checkbox,
	Dialog,
	DialogClose,
	DialogContent,
	DialogDescription,
	DialogFooter,
	DialogTitle,
	DialogTrigger,
	Field,
	FormError,
	Input,
	Label,
	toast,
} from "@/ui";
import { useCreateStaff, useRoles, useUpdateStaff } from "./queries";
import {
	type AccessRole,
	localName,
	type StaffFormValues,
	type StaffMember,
	staffFormSchema,
} from "./schemas";

function blank(member?: StaffMember): StaffFormValues {
	return {
		full_name: member?.full_name ?? "",
		email: member?.email ?? "",
		role_ids: (member?.roles ?? []).map((role) => role.id),
		is_active: member?.is_active ?? true,
	};
}

/** The roles a staff member may be given: the active ones, and any retired
 * one they already hold (kept, never newly given). */
function choices(roles: readonly AccessRole[], held: readonly number[]) {
	return roles.filter((role) => role.is_active || held.includes(role.id));
}

/** Add a staff member (no `member`; they are emailed an invite) or edit one
 * (Plan 12a §6.1): name, email, roles and, when editing, active. Nobody
 * changes their own roles or deactivates themselves (§4.2). */
export function StaffDialog({ member }: { member?: StaffMember }) {
	const { t, i18n } = useTranslation();
	const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
	const { data: me } = useMe();
	const { data: roles } = useRoles({ page_size: 100 }, { enabled: open });
	const create = useCreateStaff();
	const update = useUpdateStaff();
	const fieldError = useFieldError();
	const {
		register,
		handleSubmit,
		reset,
		setError,
		setValue,
		watch,
		formState: { errors, isSubmitting },
	} = useForm<StaffFormValues>({
		resolver: zodResolver(staffFormSchema),
		defaultValues: blank(member),
	});
	const roleIds = watch("role_ids");
	const active = watch("is_active");
	const oneself = member !== undefined && member.id === me?.id;
	const prefix = member ? `staff-${member.id}` : "new-staff";

	function openChange(next: boolean) {
		if (next) reset(blank(member));
		setOpen(next);
	}

	function toggleRole(id: number, on: boolean) {
		setValue(
			"role_ids",
			on ? [...roleIds, id] : roleIds.filter((one) => one !== id),
			{ shouldDirty: true },
		);
	}

	async function onSubmit(values: StaffFormValues) {
		const base = {
			full_name: values.full_name.trim(),
			email: values.email.trim(),
			role_ids: values.role_ids,
		};
		try {
			if (member) {
				await update.mutateAsync({
					id: member.id,
					body: { ...base, is_active: values.is_active },
				});
				toast({ description: t("access.staff.saved"), variant: "success" });
			} else {
				await create.mutateAsync(base);
				toast({
					description: t("access.staff.invitedDone", { email: base.email }),
					variant: "success",
				});
			}
			// Closed now, before the refreshed list can drop this row (a
			// filter on the status or role): React would otherwise batch the
			// close with the unmount and lose focus (Plan 11's finding).
			flushSync(() => setOpen(false));
		} catch (error) {
			applyServerErrors(error, setError);
		}
	}

	return (
		<Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={openChange}>
			<DialogTrigger asChild>
				{member ? (
					<Button
						size="sm"
						variant="outline"
						aria-label={t("access.staff.editName", { name: member.full_name })}
					>
						{t("access.staff.edit")}
					</Button>
				) : (
					<Button size="sm">{t("access.staff.add")}</Button>
				)}
			</DialogTrigger>
			<DialogContent>
				<DialogTitle>
					{t(member ? "access.staff.editTitle" : "access.staff.add")}
				</DialogTitle>
				<DialogDescription>
					{t(member ? "access.staff.editBody" : "access.staff.addBody")}
				</DialogDescription>
				<form
					onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)}
					className="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-4"
					noValidate
				>
					<Field
						id={`${prefix}-name`}
						label={t("access.staff.fullName")}
						error={fieldError(errors.full_name?.message)}
						required
					>
						<Input {...register("full_name")} />
					</Field>
					<Field
						id={`${prefix}-email`}
						label={t("access.staff.email")}
						error={fieldError(errors.email?.message)}
						required
					>
						<Input type="email" dir="ltr" {...register("email")} />
					</Field>
					{oneself ? (
						<Alert>
							<AlertDescription>
								{t("access.staff.ownAccount")}
							</AlertDescription>
						</Alert>
					) : null}
					<fieldset className="flex flex-col gap-2">
						<legend className="text-sm font-medium">
							{t("access.staff.roles")}
						</legend>
						{choices(roles?.results ?? [], blank(member).role_ids).map(
							(role) => {
								const id = `${prefix}-role-${role.id}`;
								const name = localName(role, i18n.language);
								return (
									<div key={role.id} className="flex items-center gap-2">
										<Checkbox
											id={id}
											checked={roleIds.includes(role.id)}
											disabled={oneself}
											onCheckedChange={(on) => toggleRole(role.id, on)}
										/>
										<Label htmlFor={id} className="font-normal">
											{role.is_active
												? name
												: `${name} ${t("access.staff.retiredRole")}`}
										</Label>
									</div>
								);
							},
						)}
						{errors.role_ids ? (
							<FormError>{fieldError(errors.role_ids.message)}</FormError>
						) : null}
					</fieldset>
					{member ? (
						<div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
							<div className="flex items-center gap-2">
								<Checkbox
									id={`${prefix}-active`}
									checked={active}
									disabled={oneself}
									onCheckedChange={(on) =>
										setValue("is_active", on, { shouldDirty: true })
									}
								/>
								<Label htmlFor={`${prefix}-active`} className="font-normal">
									{t("access.staff.active")}
								</Label>
							</div>
							{errors.is_active ? (
								<FormError>{fieldError(errors.is_active.message)}</FormError>
							) : null}
						</div>
					) : null}
					{errors.root?.server ? (
						<Alert variant="destructive">
							<AlertDescription>
								{fieldError(errors.root.server.message)}
							</AlertDescription>
						</Alert>
					) : null}
					<DialogFooter className="mt-0">
						<DialogClose asChild>
							<Button type="button" variant="outline">
								{t("access.staff.cancel")}
							</Button>
						</DialogClose>
						<Button type="submit" disabled={isSubmitting}>
							{t("access.staff.save")}
						</Button>
					</DialogFooter>
				</form>
			</DialogContent>
		</Dialog>
	);
}
```

Create `dashboard/src/features/access/StaffList.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Search, UserCog } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import { Pager } from "@/components/Pager";
import { useCan } from "@/features/identity/permissions";
import type { QueryParams } from "@/lib/api";
import {
	Alert,
	AlertDescription,
	Card,
	CardContent,
	EmptyState,
	Input,
	Select,
	Spinner,
	StatusChip,
} from "@/ui";
import { useRoles, useStaff } from "./queries";
import { StaffDialog } from "./StaffDialog";
import { localName } from "./schemas";

const PAGE_SIZE = 25;

/** People → Staff (Plan 12a §6.1): search, Role and Status filters, Add
 * (which sends the invite), and per member their roles, status and Edit. */
export function StaffList() {
	const { t, i18n } = useTranslation();
	const can = useCan();
	const [params, setParams] = useState<QueryParams>({ page: 1 });
	const { data, isPending, isError } = useStaff(params);
	const { data: roles } = useRoles({ page_size: 100 });
	const rows = data?.results ?? [];
	const page = Number(params.page ?? 1);
	const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((data?.count ?? 0) / PAGE_SIZE));
	const set = (patch: QueryParams) =>
		setParams({ ...params, page: 1, ...patch });

	return (
		<div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
			<div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
				<div className="relative min-w-48 flex-1">
					<label htmlFor="staff-search" className="sr-only">
						{t("access.staff.search")}
					</label>
					<Search className="pointer-events-none absolute start-3 top-3.5 size-4 text-muted-foreground" />
					<Input
						id="staff-search"
						type="search"
						className="ps-9"
						placeholder={t("access.staff.search")}
						value={String(params.search ?? "")}
						onChange={(e) => set({ search: e.target.value })}
					/>
				</div>
				<Select
					aria-label={t("access.staff.columns.roles")}
					className="w-auto"
					value={String(params.role ?? "")}
					onChange={(e) => set({ role: e.target.value })}
				>
					<option value="">{t("access.staff.anyRole")}</option>
					{(roles?.results ?? []).map((role) => (
						<option key={role.id} value={String(role.id)}>
							{localName(role, i18n.language)}
						</option>
					))}
				</Select>
				<Select
					aria-label={t("access.staff.columns.status")}
					className="w-auto"
					value={String(params.active ?? "")}
					onChange={(e) => set({ active: e.target.value })}
				>
					<option value="">{t("access.staff.anyStatus")}</option>
					<option value="true">{t("access.staff.status.active")}</option>
					<option value="false">{t("access.staff.status.inactive")}</option>
				</Select>
				{can("staff.create") ? <StaffDialog /> : null}
			</div>
			{isError ? (
				<Alert variant="destructive">
					<AlertDescription>{t("access.staff.loadError")}</AlertDescription>
				</Alert>
			) : isPending ? (
				<div className="flex justify-center py-6">
					<Spinner />
				</div>
			) : rows.length === 0 ? (
				<Card>
					<CardContent>
						<EmptyState icon={UserCog} title={t("access.staff.empty")} />
					</CardContent>
				</Card>
			) : (
				<div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-lg border border-border">
					<table className="w-full text-sm">
						<thead className="bg-secondary text-start text-muted-foreground">
							<tr>
								{(["name", "email", "roles", "status"] as const).map((key) => (
									<th
										key={key}
										scope="col"
										className="p-3 text-start font-medium"
									>
										{t(`access.staff.columns.${key}`)}
									</th>
								))}
								<th scope="col" className="p-3">
									<span className="sr-only">
										{t("access.staff.columns.actions")}
									</span>
								</th>
							</tr>
						</thead>
						<tbody>
							{rows.map((member) => (
								<tr key={member.id} className="border-t border-border">
									<td className="p-3 font-medium">{member.full_name}</td>
									<td className="p-3" dir="ltr">
										{member.email}
									</td>
									<td className="p-3">
										{member.roles.length === 0 ? (
											<span className="text-muted-foreground">
												{t("access.staff.noRoles")}
											</span>
										) : (
											<ul className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
												{member.roles.map((role) => (
													<li
														key={role.id}
														className="rounded-full bg-secondary px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-secondary-foreground"
													>
														{localName(role, i18n.language)}
													</li>
												))}
											</ul>
										)}
									</td>
									<td className="p-3">
										<span className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
											{t(
												member.is_active
													? "access.staff.status.active"
													: "access.staff.status.inactive",
											)}
											{member.invited ? (
												<StatusChip>{t("access.staff.invited")}</StatusChip>
											) : null}
										</span>
									</td>
									<td className="p-3">
										<div className="flex justify-end">
											{can("staff.update") ? (
												<StaffDialog member={member} />
											) : null}
										</div>
									</td>
								</tr>
							))}
						</tbody>
					</table>
				</div>
			)}
			<Pager
				page={page}
				pages={pages}
				onChange={(next) => setParams({ ...params, page: next })}
			/>
		</div>
	);
}
```

```tsx
import { createFileRoute } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import { StaffList } from "@/features/access";
import { usePageTitle } from "@/features/branding";
import { PageHeader } from "@/ui";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/_authed/people/staff")({
	staticData: { permission: "staff.view_any" },
	component: function StaffRoute() {
		const { t } = useTranslation();
		usePageTitle(t("nav.staff"));
		return (
			<>
				<PageHeader
					title={t("nav.staff")}
					description={t("access.staff.subtitle")}
				/>
				<StaffList />
			</>
		);
	},
});
```

### Task 11: Dashboard: the people, catalogue, settings and website actions follow the codes

**Files:**
- Modify:
  - `dashboard/src/features/people/{schemas.ts,StudentsList,ParentsList,TeachersList,AccountActions,GuardiansPanel,StudentForm,ParentForm,TeacherForm,FamiliesList,FamilyPage,TagsSettings}.tsx`;
  - `dashboard/src/features/catalogue/{CatalogueList,CourseForm,PackageForm}.tsx`;
  - `dashboard/src/features/academy/AcademySettingsForm.tsx`, `dashboard/src/features/notifications/NotificationSettingsForm.tsx`;
  - `dashboard/src/features/website/{BrandingForm,HomePageForm,PageEditor,PagesList,TestimonialsManager,InquiriesList}.tsx`.
- Test: each of those components' existing test file gains one test (listed in Step 2).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 7's `useCan`, `CanProvider` and `staffMe`; Task 8's `ADMINS_ONLY`.
- Produces: `people/schemas.personCode(kind, verb) -> string` (`ADMINS_ONLY` for admins). Every change below follows D8:
  - entry points need `create`;
  - row actions and bulk selections need `update`;
  - Delete needs `delete`, and Duplicate needs `replicate`;
  - a detail form a staff account may only see is read-only (`useForm({ disabled: !editable })`, controlled fields read `field.disabled`) with no Save.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Each renders the component inside `CanProvider` with `staffMe(<the view code only>)` and asserts:
- that the write controls are gone;
- or, for forms, that the fields are disabled and Save is absent.

`PackageForm` checks the opposite: a staff account holding `package.create` fills in a new package.

```ts
export type Kind = "students" | "parents" | "teachers" | "admins";
export type AccountState = "no_login" | "invited" | "active" | "inactive";
```

with:

```ts
export type Kind = "students" | "parents" | "teachers" | "admins";
/** The code an action on a person of `kind` needs (Plan 12a): only admins
 * act on admins. */
export function personCode(kind: Kind, verb: string): string {
	return kind === "admins" ? ADMINS_ONLY : `${kind.slice(0, -1)}.${verb}`;
}
export type AccountState = "no_login" | "invited" | "active" | "inactive";
```

### Task 12: Dashboard: the scheduling, billing and payroll actions follow the codes

**Files:**
- Modify:
  - `dashboard/src/features/scheduling/{SubscriptionsList,SubscriptionActions,PausesPanel,SlotsPanel,SessionsList,TodayBoard,SessionPage}.tsx`;
  - `dashboard/src/features/billing/{InvoicesList,InvoicePage}.tsx`;
  - `dashboard/src/features/payroll/{PayslipsList,PayslipPage,RatesPage,AdjustmentsList}.tsx`;
  - `dashboard/src/locales/{en,ar}/common.json` (`scheduling.report.none`).
- Test: each component's existing test file gains one test (two for `SessionPage`).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 7's `useCan`, `CanProvider` and `staffMe`.
- Produces (D2's codes on the screens):
  - **Subscriptions.** New needs `subscription.create`. Renew, Edit, Cancel and every pause and slot action need `subscription.update`. Delete needs `subscription.delete`.
  - **Sessions.** Selection and the bulk bar need `session.update`, as do Cancel and Restore. The Today board's Generate buttons need `session.create`. Attendance needs `attendance.update`.
  - **The session page's report.** The card shows only with `session_report.view`. The form needs `session_report.update`; without it the report reads as facts, or "No report yet.".
  - **Invoices.** New needs `invoice.create`, and Edit and Void need `invoice.update`. Record payment needs `payment.create`, and deleting a payment needs `payment.delete`.
  - **Payroll.** Generate needs `payslip.create`, and Issue and Mark paid need `payslip.update`. The rates and adjustments need their own `create`, `update` and `delete`.

### Task 13: End-to-end through Caddy: a staff member who sees only what their role allows; STATE.md

**Files:**
- Create: `dashboard/e2e/roles.spec.ts`
- Modify: `STATE.md` (meta), submodule pointers (meta, after merge)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 1 to 12; `e2e/fixtures.ts`'s `login`, `expectLoggedIn`, `acceptInvite`, `DEMO_URL` and `DEMO_ADMIN`.
- Produces: `e2e/roles.spec.ts` (spec §8's 12a journey).
- CI: no change. The `e2e` job already migrates, runs `seed_dev` (which now seeds the preset and Sara) and serves Django, the Vite preview and the marketing site behind `caddy/Caddyfile.e2e`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the journey**

1. The admin adds a role that can only list and open students (Settings → Roles, the Students row's "view all" and "view").
2. They add a staff member holding it (People → Staff, which says the invite went).
3. The staff member accepts the invite and signs in. The nav shows Students but not Invoices or Roles, and the students list offers no New student.
4. `/app/billing/invoices`, opened directly, says "You don't have access to this page."
5. The API answers 403 there and 200 on the students list, asked from the page with its session.

Create `dashboard/e2e/roles.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";
import {
	acceptInvite,
	DEMO_ADMIN,
	DEMO_URL,
	expectLoggedIn,
	login,
} from "./fixtures";

// Plan 12a spec §8: an admin composes a role and adds a staff member holding
// it; the staff member accepts the invite, sees only the nav the role
// allows, is told "You don't have access" on a screen it doesn't, and the
// API refuses them there too. Names are stamped, so a second run on the same
// database finds only its own.
test("an admin gives a staff member a role, and the staff member sees only what it allows", async ({
	page,
	browser,
}) => {
	const stamp = Date.now();
	const role = `E2E Registrar ${stamp}`;
	const name = `E2E Staff ${stamp}`;
	const email = `e2e-staff-${stamp}@e2e.test`;

	await login(page, DEMO_URL, DEMO_ADMIN);
	await expectLoggedIn(page, /demo academy admin/i);
	await page.evaluate(() => localStorage.setItem("etqan-locale", "en"));

	// Settings → Roles: a role that lists and opens students, nothing else
	await page.goto(`${DEMO_URL}/app/settings/roles`);
	await expect(page.getByRole("row", { name: /Supervisor/ })).toBeVisible();
	await page.getByRole("link", { name: "Add role" }).click();
	await page.getByLabel(/^Name \(Arabic\)/).fill(`مسجل ${stamp}`);
	await page.getByLabel(/^Name \(English\)/).fill(role);
	const students = page.getByRole("group", { name: "Students" });
	await students.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "view all", exact: true }).click();
	await students.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "view", exact: true }).click();
	await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Save role" }).click();
	await expect(page.getByText("Role saved.", { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
	await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/app\/settings\/roles$/);
	await expect(page.getByRole("row", { name: new RegExp(role) })).toBeVisible();

	// People → Staff: the staff member, holding it, invited on save
	await page.goto(`${DEMO_URL}/app/people/staff`);
	await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Add staff member" }).click();
	const dialog = page.getByRole("dialog");
	await dialog.getByLabel(/^Full name/).fill(name);
	await dialog.getByLabel(/^Email/).fill(email);
	await dialog.getByRole("checkbox", { name: role, exact: true }).click();
	await dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "Save", exact: true }).click();
	await expect(
		page.getByText(`Invite sent to ${email}.`, { exact: true }),
	).toBeVisible();
	await page.getByRole("searchbox").fill(name);
	await expect(page.getByRole("row", { name: new RegExp(name) })).toContainText(
		role,
	);

	// The staff member signs in through the invite
	const staff = await acceptInvite(browser, email, "e2e-Staff-2026", name);
	const nav = staff.getByRole("navigation", { name: "Main navigation" });
	await expect(nav.getByRole("link", { name: "Students" })).toBeVisible();
	await expect(nav.getByRole("link", { name: "Invoices" })).toHaveCount(0);
	await expect(nav.getByRole("link", { name: "Roles" })).toHaveCount(0);
	await nav.getByRole("link", { name: "Students" }).click();
	await expect(staff).toHaveURL(/\/app\/people\/students$/);
	await expect(staff.getByRole("link", { name: "New student" })).toHaveCount(0);

	// A screen the role doesn't allow, opened directly, and its API
	await staff.goto(`${new URL(staff.url()).origin}/app/billing/invoices`);
	await expect(
		staff.getByRole("heading", {
			level: 1,
			name: "You don't have access to this page.",
		}),
	).toBeVisible();
	// Asked from the page, with its session, as the dashboard asks.
	const status = (path: string) =>
		staff.evaluate(async (url) => (await fetch(url)).status, path);
	expect(await status("/api/v1/billing/invoices/")).toBe(403);
	expect(await status("/api/v1/people/students/")).toBe(200);
	await staff.context().close();
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the whole e2e suite through the edge, as the CI job does**

With Postgres and Redis up, from the meta root. These are the CI `e2e` job's steps; on a laptop, use `backend/.venv/bin/python` and export `E2E_PYTHON` to it.

```bash
export DATABASE_URL=postgres://etqan:etqan@localhost:5432/etqan CELERY_BROKER_URL=redis://localhost:6379/0 \
  DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE=config.settings.local DJANGO_SECRET_KEY=ci-only \
  DJANGO_EMAIL_BACKEND=django.core.mail.backends.filebased.EmailBackend DJANGO_EMAIL_FILE_PATH=/tmp/etqan-mail \
  CELERY_TASK_ALWAYS_EAGER=true DJANGO_TENANT_URL_TEMPLATE='http://{domain}'
(cd backend && python manage.py migrate_schemas --shared && mkdir -p /tmp/etqan-mail && python manage.py seed_dev \
  && nohup python manage.py runserver 127.0.0.1:8000 > /tmp/django.log 2>&1 &)
(cd dashboard && npx pnpm@10 build && nohup npx pnpm@10 preview --port 4173 --host 127.0.0.1 > /tmp/preview.log 2>&1 &)
(cd marketing && npx pnpm@10 build && SITE_API_ORIGIN=http://127.0.0.1:8000 SITE_SCHEME=http HOST=127.0.0.1 PORT=4321 \
  nohup node dist/server/entry.mjs > /tmp/marketing.log 2>&1 &)
docker run -d --name edge --network host -v "$PWD/caddy/Caddyfile.e2e:/etc/caddy/Caddyfile:ro" caddy:2.11.4-alpine
cd dashboard && E2E_DEMO_URL=http://demo.etqan.localhost E2E_OTHER_URL=http://other.etqan.localhost \
  E2E_MAIL_DIR=/tmp/etqan-mail CI=1 npx pnpm@10 exec playwright test --retries=0
```

If port 80 or 8000 is taken on the machine:
- run Django on another port (e.g. `18000`), and a scratch copy of `caddy/Caddyfile.e2e` on e.g. `:8080` pointing at it;
- set `DJANGO_TENANT_URL_TEMPLATE='http://{domain}:8080'`, `E2E_BASE_URL=http://etqan.localhost:8080` and `E2E_APP_URL=http://demo.etqan.localhost:8080`;
- leave `E2E_DEMO_URL` and `E2E_OTHER_URL` unset, so they follow `E2E_BASE_URL`.

Never commit the port changes.

Expected: 21 passed (the 20 existing tests and `roles.spec.ts`), with no retry. Run it a second time on the same database: the same result. Then stop the edge (`docker rm -f edge`) and the three servers, and drop the database if it was a scratch one.

```markdown
restore, a family page), the account type and family chip, filters and bulk actions on students,
```

## Self-review

Checked against the spec after writing; fixes were made inline.

**Spec coverage**

| Spec (12a) | Task |
|---|---|
| R-1 the full matrix, greyed "not used yet" verbs | 2 (D1), 9 (D9) |
| R-2 codes in code, roles as data; no Django permissions or packages | 2 |
| R-3 the `staff` account type holding many roles | 2 (D6), 3 |
| R-4 admin is super admin, can't be restricted; only admins create or edit admins | 1, 4 (`ADMIN_ONLY`), 8 (`ADMINS_ONLY`) |
| R-5 staff see all the academy's data for what they may view | 5 (D7) |
| §3.1 staff sign in through the invite and password flows; no role → own profile only | 2, 3, 8 (the nav keeps Home and Account) |
| §3.2 `StaffRole` fields, case-insensitive unique names, unknown codes refused, retire and restore, never deleted, many-to-many | 2, 3 (D10) |
| §3.3 the registry, with pages, widgets and `session.supervise` defined, not in use | 2 (D1), 4 (in-use equals the routes) |
| §3.4 the Supervisor preset by migration and `create_academy`, idempotent by name, editable | 2 (D11) |
| §4.1 one class, the verb rule, every route declares its code, a coverage test, one load per request | 1, 4 (D2–D4) |
| §4.2 staff and roles admin, the five guards with their fields, invites, deactivation, retire and restore | 3 (D5) |
| §4.3 `me/` `permissions` and `is_super_admin` | 4 |
| §5.1 the `access/` routes, their rows and filters, no PUT or DELETE | 3 |
| §6.1 Settings → Roles (list; tabs; select all; greyed verbs), People → Staff (list, filters, dialogs, invite), `can()` on nav, routes and actions, "You don't have access", shared helpers, en and ar | 7–12 (D8, D9) |
| §7 the preset everywhere, Sara Supervisor in `demo`, idempotent | 6 (D12) |
| §8 the registry, route coverage, per-code enforcement, retired roles, admins, other roles unaffected, escalation, roles rules, isolation, query counts, dashboard units, one e2e journey | 1–13 |
| §9 risks: one declared code per route and a coverage test; the guards with tests; one load per request with query-count tests; `me/` feeds both sides and the server stays the authority | 3, 4, 8 |

**Spec gaps settled here:**
- the resource codes and their in-use verbs, and which widget gates what (D1);
- the verb for `DELETE`, `duplicate`, `PUT` and the pause and slot writes, a form lookup's code, and the admins-only routes (D2);
- how a view declares its code, and what each old composition becomes (D3);
- where the codes are loaded, without the platform importing a business app (D4);
- the guards' status and fields, and which changes a staff user may still make (D5);
- how a staff account reuses identity's invite and deactivation (D6);
- every place admins alone saw everything, and notifications left admin-only (D7);
- how the dashboard hides actions without hiding a teacher's own (D8);
- the editor's form, its place, and the Arabic label clash (D9);
- what `members` counts and how retired roles behave on assignment (D10);
- holding `session.supervise` now (D11);
- Sara's login (D12);
- the dashboard's `Me` type (D13).

**Placeholder scan:** no "TBD", "TODO", "similar to Task N" or step without its code. Every name a later task uses is produced by an earlier task's Interfaces block.

**Type and name consistency:**
- `permission_codes` (views), `required_code` and `HasCode` (Task 1) are what Task 3's and Task 4's views use and what `test_routes.py` reads.
- `codes_for` (Task 2) is the loader `AccessConfig.ready()` registers for `codes_of` (Task 1), which `me/` reads (Task 4).
- The roles row's `members` is `role.member_count` (Task 2's annotation) through `role_row` (Task 3), and `AccessRole.members` (Task 7).
- The staff row's `roles`, `invited` and `created_at` match `StaffMember`; `role_ids` and `is_active` are the fields the guards name and the dialog's form fields, so `applyServerErrors` lands them.
- The route table's codes, the registry's `IN_USE`, the screens' `staticData.permission` and the actions' `can(...)` use the same strings (D1, D2, D8).

**Review Focus:** each of the five lines names its tests, which are in the owning task's test files.
